lean/LeanUrat/OM/UniformCapstone.lean — theorem

2 Digit moves and the carry algebra

At a wild prime the classifier's higher-order steps mix coefficient digits through carries. These stages compute that mixing once, inside a fixed quotient ring, and prove transport theorems moving weights and residues level to level. The abstract engine is proved; identifying its stages with the classifier's actual reads at every degree is part of the open bridge work recorded in the amber panel, and the two hard-core corpora carry the still-open construction duties (two and nine named unproved statements respectively).

$$D \;=\; \mathbb{F}[u]/\left(u^{e}-\bar{z}\right)$$
The residual and landing engine (Moves) 3 definition · 1 open · 15 proved · 1 conditional
Residual and landing engine: stratum membership becomes exact digit counts

This corpus is the ground-floor engine of the wild (MacLane/Okutsu–Montes) side of the proof. It fixes an abstract "stage" — one level of the valuation tower at a prime, carrying a weight function, a residual (initial-form) map into Laurent polynomials over the current residue field, and realizer axioms — and develops the whole calculus of key-adic developments over it: existence and uniqueness of developments, the attained-minimum weight law, slot decomposition, digit multiplicativity, graded-piece structure, and the transition-step realizer supply. Its capstone converts membership in a higher-Newton-polygon stratum into an explicit "landing cylinder" — finitely many triangular digit equations on development coefficients — and — given coordinate presentations of the stratum that are hypothesized here rather than constructed — a stratum cut by k digit equations has exact, level-independent density 1/pᵏ: the mechanism that makes wildly ramified densities computable and prime-uniform. The definition layers (the stage, the stage-core invariant, the landing cylinder) are the interface the HC1 tower-induction and HC2 classifier-bridge corpora build on; three statements were machine-refuted during development and restated, with the refutation records kept on disk.

definition A stage of the valuation tower

The single abstract carrier every theorem in this corpus quantifies over. The realizer axiom (S6a) was restated to the coset form after its earlier untwisted transcription (base unit hard-coded to 1) was machine-refuted on the below-threshold strip (finite-field numerics, 2026-07-28).

One level of the valuation tower over $\mathbb{Z}_p[X]$, with its bookkeeping: coprime side data $(e,h)$ with Bézout $es+ht=1$ (and $t=0$ when $e=1$); a monic key polynomial $\Phi$; a stage valuation $w$ obeying the exact product law $w(fg)=w(f)+w(g)$ for nonzero $f,g$ (a genuine valuation — the graded-ring corpus's side valuations, by contrast, postulate only the inequality $w(f)+w(g)\le w(fg)$), the ultrametric law, value group $\mathbb{Z}$, and $w(\Phi)=h$ together with the parent stage's valuation $w_{\mathrm{prev}}$, related by the stretch $w(B)=e\,w_{\mathrm{prev}}(B)$ on the coefficient space $C_\Phi=\{B:\deg B<\deg\Phi\}$; residue fields $F_Q\le K$ (the fixed base residue field $F_Q$ inside the current residue field $K$) inside a fixed ambient finite field $F$; and a residual (weight-normalized initial form) map $R:\mathbb{Z}_p[X]\to K[z^{\pm1}]$ with $R(fg)=R(f)R(g)$, the ultrametric addition/dominance laws, $R(\Phi)=z^s$, monomial coefficient digits $R(B)=c\cdot z^{-t\,w_{\mathrm{prev}}(B)}$ ($c\in K^\times$), the attained-minimum law at the key, and realizers: each occurring coefficient weight is realized with digit scalars sweeping a coset $F_Q^\times\,b$ of the base-field orbit, and above the key threshold with arbitrary scalars in $K^\times$.

The Lean statement 
structure Stage (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (F : Type u) [Field F] [Finite F] where
  e : ℕ
  h : ℕ
  s : ℤ
  t : ℤ
  he : 1 ≤ e
  hh : 1 ≤ h
  hcop : Nat.gcd e h = 1
  hbez : (e : ℤ) * s + (h : ℤ) * t = 1
  he1t : e = 1 → t = 0
  Φ : Polynomial ℤ_[p]
  hmonic : Φ.Monic
  hdeg : 1 ≤ Φ.natDegree
  w : Polynomial ℤ_[p] → ℤ
  wPrev : Polynomial ℤ_[p] → ℤ
  K : Subfield F
  FQ : Subfield F
  hFQ_le : FQ ≤ K
  R : Polynomial ℤ_[p] → LaurentPolynomial ↥K
  hwmul : ∀ f g, f ≠ 0 → g ≠ 0 → w (f * g) = w f + w g
  hwult : ∀ f g, f ≠ 0 → g ≠ 0 → f + g ≠ 0 → min (w f) (w g) ≤ w (f + g)
  hvalgrp : AddSubgroup.closure {n : ℤ | ∃ f g, f ≠ 0 ∧ g ≠ 0 ∧ w f - w g = n} = ⊤
  hwΦ : w Φ = (h : ℤ)
  hStretch : ∀ B, B ≠ 0 → inC Φ B → w B = (e : ℤ) * wPrev B
  hR0 : R 0 = 0
  hRne : ∀ f, f ≠ 0 → R f ≠ 0
  hRmul : ∀ f g, f ≠ 0 → g ≠ 0 → R (f * g) = R f * R g
  hRadd : ∀ f g, f ≠ 0 → g ≠ 0 → f + g ≠ 0 → w f = w g → w (f + g) = w f →
            R (f + g) = R f + R g
  hRlt : ∀ f g, f ≠ 0 → g ≠ 0 → f + g ≠ 0 → w f < w g → R (f + g) = R f
  hRΦ : R Φ = LaurentPolynomial.T s
  hK1 : K1At w Φ (w Φ)
  hS5 : ∀ B, B ≠ 0 → inC Φ B →
          ∃ c : (↥K)ˣ,
            R B = LaurentPolynomial.C (c : ↥K) * LaurentPolynomial.T (- t * wPrev B)
  reps : List (Polynomial ℤ_[p])
  hreps : ∀ ũ ∈ reps, ũ ≠ 0 ∧ inC Φ ũ
  Tvec : List (Polynomial ℤ_[p] × ℤ)
  hTvec : Tvec.map Prod.fst = reps
  weightSet : Set ℤ
  hWS : ∀ B, B ≠ 0 → inC Φ B → wPrev B ∈ weightSet
  hS6a : ∀ ν : ℤ, ν ∈ weightSet → ∃ b : (↥K)ˣ, ∀ c : (↥K)ˣ, ((c : ↥K) : F) ∈ FQ →
           ∃ B, B ≠ 0 ∧ inC Φ B ∧ wPrev B = ν ∧
             R B = LaurentPolynomial.C ((c * b : (↥K)ˣ) : ↥K) * LaurentPolynomial.T (- t * ν)
  hS6b : ∀ (ν : ℤ) (a : (↥K)ˣ), wPrev Φ < ν →
           ∃ B, B ≠ 0 ∧ inC Φ B ∧ wPrev B = ν ∧
             R B = LaurentPolynomial.C (a : ↥K) * LaurentPolynomial.T (- t * ν)

lean/LeanUrat/Moves/Defs.lean — structure

definition The full stage-core invariant, stated at the genuine localization objects

The invariant the base stage constructs and every transition transports (the tower induction itself lives in the HC1 corpus, which consumes this interface); the two localization laws were re-stated at the actual unit/subring objects after semantic audit round 4 found the earlier flattened forms faithless.

The inductive invariant carried up the tower, in three layers. (i) Core laws: the parent valuation is multiplicative and ultrametric, $p$ is among the representatives, strictness ($w(f+g)=w(f)$ when $w(f)<w(g)$) and the jump law (equal weights whose sum jumps force residual cancellation $R(f)+R(g)=0$), the augmentation inequality $e\,w_{\mathrm{prev}}(\Phi)<h$, slot decomposition, and the coefficient-field laws. (ii) The T-vector product law at the actual unit (the T-vector is the stage's recorded list of (representative, integer exponent) pairs): in the two-variable Laurent model $L=K[z^{\pm1}][T^{\pm1}]$ of the graded algebra, the product of the initial-form units of the recorded representatives to their recorded exponents, times the initial form of $\Phi$ to the Bézout power $t$, equals the normalizing unit $T$ — as an identity of units. (iii) The coefficient-localization law (S4): the subring generated by initial forms of coefficients and inverses of representative units has degree-0 part exactly the constants $K$, and $z$ is exactly (scalar 1) the value of an explicit weight-0 monomial vector in the representatives and the key — the pinning $z=V^{-h}Y^{e}$.

The Lean statement 
def TvecUnitLaw (σ : Stage p F) : Prop :=
  ∃ (uY : (LTwo ↥σ.K)ˣ) (ru : Fin σ.Tvec.length → (LTwo ↥σ.K)ˣ),
    IsGrUnit σ σ.Φ uY ∧
    (∀ i, IsGrUnit σ (σ.Tvec.get i).1 (ru i)) ∧
    (∏ i, ru i ^ (σ.Tvec.get i).2) * uY ^ σ.t = locT ↥σ.K

def CoeffLocLaw (σ : Stage p F) : Prop :=
  (∀ ℓ : LaurentPolynomial ↥σ.K, LaurentPolynomial.C ℓ ∈ σ.coeffLocSR →
      ∃ d : ↥σ.K, ℓ = LaurentPolynomial.C d)
  ∧ (∀ d : ↥σ.K, LaurentPolynomial.C (LaurentPolynomial.C d) ∈ σ.coeffLocSR)
  ∧ (∃ (zv : LVec σ) (u : (LTwo ↥σ.K)ˣ),
      σ.lvecWeight zv = 0 ∧ IsLVecVal σ zv u ∧
      (u : LTwo ↥σ.K) = LaurentPolynomial.C (zvar ↥σ.K))

structure StageCoreL (σ : Stage p F) : Prop where
  core : StageCore σ
  tvec_unit : TvecUnitLaw σ
  coeff_loc : CoeffLocLaw σ

lean/LeanUrat/Moves/DefsL.lean — structure

definition The landing cylinder with its triangular carry chain

The digit-cylinder shape that turns stratum membership into finitely many triangular equations; two audit-forced fixes are built in (slot terms kept as Laurent elements, terminal no-overflow recorded), while a third omission — the anchor-exactness pin — was later exposed by a countermodel family and is supplied as an explicit hypothesis in the two-sided landing theorem.

Conditions on the development data $B_0,\dots,B_{N-1}$ of a polynomial along a lifted key, defining the landing locus of a stratum: there is a side-line value $m_0$ with slot $\mu$ on it ($w(B_\mu)+\mu\, e h g=m_0$), no slot below it, and every slot $j<\mu$ strictly above it (BOX/VERTEX); and the anchored minimizing-slot Laurent terms $c_j = z^{j\hat m - a}\,R(B_j)$ (where $\hat m=-thg$; zero at non-minimizing slots) reproduce a prescribed digit pattern $(C_j)$ through a triangular carry chain: $\mathrm{carry}_0=0$, $c_j+\mathrm{carry}_j = C_j + \psi\cdot\mathrm{carry}_{j+1}$ with each digit $C_j$ the canonical representative of degree $<\deg\psi$, and — the recorded terminal no-overflow clause — every carry from level $N$ on vanishes.

The Lean statement 
def IsCarryChainL {K : Type*} [Field K] (ψ : Polynomial K) (c : ℕ → LaurentPolynomial K)
    (Cdig : ℕ → Polynomial K) (carry : ℕ → LaurentPolynomial K) : Prop :=
  carry 0 = 0 ∧
  ∀ j, (Cdig j).degree < ψ.degree ∧
    c j + carry j = Polynomial.toLaurent (Cdig j) + Polynomial.toLaurent ψ * carry (j + 1)

def CarryTransportL {K : Type*} [Field K] (ψ : Polynomial K) (c : ℕ → LaurentPolynomial K)
    (Cdig : ℕ → Polynomial K) (N : ℕ) : Prop :=
  ∃ carry : ℕ → LaurentPolynomial K,
    IsCarryChainL ψ c Cdig carry ∧ ∀ j, N ≤ j → carry j = 0

def LandingCylinderL (σ : Stage p F) (ψ : Polynomial ↥σ.K) (g : ℕ) (μ : ℕ) (a : ℤ)
    (Cdig : ℕ → Polynomial ↥σ.K) (B : ℕ → Polynomial ℤ_[p]) (N : ℕ) : Prop :=
  ∃ m₀ : ℤ,
    B μ ≠ 0 ∧ σ.w (B μ) + (μ : ℤ) * ((σ.e : ℤ) * σ.h * g) = m₀ ∧
    (∀ j, j < N → B j ≠ 0 → m₀ ≤ σ.w (B j) + (j : ℤ) * ((σ.e : ℤ) * σ.h * g)) ∧
    (∀ j, j < μ → B j ≠ 0 → m₀ < σ.w (B j) + (j : ℤ) * ((σ.e : ℤ) * σ.h * g)) ∧
    ∃ cL : ℕ → LaurentPolynomial ↥σ.K,
      (∀ j, j < N →
        ((B j ≠ 0 ∧ σ.w (B j) + (j : ℤ) * ((σ.e : ℤ) * σ.h * g) = m₀) →
          cL j = LaurentPolynomial.T ((j : ℤ) * (- σ.t * σ.h * g) - a) * σ.R (B j)) ∧
        (¬ (B j ≠ 0 ∧ σ.w (B j) + (j : ℤ) * ((σ.e : ℤ) * σ.h * g) = m₀) → cL j = 0)) ∧
      (∀ j, N ≤ j → cL j = 0) ∧
      CarryTransportL ψ cL Cdig N

lean/LeanUrat/Moves/DefsL.lean — definition

proved Every polynomial has a development along a monic key

The base-of-everything Fact A: stated polymorphically over any commutative ring so it descends to the reduced digit boxes mod p^N used by the counting endpoint; heavily imported by the HC1/HC2 hard-core corpora.

Over any commutative ring $R$: for every monic $\Phi\in R[X]$ with $\deg\Phi\ge 1$ and every $f\in R[X]$ there exist coefficients $B_0,B_1,\dots$ and a length $N$ with $\deg B_j<\deg\Phi$ for all $j$, $B_j=0$ for $j\ge N$, and $f=\sum_{j<N}B_j\,\Phi^{\,j}$ — the $\Phi$-adic development.

The Lean statement 
theorem L0_FactA_exists {R : Type*} [CommRing R] (Φ : Polynomial R) (hΦ : Φ.Monic)
    (hd : 1 ≤ Φ.natDegree) (f : Polynomial R) :
    ∃ (B : ℕ → Polynomial R) (N : ℕ), IsDevelopment Φ f B N

lean/LeanUrat/Moves/L0_FactA_exists.lean — theorem

proved Developments along a monic key are unique

Fact B: uniqueness is what lets digit patterns be read off canonically — the landing theorems and the pattern-recovery direction of the capstone lean on it directly.

Over any commutative ring: two $\Phi$-adic developments of the same polynomial $f$ along the same monic key $\Phi$ agree in every slot, $B_j = B'_j$ for all $j$ (regardless of their recorded lengths).

The Lean statement 
theorem L0_FactB_unique {R : Type*} [CommRing R] (Φ : Polynomial R) (hΦ : Φ.Monic) (f : Polynomial R) {B B' : ℕ → Polynomial R} {N N' : ℕ} (h : IsDevelopment Φ f B N) (h' : IsDevelopment Φ f B' N') : ∀ j, B j = B' j

lean/LeanUrat/Moves/L0_FactB_unique.lean — theorem

proved The Gauss valuation is multiplicative

The zeroth-level valuation of the tower; the base stage valuation is built from it.

For nonzero $f,g\in\mathbb{Z}_p[X]$, the Gauss (content) valuation $v(f)=\min_i v_p(a_i)$ over the coefficients satisfies $v(fg)=v(f)+v(g)$ — Gauss's lemma in valuation form.

The Lean statement 
theorem L1_gaussVal_mul {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] (f g : Polynomial ℤ_[p]) (hf : f ≠ 0) (hg : g ≠ 0) : gaussVal (f * g) = gaussVal f + gaussVal g

lean/LeanUrat/Moves/L1_gaussVal.lean — theorem

proved The first-level tower valuation is multiplicative (the MacLane base case)

The base case that anchors the whole tower in a genuine valuation; it was left open (a fenced unproved goal) for one development round and was discharged 2026-07-27 by a direct MacLane-style argument (Cauchy-product development with carries), with no graded-ring machinery.

Let $\varphi\in\mathbb{Z}_p[X]$ be monic of degree $\ge 1$ and irreducible mod $p$, and let $(e,h)$ be coprime positive integers. Define $w(f)=\min_i\big(e\,v(a_i)+i\,h\big)$ over the $\varphi$-adic development $f=\sum_i a_i\varphi^i$ ($v$ the Gauss valuation). Then $w(fg)=w(f)+w(g)$ for nonzero $f,g$ — the first level of the tower is a genuine valuation.

The Lean statement 
theorem L1_baseWeight_valuation {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] (φ : Polynomial ℤ_[p]) (e h : ℕ) (hmon : φ.Monic) (hd : 1 ≤ φ.natDegree) (he : 1 ≤ e) (hh : 1 ≤ h) (hcop : Nat.gcd e h = 1) (hirred : Irreducible (φ.map (PadicInt.toZMod : ℤ_[p] →+* ZMod p))) (f g : Polynomial ℤ_[p]) (hf : f ≠ 0) (hg : g ≠ 0) : baseWeight φ e h (f * g) = baseWeight φ e h f + baseWeight φ e h g

lean/LeanUrat/Moves/L1_baseWeight_R3.lean — theorem

proved Slot decomposition and slot independence of the residual map

Converts the raw residual laws into the graded decomposition plus the transcendence half a semantic audit demanded — the two facts every downstream landing and transition argument transports.

Given the strictness law ($w(f+g)=w(f)$ when $w(f)<w(g)$), the jump law (equal weights whose sum's weight jumps force $R(f)+R(g)=0$), and $R(-f)=-R(f)$, the stage satisfies both halves of slot decomposition: (i) within one graded piece, the residual of a development whose nonzero slots all have total weight $\gamma$ and whose sum stays at weight $\gamma$ is the sum of the slot residuals; (ii) independence: a combination $\sum_j R(B_j)\,R(\Phi)^j$ whose nonzero slots all live in one graded piece vanishes only termwise — the class of the key is transcendental over the coefficient classes, degreewise.

The Lean statement 
theorem L2_slotDecomp {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] {F : Type*} [Field F] [Finite F] (σ : Stage p F) (hstrict : ∀ f g, f ≠ 0 → g ≠ 0 → σ.w f < σ.w g → σ.w (f + g) = σ.w f) (hjump : ∀ f g, f ≠ 0 → g ≠ 0 → f + g ≠ 0 → σ.w f = σ.w g → σ.w f < σ.w (f + g) → σ.R f + σ.R g = 0) (hneg : ∀ f, σ.R (-f) = - σ.R f) : SlotDecomp σ

lean/LeanUrat/Moves/L2_slotDecomp_R4.lean — theorem

proved The stride rule: residuals of coefficient-times-key-power

The workhorse computation rule: it forces the distinct z-positions that make slot terms independent, and feeds the realizer and landing arguments throughout.

For a nonzero coefficient $B$ (i.e. $\deg B<\deg\Phi$) and any $j\ge 0$: $R(B\cdot\Phi^j)=R(B)\cdot z^{\,s j}$. Multiplying by the key strides the residual by a fixed power of $z$.

The Lean statement 
theorem L2_strideRule {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] {F : Type*} [Field F] [Finite F] (σ : Stage p F) (B : Polynomial ℤ_[p]) (hB : B ≠ 0) (hBc : inC σ.Φ B) (j : ℕ) : σ.R (B * σ.Φ ^ j) = σ.R B * LaurentPolynomial.T (σ.s * j)

lean/LeanUrat/Moves/L2_strideRule.lean — theorem

proved Structure of a graded piece: injective, additive, base-field-linear, with known image

Identifies each graded piece of the valuation completely; the scalar clause was repaired at audit round 4 (the earlier form did not conclude weight preservation, so it was not an endomorphism of the piece), and this final restatement is fully proved.

Fix a weight $\gamma$. On the weight-$\gamma$ piece: (1) the residual map is injective up to higher weight — $R(f)=R(f')$ forces $f=f'$ or $w(f-f')>\gamma$; (2) it is additive when the sum stays at weight $\gamma$; (3) multiplication by a weight-0 coefficient lift $\hat u$ whose residual is a base-field constant $c\in F_Q$ preserves the piece and scales the residual by $c$ — an $F_Q$-linear endomorphism of the graded piece; (4)+(5) the image of the piece under $R$ is exactly the nonzero part of the additive span of the slot images.

The Lean statement 
theorem L2_P6i {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] {F : Type*} [Field F] [Finite F] (σ : Stage p F) (hcore : StageCore σ) (γ : ℤ) : (∀ f f', f ≠ 0 → f' ≠ 0 → σ.w f = γ → σ.w f' = γ → σ.R f = σ.R f' → f - f' = 0 ∨ γ < σ.w (f - f')) ∧ (∀ f f', f ≠ 0 → f' ≠ 0 → f + f' ≠ 0 → σ.w f = γ → σ.w f' = γ → σ.w (f + f') = γ → σ.R (f + f') = σ.R f + σ.R f') ∧ (∀ (û : Polynomial ℤ_[p]) (c : ↥σ.FQ), û ≠ 0 → c ≠ 0 → inC σ.Φ û → σ.w û = 0 → σ.R û = LaurentPolynomial.C (Subfield.inclusion σ.hFQ_le c) * LaurentPolynomial.T (0 : ℤ) → ∀ f, f ≠ 0 → σ.w f = γ → σ.w (û * f) = γ ∧ σ.R (û * f) = LaurentPolynomial.C (Subfield.inclusion σ.hFQ_le c) * σ.R f) ∧ (∀ f, f ≠ 0 → σ.w f = γ → σ.R f ∈ AddSubgroup.closure (σ.slotImages γ)) ∧ (∀ x ∈ AddSubgroup.closure (σ.slotImages γ), x ≠ 0 → ∃ f, f ≠ 0 ∧ σ.w f = γ ∧ σ.R f = x)

lean/LeanUrat/Moves/L2_P6i_R5_final.lean — theorem

proved The attained-minimum weight law at a lifted key

The central weight formula of the engine (MacLane's key lemma at the lifted key): it is what lets weights and residuals be read off development slots, and both landing directions reduce to it.

Let $\psi$ be monic irreducible over the current residue field $K$, $\psi\ne z$, $\deg\psi=g$, and let $\hat\Phi$ be its standard lift (the displayed formula $\hat\Phi=\Phi^{eg}+\sum_k t_k\Phi^{ek}$ with prescribed realizer coefficients). Then for every nonzero $f$ and every development $f=\sum_j B_j\hat\Phi^j$: $w(f)=\min_j\big(w(B_j)+j\cdot ehg\big)$, and the minimum is attained.

The Lean statement 
theorem L3_K1 {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] {F : Type*} [Field F] [Finite F] (σ : Stage p F) (ψ : Polynomial ↥σ.K) (g : ℕ) (hg : ψ.natDegree = g) (hψ : Irreducible ψ) (hψz : ψ ≠ Polynomial.X) (hmon : ψ.Monic) (Φhat : Polynomial ℤ_[p]) (hlift : IsStandardLift σ ψ g Φhat) : K1At σ.w Φhat ((σ.e : ℤ) * σ.h * g)

lean/LeanUrat/Moves/L3_K1.lean — theorem

proved Weights and digits multiply through division by the lifted key

The one-step residue-field multiplication: it is how the residue field grows by the degree of psi at each tower step, feeding the transition construction in HC1.

Same setup, with $\bar z\in F$ a root of $\psi$: for nonzero $B,B''$ in the coefficient space of the lifted key $\hat\Phi$ (degree $<eg\cdot\deg\Phi$), writing $B\cdot B''=Q\hat\Phi+R_{\mathrm{dev}}$ with $\deg R_{\mathrm{dev}}<eg\cdot\deg\Phi$: the remainder is nonzero, $w(R_{\mathrm{dev}})=w(B)+w(B'')$, and the residue digit is multiplicative, $\mathrm{dig}'_{\bar z}(R_{\mathrm{dev}})=\mathrm{dig}'_{\bar z}(B)\cdot\mathrm{dig}'_{\bar z}(B'')$.

The Lean statement 
theorem L3_DIV {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] {F : Type*} [Field F] [Finite F] (σ : Stage p F) (ψ : Polynomial ↥σ.K) (g : ℕ) (hg : ψ.natDegree = g) (hψ : Irreducible ψ) (hψz : ψ ≠ Polynomial.X) (Φhat : Polynomial ℤ_[p]) (hlift : IsStandardLift σ ψ g Φhat) (zbar : Fˣ) (hzbar : Polynomial.eval₂ σ.K.subtype (zbar : F) ψ = 0) (B B'' : Polynomial ℤ_[p]) (hB : B ≠ 0) (hB'' : B'' ≠ 0) (hBd : B.natDegree < σ.e * g * σ.Φ.natDegree) (hB''d : B''.natDegree < σ.e * g * σ.Φ.natDegree) (Rdev Q : Polynomial ℤ_[p]) (hdiv : B * B'' = Q * Φhat + Rdev) (hRd : Rdev.natDegree < σ.e * g * σ.Φ.natDegree) : Rdev ≠ 0 ∧ σ.w Rdev = σ.w B + σ.w B'' ∧ σ.digPrime zbar Rdev = σ.digPrime zbar B * σ.digPrime zbar B''

lean/LeanUrat/Moves/L3_DIV.lean — theorem

proved The index recursion and single-orbit realizers at a tower transition

The realizer supply that lets the induction climb one level: how the coefficient-weight index set and its realizing digits recur at a transition; its conclusion was weakened from a fixed root-monomial class to the coset form (2026-07-28) after the stronger untwisted form was machine-refuted, and a companion child-stage-free variant was added to break a circularity found in the HC1 induction.

At a recorded transition from stage $\sigma$ to child $\sigma'$ along a lifted key of degree $eg\cdot\deg\Phi$: (1) the parent-scale weights occurring in the child coefficient space are exactly $\{\gamma+jh : \gamma$ an occurring parent weight, $0\le j<eg\}$; and (2) for each such index $(\gamma,j)$ there is one fixed base unit $d_0$ of the child residue field such that every base-field orbit scalar $c\in F_Q^\times$ is realized by an element $B$ of the child coefficient space with parent weight $\gamma+jh$, child weight $e'(\gamma+jh)$, and child residual a monomial at the forced position with digit scalar $d=c\cdot d_0$ — the realizer digits of one index sweep exactly one coset of the base-field orbit.

The Lean statement 
theorem L4_TRANSviii_a {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] {F : Type*} [Field F] [Finite F] (σ σ' : Stage p F) (hcore : StageCoreL σ) (g : ℕ) (Φhat : Polynomial ℤ_[p]) (hΦdeg : Φhat.natDegree = σ.e * g * σ.Φ.natDegree) (e' h' : ℕ) (zbar : Fˣ) (htrans : TransitionCoreL σ σ' Φhat e' h' zbar) : (∀ W' : ℤ, (∃ B, B ≠ 0 ∧ inC Φhat B ∧ σ'.wPrev B = W') ↔ (∃ (γ : ℤ) (j : ℕ), (∃ C, C ≠ 0 ∧ inC σ.Φ C ∧ σ.w C = γ) ∧ j < σ.e * g ∧ W' = γ + (j : ℤ) * σ.h)) ∧ (∀ (γ : ℤ) (j : ℕ), (∃ C, C ≠ 0 ∧ inC σ.Φ C ∧ σ.w C = γ) → j < σ.e * g → ∃ d₀ : (↥σ'.K)ˣ, ∀ c : (↥σ'.K)ˣ, ((c : ↥σ'.K) : F) ∈ σ'.FQ → ∃ B, B ≠ 0 ∧ inC Φhat B ∧ σ'.wPrev B = γ + (j : ℤ) * σ.h ∧ σ'.w B = (σ'.e : ℤ) * (γ + (j : ℤ) * σ.h) ∧ ∃ d : (↥σ'.K)ˣ, σ'.R B = LaurentPolynomial.C (d : ↥σ'.K) * LaurentPolynomial.T (- σ'.t * (γ + (j : ℤ) * σ.h)) ∧ ((d : ↥σ'.K) : F) = ((c : ↥σ'.K) : F) * ((d₀ : ↥σ'.K) : F))

lean/LeanUrat/Moves/L4_TRANSviii_a_R5_final.lean — theorem

proved The two-sided landing theorem: stratum membership equals cylinder membership

THE capstone of this corpus, proved in both directions with no sorry: it converts Newton-polygon stratum membership into digit equations on development data; the hexact hypothesis was added in a round-6 repair after a countermodel family (shifting the anchor and re-developing) showed the pin-free version false, and the audit record scopes the result to the abstract stage laws (the graded-localization provenance is certified separately in HC1).

Let $\psi$ be monic irreducible over $K$, $\psi\ne z$, of degree $g$, with standard lift $\hat\Phi$; let $f\ne 0$ have $\hat\Phi$-adic development $(B_j)_{j<N}$, let $\mu<N$, and assume the anchor-exactness pin: $X\nmid\sum_{j<N}C_j\psi^j$ (the prescribed digit pattern's total has nonzero constant coefficient). Then $f$ lies in the $(\mu,a,(C_j))$-stratum — meaning $z^{-a}R(f)$ is a polynomial with nonzero constant term whose $\psi$-adic development has digits $(C_j)$, vanishing below slot $\mu$ and nonzero at $\mu$ — if and only if the development data $(B_j)$ lies in the landing cylinder (the side-line BOX/VERTEX conditions plus the anchored Laurent slot terms reproducing $(C_j)$ through the terminating carry chain).

The Lean statement 
theorem L5_landTwoSided {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] {F : Type*} [Field F] [Finite F] (σ : Stage p F) (hcore : StageCore σ) (ψ : Polynomial ↥σ.K) (g : ℕ) (hg : ψ.natDegree = g) (hgpos : 1 ≤ g) (hmon : ψ.Monic) (hψ : Irreducible ψ) (hψz : ψ ≠ Polynomial.X) (Φhat : Polynomial ℤ_[p]) (hlift : IsStandardLift σ ψ g Φhat) (μ : ℕ) (f : Polynomial ℤ_[p]) (hf : f ≠ 0) (B : ℕ → Polynomial ℤ_[p]) (N : ℕ) (hμN : μ < N) (hdev : IsDevelopment Φhat f B N) (a : ℤ) (Cdig : ℕ → Polynomial ↥σ.K) (hexact : ¬ Polynomial.X ∣ ∑ j ∈ Finset.range N, Cdig j * ψ ^ j) : StratumData σ ψ μ a Cdig N f ↔ LandingCylinderL σ ψ g μ a Cdig B N

lean/LeanUrat/Moves/L5_landTwoSided_repair.lean — theorem

proved Residuals are determined by slot data (transport)

The determinacy fact behind measure exactness — elements with the same digit data are residually indistinguishable; this is the corrected restatement after the untied original was refuted (see the refutation-record node).

With $\hat\Phi$ the standard lift of an irreducible $\psi\ne z$ and the attained-minimum law holding at $\hat\Phi$: if two nonzero polynomials $f,f'$ have developments along $\hat\Phi$ that agree slotwise in both residual and weight ($R(B_j)=R(B'_j)$ and $w(B_j)=w(B'_j)$ for all $j<N$), then $R(f)=R(f')$.

lean/LeanUrat/Moves/L5_landTransport_R4.lean — theorem

open Transport without tying the key to the stage is false (refutation record)

A refutation record kept on disk: the fenced round-3 statement is deliberately left unproved because it is refuted — the explicit countermodel is documented in the file header (as a verified analysis in the intended model, not a compiled Lean disproof) — and the repaired restatement with the key tied to the stage is the proved transport node.

The same transport statement quantified over an arbitrary development key $\hat\Phi$, untied to the stage (no standard-lift or attained-minimum hypothesis), is false in the intended base-stage model: with $\hat\Phi=1+X$, $f=p+X$, $f'=p^2+X$, all slot residuals and slot weights agree, yet $R(f)\ne R(f')$ — the minimal-weight block cancels residually and the residual is read off sub-leading data that slot residuals do not pin.

lean/LeanUrat/Moves/L5_landTransport_R3.lean — countermodel

proved Machine-checked disproof: reduction of developments fails at level zero

A compiled refutation from development: the originally specified statement is false-as-stated and was disproved in Lean rather than silently weakened — the false version is kept on record behind a statement fence (an unproved goal), and the corrected version (next node) was proved separately.

It is NOT the case that for every prime $p$, monic key $\hat\Phi$, development $f=\sum_j B_j\hat\Phi^j$, and every $M\ge 0$, the coefficientwise reduction mod $p^M$ is again a development: at $M=0$ the target ring $\mathbb{Z}/p^0=\mathbb{Z}/1$ is trivial, so all mapped polynomials vanish and the degree clause $\deg B_j<\deg\hat\Phi$ fails. Compiled disproof by the witness $\hat\Phi=X$, $f=1$ over $\mathbb{Z}_2$.

The Lean statement

        ∀ (B : ℕ → Polynomial ℤ_[p]) (N M : ℕ), IsDevelopment Φhat f B N →
        IsDevelopment (Φhat.map (PadicInt.toZModPow M)) (f.map (PadicInt.toZModPow M))
          (fun j => (B j).map (PadicInt.toZModPow M)) N)

lean/LeanUrat/Moves/L6_moveReduceCommute_R3.lean — countermodel

proved Developments commute with reduction modulo prime powers

The bridge from p-adic development data to the finite digit boxes where counting happens; corrected form (adds M >= 1) after the machine-checked disproof of the unguarded original.

For every $M\ge 1$: if $f=\sum_{j<N}B_j\hat\Phi^j$ is a development along the monic key $\hat\Phi$ over $\mathbb{Z}_p$, then reducing every ingredient mod $p^M$ yields a development of the reduction of $f$ along the reduction of $\hat\Phi$, with the same slots and length.

The Lean statement 
theorem L6_moveReduceCommute {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] (Φhat f : Polynomial ℤ_[p]) (hmon : Φhat.Monic) (B : ℕ → Polynomial ℤ_[p]) (N M : ℕ) (hM : 1 ≤ M) (hdev : IsDevelopment Φhat f B N) : IsDevelopment (Φhat.map (PadicInt.toZModPow M)) (f.map (PadicInt.toZModPow M)) (fun j => (B j).map (PadicInt.toZModPow M)) N

lean/LeanUrat/Moves/L6_moveReduceCommute_R4.lean — theorem

conditional The stratum measure is exactly 1/p^k, independently of the reading level

The counting endpoint of the corpus: machine-checked with no axioms beyond Lean's core since a 2026-07-30 revision (approved by the project lead) made it consume the two-sided landing theorem — but conditional: the jet-coordinate presentations are hypothesized, not built, so this is the shape of the measure-exactness endpoint rather than the derived endpoint itself; the corresponding statement change still awaits an independent semantic review.

Fix a stratum (a vertex $\mu$, anchor $a$, digit pattern $(C_j)$ with the anchor-exactness pin) and let $D$ be the degree budget. Suppose that at two levels $N$ and $M$ we are given coordinate presentations: surjective codes of polynomials of degree $<D$ onto the digit boxes $(\mathbb{Z}/p)^{DN}$ and $(\mathbb{Z}/p)^{DM}$ presenting the stratum, coordinate changes $\Theta$, and digit systems each pinning exactly $k$ coordinates whose solution sets present the landing cylinder (and reject the zero polynomial's coordinates). Then the two densities agree: $\#\{\text{stratum solutions at level }N\}/p^{DN} = \#\{\text{stratum solutions at level }M\}/p^{DM}$ — the stratum has one exact measure, $1/p^k$, at every level.

Hypotheses this rests on: The supplied jet-coordinate presentations, taken as named hypotheses rather than constructed: hcodeN/hcodeM (surjective coefficient codes onto the digit boxes) + hstratN/hstratM (the codes present the stratum); hcylN/hcylM + hENk/hEMk (digit systems pinning exactly k coordinates whose solutions present the landing cylinder through the coordinate changes Θ); hzsolN/hzsolM (the digit systems reject the zero polynomial's coordinates); hexact (the anchor-exactness pin on the digit pattern).

The Lean statement 
theorem L6_measureExact {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] {F : Type*} [Field F] [Finite F] (σ : Stage p F) (hcore : StageCore σ) (ψ : Polynomial ↥σ.K) (g : ℕ) (hg : ψ.natDegree = g) (hgpos : 1 ≤ g) (hmon : ψ.Monic) (hψ : Irreducible ψ) (hψz : ψ ≠ Polynomial.X) (Φhat : Polynomial ℤ_[p]) (hlift : IsStandardLift σ ψ g Φhat) (μ : ℕ) (a : ℤ) (Cdig : ℕ → Polynomial ↥σ.K) (Nslots : ℕ) (hμN : μ < Nslots) lean/LeanUrat/Moves/L6_measureExact_R4.lean — theorem

proved A stratum cut by k digit equations has exactly p^(nN-k) points

The final arithmetic step of the story: once the landing theorems have turned a stratum into k triangular digit equations, this is why its density is a power of 1/p — the q-uniformity the whole project is about.

If the solution set of a stratum on the digit box $(\mathbb{Z}/p^N)^n$ is in bijection with $(\mathbb{Z}/p)^{nN-k}$ — the shape produced by $k$ unitriangular digit equations — then its cardinality is exactly $p^{\,nN-k}$.

The Lean statement 
theorem L6_ledgerCount {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] (n N k : ℕ) (stratum : (Fin n → ZMod (p ^ N)) → Prop) (e : {x // stratum x} ≃ (Fin (n * N - k) → ZMod p)) : Nat.card {x // stratum x} = p ^ (n * N - k)

lean/LeanUrat/Moves/L6_ledgerCount.lean — theorem

Coordinates, masses, Theorem C (MovesC) 4 definition · 9 proved · 6 conditional
Each classifier read cuts exact volume; masses multiply to Theorem C

MovesC formalizes the composition-and-counting theorem (Theorem C) of the classifier analysis: it makes exact the volume bookkeeping of the Okutsu–Montes classifier's successive reads on the digit box of a monic degree-n p-adic polynomial truncated at p-adic level N. The bottom layers prove that triangular digit systems have mass p^(m−pins) (pins = the number of coordinates the system forces to a single value), that pinned-or-free status is determined by the solution set alone, and that the classifier's coordinate moves are pin-preserving bijections of the box; the middle layers prove each read cuts its state cylinder by exactly its nominal volume p^(−m*); the top layer assembles Theorem C: the classifier locus S(H,Z) satisfies #S(H,Z)·p^(Σm* + pins(Z)) = p^(nN), division-free. All 36 units (one stated Lean theorem or definition per unit) are proved — machine-checked, with no axioms beyond Lean's core — after six rounds of adversarial review that killed several vacuity loopholes (including a certified empty-stratum countermodel). The conditionality: every theorem about a real read history is stated against a hypothesized jet-presentation interface whose construction from the actual classifier — the graded-ring bridge — is the corpus's declared open boundary.

Builds on: Moves

definition Loci as triangular digit systems, and their mass

The common currency of the corpus: every counting statement is an identity between masses of loci, and "codimension" always means number of pinned coordinates (the underlying digit-system structure is inherited from the Moves corpus).

The ambient box is $\mathbb{F}_p^{\,m}$ with $m = nN$: the first $N$ base-$p$ digits of each of the $n$ coefficients of a monic degree-$n$ polynomial over $\mathbb{Z}_p$, listed in a fixed global solving order $\prec$ (digit level ascending; within a level, top coefficient first). A locus is a triangular digit system on this box: a subset of pinned coordinates, each pinned coordinate given as a function of the strictly $\prec$-earlier coordinates. Its solution set is a cylinder, and its mass is the number of solutions among the $p^m$ box points.

The Lean statement 
-- section variables: {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
abbrev Locus (p m : ℕ) : Type := DigitSystem m (ZMod p)

noncomputable def Locus.mass {p m : ℕ} (D : Locus p m) : ℕ :=
  Nat.card {x : Fin m → ZMod p // D.IsSolution x}

lean/LeanUrat/MovesC/Defs.lean — definition

definition Address-free fresh clause: support, codimension, exact count

The audit-driven "address-free" design: counting consumes only support, codimension, and the exact-count law, so no clause can smuggle in an unverified pin address — and the count law is self-policing (an unsatisfiable clause cannot even be instantiated).

A fresh clause on the box is carried by exactly three statistics: a support $S$ (the coordinates it is allowed to read — enforced as an axiom of the structure), a codimension $a$, and the exact-count law $\#\{y \text{ supported on } S : y \text{ satisfies the clause}\}\cdot p^{a} = p^{|S|}$ — the clause cuts exactly the fraction $p^{-a}$, stated division-free. The fresh data of one classifier read is a finite list of such clauses with pairwise disjoint supports; its total codimension is $m^* = \sum a$.

The Lean statement 
structure LevelClause (p m : ℕ) where
  support : Finset (Fin m)
  codim : ℕ
  sat : (Fin m → ZMod p) → Prop
  dep : ∀ x y : Fin m → ZMod p, (∀ c ∈ support, x c = y c) → (sat x ↔ sat y)
  count : Nat.card {y : Fin m → ZMod p // sat y ∧ ∀ c ∉ support, y c = 0} * p ^ codim
    = p ^ support.card

structure FreshData (p m : ℕ) where
  clauses : List (LevelClause p m)
  disj : clauses.Pairwise (fun c₁ c₂ => Disjoint c₁.support c₂.support)

def FreshData.mstar {p m : ℕ} (fd : FreshData p m) : ℕ :=
  (fd.clauses.map LevelClause.codim).sum

lean/LeanUrat/MovesC/Defs.lean — structure

definition Admissible transitions and realizable histories (the empty-stratum fence)

An earlier two-condition version admitted a certified countermodel (the "F-EMPTY" instance — a history with nominal fresh volume $2^{-6}$ whose actual stratum census is 0 points); the strict span-entry condition (SAE) was added at audit round 4 to exclude it, and all downstream theorems now hypothesize the full class.

A read $\nu$ appended after the standing node $\nu_p$ is transition-admissible when three conditions hold on the recorded data alone: (NA) if the new side ends strictly left of the standing window vertex, the extended new line passes strictly below the pinned old vertex height; (HV) if the read is adjacent (its side reaches the vertex), its lead pattern digit equals the transported vertex value in the residue field; (SAE) the new side lies strictly above the old line at every span slot left of the vertex, with equality at the vertex in the adjacent case. A history is realizable when every consecutive pair of its nodes is transition-admissible. Theorem C's quantifier ranges over realizable histories only: a history failing any of the three conditions has empty stratum and is never emitted by the classifier.

The Lean statement 
-- section variables: {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] {F : Type*} [Field F] [Finite F]
def TransitionAdmissible (νp ν : Node p F) : Prop :=
  (ν.s0 + ν.wSide < νp.μ →
    ν.line.at (νp.μ * νp.childWidth) < νp.line.at (νp.μ * νp.childWidth)) ∧
  (ν.s0 + ν.wSide = νp.μ →
    ((ν.pat (ν.wSide / ν.e) : ↥ν.σ.K) : F) = νp.vtx) ∧
  (∀ j : ℕ, ν.s0 ≤ j → j ≤ ν.s0 + ν.wSide → j < νp.μ →
    νp.line.at (j * νp.childWidth) < ν.line.at (j * νp.childWidth)) ∧
  (ν.s0 + ν.wSide = νp.μ →
    ν.line.at (νp.μ * νp.childWidth) = νp.line.at (νp.μ * νp.childWidth))

def Realizable (H : History p F) : Prop :=
  ∀ (i : ℕ) (hi : i + 1 < H.nodes.length),
    TransitionAdmissible (H.nodes[i]'(by omega)) (H.nodes[i+1]'hi)

lean/LeanUrat/MovesC/Defs.lean — definition

definition The jet-presentation interface: the declared boundary with the unbuilt graded ring

The corpus's declared trust boundary, hardened over six adversarial audit rounds (the fresh-band laws kill clause-permutation and all-singleton-zero impersonations that earlier rounds admitted); what remains open is constructing this bundle for a real classifier history — the unbuilt graded-ring bridge — plus two flagged identifications on the height function.

A jet presentation of a read history $H$ at level cutoff $N \geq 1$ bundles everything Theorem C needs, each field a provenance sentence tied to the recorded nodes: a $\prec$-sorted chart of the $nN$-digit box; coherence and realizability of $H$; state cylinders $\Sigma_i$ (with $\Sigma_0$ the full box); per-read fresh clause data; unitriangular move maps $\Theta_i$; recorded landing keys; a presentation predicate "$x$ is the frame-$i$ digit presentation of the polynomial $f$" that is literal $p$-adic digits at frame 0, total on monic degree-$n$ polynomials, intertwined by the moves, and block-local over key developments; the stratum recursion $\Sigma_{i+1} = \Theta_i^*(\Sigma_i \cap \text{stratum}_i)$ with inherited content implied on the cylinder; the exact zero-downset invariant (a coordinate is pinned iff its height is at most the cumulative floor, and interior pins are literal zeros); and the fresh-band laws forcing every clause to be either a strip (one coordinate, codimension 1, a literal zero, never at a value position) or a value clause (the complete level set of one recorded span slot, codimension = its size), with the total $m^*$ equal to the geometric band count computed from node data.

The Lean statement 
-- section variables: {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] {F : Type*} [Field F] [Finite F]
structure JetSetup (H : History p F) (n N m : ℕ) where
  hm : m = n * N
  hN : 1 ≤ N
  coordOf : Fin m → Coord
  coordOf_sorted : ∀ j j' : Fin m, j < j' ↔ CoordPrec (coordOf j) (coordOf j')
  coordOf_lt : ∀ j : Fin m, (coordOf j).1 < N ∧ (coordOf j).2 < n
  coherent : HistoryCoherent H
  realizable : Realizable H
  root_height : ∀ (hj : 0 < H.nodes.length) (b : ℕ),
    b < (H.nodes[0]'hj).μ * (H.nodes[0]'hj).childWidth → 1 ≤ (H.nodes[0]'hj).line.at b
  Sigma : ℕ → Locus p m
  init : ∀ c : Fin m, (Sigma 0).pinned c = false
  fresh : ℕ → FreshData p m
  Theta : ℕ → ((Fin m → ZMod p) → (Fin m → ZMod p))
  Theta_uni : ∀ i : ℕ, IsUnitriangular (Theta i)
  keys : ℕ → Polynomial ℤ_[p]
  keys_mid : ∀ (i : ℕ) (hi : i + 1 < H.nodes.length), keys i = (H.nodes[i+1]'hi).σ.Φ
  landing : ∀ (i : ℕ) (hi : i < H.nodes.length), LandingKey (H.nodes[i]'hi) (keys i)
  pres : ℕ → (Fin m → ZMod p) → Polynomial ℤ_[p] → Prop
  pres_zero : ∀ (x : Fin m → ZMod p) (f : Polynomial ℤ_[p]),
    pres 0 x f ↔ ∀ j : Fin m, x j = baseDigit p (coordOf j).1 (f.coeff (coordOf j).2)
  pres_total : ∀ i : ℕ, i ≤ H.nodes.length → ∀ f : Polynomial ℤ_[p],
    f.Monic → f.natDegree = n → ∃ x, pres i x f
  pres_theta : ∀ i : ℕ, i < H.nodes.length →
    ∀ (x : Fin m → ZMod p) (f : Polynomial ℤ_[p]), pres (i+1) x f → pres i (Theta i x) f
  pres_block : ∀ i : ℕ, i < H.nodes.length →
    ∀ (f f' : Polynomial ℤ_[p]) (x x' : Fin m → ZMod p), pres (i+1) x f → pres (i+1) x' f' →
    ∀ (B B' : ℕ → Polynomial ℤ_[p]) (Nd Nd' : ℕ),
      IsDevelopment (keys i) f B Nd → IsDevelopment (keys i) f' B' Nd' →
      ∀ c : Fin m,
        B ((coordOf c).2 / (keys i).natDegree) = B' ((coordOf c).2 / (keys i).natDegree) →
        x c = x' c
  stratum : ℕ → (Fin m → ZMod p) → Prop
  recursion : ∀ i : ℕ, i < H.nodes.length → ∀ x : Fin m → ZMod p,
    (Sigma (i+1)).IsSolution x ↔ ((Sigma i).IsSolution (Theta i x) ∧ stratum i (Theta i x))
  inh_implied : ∀ i : ℕ, i < H.nodes.length → ∀ y : Fin m → ZMod p,
    (Sigma i).IsSolution y → (stratum i y ↔ (fresh i).sat y)
  zc : ∀ (i : ℕ) (hi : i < H.nodes.length),
    ZCData (Sigma (i+1)) coordOf (H.htH (i+1)) (H.floorH (i+1))
      ((H.nodes[i]'hi).μ * (H.nodes[i]'hi).childWidth)
  fresh_band : ∀ (i : ℕ) (hi : i < H.nodes.length), ∀ cl ∈ (fresh i).clauses,
    ∀ c ∈ cl.support, inFreshBand H n coordOf i (H.nodes[i]'hi) c
  fresh_cover : ∀ (i : ℕ) (hi : i < H.nodes.length), ∀ c : Fin m,
    inFreshBand H n coordOf i (H.nodes[i]'hi) c → ∃ cl ∈ (fresh i).clauses, c ∈ cl.support
  fresh_assembled : ∀ (i : ℕ) (hi : i < H.nodes.length), ∀ cl ∈ (fresh i).clauses,
    (∃ c : Fin m, cl.support = {c} ∧ cl.codim = 1 ∧ (∀ x, (cl.sat x ↔ x c = 0)) ∧
      ¬ IsValueCoord H coordOf i (H.nodes[i]'hi) c) ∨
    (IsValueSupport H coordOf i (H.nodes[i]'hi) cl.support ∧
      cl.codim = cl.support.card ∧
      ∃ (T : TypObject p m cl.support (Fin cl.codim → ZMod p)) (v : Fin cl.codim → ZMod p),
        ∀ x, (cl.sat x ↔ T.φ x = v))
  mstar_eq : ∀ (i : ℕ) (hi : i < H.nodes.length),
    (fresh i).mstar = Nat.card {c : Fin m // inFreshBand H n coordOf i (H.nodes[i]'hi) c}

lean/LeanUrat/MovesC/Defs.lean — structure

proved Mass of a digit system is p to the number of free coordinates

The base counting law: every density computation in the corpus reduces to pin bookkeeping through this identity (a specialization of the general alphabet count proved in the Moves corpus).

Let $D$ be a triangular digit system on $m$ coordinates over $\mathbb{F}_p$ with $k$ pinned coordinates. Then the number of solutions of $D$ in the box $\mathbb{F}_p^m$ is exactly $p^{m-k}$.

The Lean statement 
-- section variables: {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
theorem C0_digitSystemMass {m : ℕ} (D : Locus p m) : D.mass = p ^ (m - D.numPinned)

lean/LeanUrat/MovesC/C0_digitSystemMass.lean — theorem

proved Pinned-or-free status is determined by the solution set alone

Makes codimension bookkeeping well-defined coordinate by coordinate (strengthening the mass-equality version), so systems transported through moves can be compared pin by pin.

If two triangular digit systems $D, D'$ on the same $m$-coordinate box have identical solution sets, then every coordinate has the same pinned/free status in both. In particular the codimension (pin count) is an invariant of the cylinder, not of its presentation.

The Lean statement 
-- section variables: {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
theorem C0_pinStatus {m : ℕ} (D D' : Locus p m) (h : ∀ x, D.IsSolution x ↔ D'.IsSolution x) : ∀ j : Fin m, D.pinned j = D'.pinned j

lean/LeanUrat/MovesC/C0_pinStatus.lean — theorem

proved Unitriangular moves are bijections of the digit box

The classifier's coordinate moves (its division-and-carry accounting) have exactly this shape, so they permute the box and preserve all counting; inversion proceeds coordinate by coordinate in the solving order.

Call a self-map $\Theta$ of $\mathbb{F}_p^m$ unitriangular for the solving order if $(\Theta x)_i = x_i + \varphi_i(x_j : j \prec i)$ for every coordinate $i$, where each correction $\varphi_i$ reads only strictly earlier coordinates. Every unitriangular map is a bijection of the box.

The Lean statement 
-- section variables: {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
theorem C0_unitriBij {m : ℕ} {Θ : (Fin m → ZMod p) → (Fin m → ZMod p)} (h : IsUnitriangular Θ) : Function.Bijective Θ

lean/LeanUrat/MovesC/C0_unitriBij.lean — theorem

proved Pulling back a system along a move preserves its pins

Why coordinate changes between frames never distort mass or codimension: the transported constraint system is constructed explicitly, with pins unchanged.

For a digit system $D$ and a unitriangular move $\Theta$, the pullback $\{x : \Theta x \text{ solves } D\}$ is again the solution set of a digit system $D'$, and $D'$ has exactly the same pinned coordinates as $D$.

The Lean statement 
-- section variables: {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
theorem C0_pinTransport {m : ℕ} (D : Locus p m) {Θ : (Fin m → ZMod p) → (Fin m → ZMod p)} (h : IsUnitriangular Θ) : ∃ D' : Locus p m, (∀ x, D'.IsSolution x ↔ D.IsSolution (Θ x)) ∧ ∀ j : Fin m, D'.pinned j = D.pinned j

lean/LeanUrat/MovesC/C0_pinTransport.lean — theorem

conditional Value digits cut exactly the reciprocal of their alphabet

The counting content of the fresh value clauses: a value digit cuts exactly the reciprocal of its alphabet — the reason level-set constraints contribute exact codimension.

Let $S$ be a set of coordinates and $\varphi \colon \mathbb{F}_p^m \to \alpha$ an additive homomorphism onto a finite abelian group $\alpha$ that reads only the coordinates in $S$. Then all fibers are equal kernel cosets: for every value $v \in \alpha$, $\#\{y \text{ supported on } S : \varphi(y) = v\} \cdot |\alpha| = p^{|S|}$.

Hypotheses this rests on: TypObject T — hypothesizes an additive surjection reading only the level set S; machine-checked for every such T, but the instance tying it to a real fresh digit (the graded-piece map of the classifier) awaits the unbuilt graded-ring bridge.

The Lean statement 
-- section variables: {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
theorem C1_TYP_fiberCount {m : ℕ} {S : Finset (Fin m)} {α : Type*} [AddCommGroup α] [Fintype α] (T : TypObject p m S α) (v : α) : Nat.card {y : Fin m → ZMod p // T.φ y = v ∧ ∀ c ∉ S, y c = 0} * Nat.card α = p ^ S.card

lean/LeanUrat/MovesC/C1_TYP_fiberCount.lean — theorem

proved Disjoint fresh clauses cut the box by exactly their total codimension

The multi-clause counting engine: disjoint supports make the conditional factors multiply exactly, division-free; consumed by the per-step conditional-exactness theorem.

Let one read's fresh data be a finite list of clauses with pairwise disjoint supports, each cutting exactly $p^{-\mathrm{codim}}$ among its supported assignments. Then the joint cut on the whole box is exactly the product: $\#\{x \in \mathbb{F}_p^m : x \text{ satisfies every clause}\}\cdot p^{m^*} = p^m$, where $m^* = \sum \mathrm{codim}$.

The Lean statement 
-- section variables: {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
theorem C1_clausesCount {m : ℕ} (fd : FreshData p m) : Nat.card {x : Fin m → ZMod p // fd.sat x} * p ^ fd.mstar = p ^ m

lean/LeanUrat/MovesC/C1_clausesCount.lean — theorem

conditional Fresh clauses only constrain coordinates still free on the state cylinder

The bridge from geometry to counting: fresh content sits strictly above the cumulative Newton floor, and the exact-downset invariant converts "above the floor" into "unpinned" — so no read double-charges a coordinate already pinned by earlier reads.

In any jet presentation of a read history, every coordinate in the support of a fresh clause of read $i$ is free (unpinned) on the state cylinder $\Sigma_i$ entering that read.

Hypotheses this rests on: JetSetup J — the hypothesized jet presentation of the history (sorted chart, state cylinders, fresh data, unitriangular moves, presentation spine, exact-downset invariant, fresh-band laws); machine-checked for every such bundle, but constructing one for a real classifier history is the declared open boundary (the unbuilt graded-ring bridge).

The Lean statement 
-- section variables: {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] {F : Type*} [Field F] [Finite F]
theorem C2_freshFree {n N m : ℕ} {H : History p F} (J : JetSetup H n N m) (i : ℕ) (hi : i < H.nodes.length) : ∀ cl ∈ (J.fresh i).clauses, ∀ c ∈ cl.support, (J.Sigma i).pinned c = false

lean/LeanUrat/MovesC/C2_freshFree.lean — theorem

proved Along a realizable history, the newest line dominates all earlier ones on its interior

The Newton-line domination behind the floor bookkeeping; restated at audit round 4 to hypothesize the full realizability class after the empty-stratum countermodel showed coherence alone is insufficient — the span-entry inequality is sourced from admissibility, not assumed.

Let $H$ be a coherent, realizable read history. For $j \le i$ and every base index $b$ in read $i$'s factor interior ($b < \mu_i \cdot w_i$, where $w_i$ is the child block width), the earlier line lies below the current one: $\mathrm{line}_j(b) \le \mathrm{line}_i(b)$. Consequently, on each factor interior the cumulative floor of all recorded lines equals the current line.

The Lean statement 
-- section variables: {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] {F : Type*} [Field F] [Finite F]
theorem C3_lineDom (H : History p F) (hcoh : HistoryCoherent H) (hreal : Realizable H) (i : ℕ) (hi : i < H.nodes.length) (j : ℕ) (hij : j ≤ i) (b : ℕ) (hb : b < (H.nodes[i]'hi).μ * (H.nodes[i]'hi).childWidth) : (H.nodes[j]'(by omega)).line.at b ≤ (H.nodes[i]'hi).line.at b

lean/LeanUrat/MovesC/C3_lineDom.lean — theorem

proved The transported vertex value's polynomial factor is nonzero

A deliberately partial result: the file explicitly does NOT claim the transported vertex value itself is nonzero — evaluating at the residue root would need the field isomorphism $K(\bar z) \cong K[z]/(\psi)$, which is outside this corpus's scope.

Let $\psi$ be a monic polynomial of positive degree over a field $K$, and let $R$ have $\psi$-order exactly $\mu$ (that is, $\psi^{\mu} \mid R$ but $\psi^{\mu+1} \nmid R$). Then $(R/\psi^{\mu}) \bmod \psi \neq 0$.

The Lean statement 
theorem C3_vtxPolyNe {K : Type*} [Field K] (ψ Ranch : Polynomial K) (μ : ℕ) (hmonic : ψ.Monic) (hdeg : 0 < ψ.natDegree) (hord : OrdPsiPoly ψ Ranch μ) : (Ranch /ₘ ψ ^ μ) %ₘ ψ ≠ 0

lean/LeanUrat/MovesC/C3_vtxPolyNe.lean — theorem

proved One read cuts the state cylinder by exactly its nominal volume

THE per-step conditional exactness of the counting argument, in fully address-free form: proved by solving the cylinder onto its free coordinates and applying the disjoint-clause product law inside them.

Let $\Sigma$ be a state cylinder (a digit system) and let fresh data with pairwise disjoint supports constrain only coordinates free on $\Sigma$. Then the fresh clauses cut $\Sigma$ exactly: $\#\{x : x \text{ solves } \Sigma \text{ and satisfies all clauses}\} \cdot p^{m^*} = \mathrm{mass}(\Sigma)$ — the conditional volume is exactly $p^{-m^*}$, an equality rather than a bound.

The Lean statement 
-- section variables: {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
theorem C4_conditionalMass {m : ℕ} (Sigma : Locus p m) (fd : FreshData p m) (hfree : ∀ cl ∈ fd.clauses, ∀ c ∈ cl.support, Sigma.pinned c = false) : Nat.card {x : Fin m → ZMod p // Sigma.IsSolution x ∧ fd.sat x} * p ^ fd.mstar = Sigma.mass

lean/LeanUrat/MovesC/C4_conditionalMass.lean — theorem

conditional The mass recursion: each read divides the surviving mass by its fresh count

The recursion assembled on the interface: move bijectivity transports the count, the stratum recursion plus the inherited-content law reduce read i to its fresh clauses, and the per-step conditional exactness fires.

In any jet presentation $J$ of a history $H$: $\mathrm{mass}(\Sigma_{i+1}) \cdot p^{m^*(\nu_i)} = \mathrm{mass}(\Sigma_i)$, where $m^*(\nu_i)$ is read $\nu_i$'s total fresh codimension — consuming one read divides the mass by exactly $p^{m^*}$.

Hypotheses this rests on: JetSetup J — the hypothesized jet presentation of the history; machine-checked for every such bundle, but constructing one for a real classifier history is the declared open boundary (the unbuilt graded-ring bridge).

The Lean statement

conditional After k reads the surviving mass is the box divided by the accumulated codimension

lean/LeanUrat/MovesC/C6_transportedAdmissible.lean — theorem

conditional Theorem C(a): the classifier locus is the bijective transport of the final joint locus

Capstone (a): the audit forced the classifier locus to be defined independently (an existential chain over the recorded moves, no images, no cylinders), so the proved equality with the transported final locus is contentful rather than definitional.

Fix a jet presentation $J$ of a read history $H$ with $k$ reads and an admissible terminal system $Z$ (pinning only coordinates free on the final cylinder $\Sigma_k$). The classifier locus $S(H,Z)$ is defined with no reference to the cylinders: a frame-0 digit vector $x$ lies in $S(H,Z)$ iff it carries a chain $y_0 = x$, $y_i = \Theta_i(y_{i+1})$ through the recorded moves such that each $y_i$ satisfies read $\nu_i$'s fresh clauses and $y_k$ solves $Z$. Then (i) the composite move $\Psi_H = \Theta_0 \circ \cdots \circ \Theta_{k-1}$ is a bijection of the box; (ii) $S(H,Z)$ equals the $\Psi_H$-image of $\{x : x \text{ solves } \Sigma_k \text{ and } Z\}$; (iii) the two sets have equal cardinality.

Hypotheses this rests on: JetSetup J — the hypothesized jet presentation of the history; machine-checked for every such bundle, but constructing one for a real classifier history is the declared open boundary (the unbuilt graded-ring bridge). AdmissibleZ — the terminal system pins only coordinates free on the final cylinder (a genuine mathematical hypothesis of Theorem C, not a deferral).

The Lean statement

lean/LeanUrat/MovesC/C6_thmC_b.lean — theorem

Base relations (MovesRBase) 9 definition · 7 proved · 1 conditional
Unramified Witt coefficient rings as literal subrings; composition is index arithmetic

MovesRBase is the Witt-vector base layer for the tower's coefficient rings. It fixes one ambient ring W(F̄_p) — the p-typical Witt vectors over a fixed algebraic closure of F_p — and realizes every unramified coefficient ring literally inside it: F_m is THE subfield of F̄_p with p^m elements (the fixed field of the m-th Frobenius iterate, with the cardinality proved, not assumed) and O_m := W(F_m) is an actual subring of W(F̄_p), so towers of base rings nest as genuine set inclusions with no per-pair embedding choices. Unramified base change is then index arithmetic by convention — the degree-δ′ extension of O_m IS O_{mδ′}, so composing base changes is literally multiplying indices — and the corpus proves the nesting, composition, chain, and residue-size laws (q_{mδ′} = q_m^{δ′}) that the tower recursion's bookkeeping rides on, all machine-checked with no axioms beyond Lean's core. Two statement-layer carriers (species letters, alphabet data) are transcribed for typing only, and the one substantive law about them — the re-basing law EQ-2 — is stated as an explicitly open hypothesis, with its lone consequence proved conditionally from it.

definition The ambient ring: Witt vectors over a fixed algebraic closure

The design decision the whole corpus rests on: one ambient ring, so nested base changes compose as actual subrings rather than up to isomorphism; every declaration below is parameterized by an arbitrary prime p via this variable line.

Fix once and for all an algebraic closure $\bar{\mathbb{F}}_p$ of the field with $p$ elements, and form the ring of $p$-typical Witt vectors $W(\bar{\mathbb{F}}_p)$ (the completed ring of integers of the maximal unramified extension of $\mathbb{Q}_p$). Every coefficient ring in this corpus is a literal subring of this one ambient ring.

The Lean statement 
variable (p : ℕ) [hp : Fact p.Prime]

abbrev Kbar := AlgebraicClosure (ZMod p)

abbrev Wbar := WittVector p (Kbar p)

lean/LeanUrat/MovesRBase/Defs.lean — definition

definition The finite subfield of each order, fixed once

The residue fields of all unramified extensions, realized as concrete subfields of one closure so their lattice is literal set inclusion.

For each $m \ge 1$, $F_m$ is the subfield of $\bar{\mathbb{F}}_p$ fixed by the $m$-th iterate of the Frobenius $x \mapsto x^p$; equivalently $F_m = \{x \in \bar{\mathbb{F}}_p : x^{p^m} = x\}$. It is the unique subfield with $p^m$ elements — the cardinality is a separate theorem of this corpus, not part of the definition.

The Lean statement 
noncomputable def Fsub (m : ℕ+) : Subfield (Kbar p) :=
  RingHom.eqLocusField (iterateFrobenius (Kbar p) p (m : ℕ)) (RingHom.id _)

lean/LeanUrat/MovesRBase/Defs.lean — definition

definition Unramified coefficient rings as literal subrings

The central object: the base rings over which the tower induction's polynomials live, with no per-pair embedding choices anywhere in the corpus.

$\mathcal{O}_m := W(F_m)$, realized inside $W(\bar{\mathbb{F}}_p)$ as the image of the Witt-vector functor applied to the inclusion $F_m \hookrightarrow \bar{\mathbb{F}}_p$. Mathematically this is the ring of integers of the degree-$m$ unramified extension of $\mathbb{Q}_p$, but realized as an actual subring, so towers of base rings nest literally.

The Lean statement 
noncomputable def O (m : ℕ+) : Subring (Wbar p) :=
  (WittVector.map (Subfield.subtype (Fsub p m))).range

lean/LeanUrat/MovesRBase/Defs.lean — definition

definition Unramified base change by absolute index

The convention that turns the recursion's base-change bookkeeping into arithmetic of indices; its composition laws are the one-line theorems below.

The degree-$\delta'$ unramified base change of $\mathcal{O}_m$ is defined to be $\mathcal{O}_{m\delta'}$. Indices are absolute, so the composition rule $(\mathcal{O}_\delta)_{\delta'} = \mathcal{O}_{\delta\delta'}$ holds by definition — 'never an identification up to isomorphism'.

The Lean statement 
noncomputable def relExt (m δ' : ℕ+) : Subring (Wbar p) := O p (m * δ')

lean/LeanUrat/MovesRBase/Defs.lean — definition

definition Digit cylinders: congruence classes mod p^N

Defines the counting object (the level-N box) that the density and volume layers of the parked parent corpus are built over.

For a tuple $g = (g_1, \ldots, g_n)$ of elements of $\mathcal{O}_m$ (the coefficients of a monic degree-$n$ polynomial), the level-$N$ cylinder at $g$ is the set of tuples agreeing with $g$ in their first $N$ Witt coordinates. Since $F_m$ is perfect this is exactly the congruence class of $g$ modulo $p^N$ — the basic event whose normalized count the splitting-density computations measure.

The Lean statement 
def cyl (m : ℕ+) {n : ℕ} (N : ℕ) (g : Fin n → ↥(O p m)) : Set (Fin n → ↥(O p m)) :=
  {h | ∀ (j : Fin n) (i : ℕ), i < N → ((h j : Wbar p)).coeff i = ((g j : Wbar p)).coeff i}

lean/LeanUrat/MovesRBase/Defs.lean — definition

proved Functoriality of the Witt-vector construction

A three-line fact absent from Mathlib at formalization time; the subring-nesting theorem consumes it to rewrite inclusions through the Witt functor.

Applying the Witt-vector functor to ring homomorphisms $f: R \to S$ and $g: S \to T$ in succession is the same as applying it once to $g \circ f$: $W(g)(W(f)(x)) = W(g \circ f)(x)$ coordinatewise.

The Lean statement 
theorem map_comp {R S T : Type*} [CommRing R] [CommRing S] [CommRing T]
    (g : S →+* T) (f : R →+* S) (x : WittVector p R) :
    WittVector.map g (WittVector.map f x) = WittVector.map (g.comp f) x

lean/LeanUrat/MovesRBase/MapComp.lean — lemma

proved Subfields nest along divisibility

The residue-field half of 'nested base changes compose literally as subrings'; feeds directly into the ring-level nesting theorem.

If $d \mid m$ then $F_d \subseteq F_m$ inside $\bar{\mathbb{F}}_p$: any $x$ with $x^{p^d} = x$ also satisfies $x^{p^m} = x$ (iterate the relation $m/d$ times).

The Lean statement 
theorem Fsub_le {d m : ℕ+} (h : d ∣ m) : Fsub p d ≤ Fsub p m

lean/LeanUrat/MovesRBase/U2_Fsub_le.lean — theorem

proved Unramified rings nest along divisibility

The workhorse inclusion every base-change step of the tower recursion rides on; proved via subfield nesting plus Witt functoriality.

If $d \mid m$ then $W(F_d) \subseteq W(F_m)$ inside $W(\bar{\mathbb{F}}_p)$. This is the substantive content behind the composition convention: once the subfields nest, the Witt rings nest as actual subrings.

The Lean statement 
theorem O_le {d m : ℕ+} (h : d ∣ m) : O p d ≤ O p m

lean/LeanUrat/MovesRBase/U3_O_le.lean — theorem

proved Base changes compose by multiplying degrees

Together with the companion facts in the same file (the base-change convention is a definitional equality, and each ring sits inside its own base change), this makes '(O_δ)_δ′ = O_δδ′' pure bookkeeping.

A degree-$\delta''$ base change applied to the degree-$\delta'$ base change of $\mathcal{O}_m$ equals the degree-$\delta'\delta''$ base change of $\mathcal{O}_m$: under the absolute-index convention, composition of unramified base changes is literally associativity of index multiplication.

The Lean statement 
theorem relExt_relExt (m δ' δ'' : ℕ+) :
    relExt p (m * δ') δ'' = relExt p m (δ' * δ'')

lean/LeanUrat/MovesRBase/U4_relExt_comp.lean — theorem

proved Residue-field size under base change

The uniformity theorem substitutes q = p^δ into one fixed rational function; this is the bookkeeping for how that argument changes along the tower. The unit's second half (the same identity read through the tower's parameter bundle) stays with the parked parent corpus, per the recorded deviation.

With $q_m := p^m$ (the size of the residue field $F_m$), one has $q_{m\delta'} = q_m^{\delta'}$ — the identity governing how the density formulas' variable $q$ transforms when the base ring is extended.

The Lean statement 
theorem qq_mul (m δ' : ℕ+) : qq p (m * δ') = qq p m ^ (δ' : ℕ)

lean/LeanUrat/MovesRBase/U5_qq_mul.lean — theorem

proved The fixed field has exactly p^m elements

The corpus's hardest unit, and the one that makes F_m 'THE subfield of order p^m' rather than merely 'a fixed field' — added after an audit found the cardinality claim was part of the display, not a free convention.

$|F_m| = p^m$: the fixed field of the $m$-th Frobenius iterate is the finite field with exactly $p^m$ elements. Proof: $F_m$ is the root set of $X^{p^m} - X$, a separable polynomial that splits with $p^m$ distinct roots in the algebraically closed field.

The Lean statement 
theorem Fsub_card (m : ℕ+) : Nat.card ↥(Fsub p m) = p ^ (m : ℕ)

lean/LeanUrat/MovesRBase/U6_Fsub_card.lean — theorem

proved The subring chain a tower branch descends

The ring-theoretic witness for the recursion clause of the tower induction; restated here with explicit indices in place of the parked corpus's parameter bundle (a definitional translation recorded in the MANIFEST — ring content unchanged).

For any $m, \delta, \delta''$, the literal subring chain $W(F_m) \subseteq W(F_{m\delta}) \subseteq W(F_{m\delta\delta''})$ holds inside $W(\bar{\mathbb{F}}_p)$: a branch of relative degree $\delta''$ created at accumulated level $m\delta$ lands in exactly the ring the recursion says it should.

The Lean statement 
theorem O_chain (m δrel δ'' : ℕ+) :
    O p m ≤ O p (m * δrel) ∧ O p (m * δrel) ≤ O p (m * δrel * δ'')

definition Species letters as finite syntax

Statement-layer carrier only — transcribed so the re-basing law below can be typed; it is never constructed in this corpus (instantiation belongs to the tower-induction hard core).

A 'species letter' — the label classifying local behavior at a tower position — is purely syntactic data: a stage tuple $(e, h, g, \mu)$ of numerical invariants together with side/lattice and polygon-convention data, with the requirement that a letter is determined by these data (defined by syntax, never by the residue field's size). Each level $\delta$ carries a finite menu of realized letters.

The Lean statement 
structure SpeciesSyntax where

  shape : Letter → ℕ × ℕ × ℕ × ℕ
  SideData : Type
  side : Letter → SideData
  PolyConv : Type

lean/LeanUrat/MovesRBase/Defs.lean — structure

definition Alphabet carriers with their cardinality law

The interface packaging the note's alphabet objects together with their laws, per the recorded design lesson that a faithful obligation statement must be false for instances the source would reject; not constructed in this corpus.

The data of an 'alphabet' at each shape-position class $x$ and each level $\delta$: an $F_{p^\delta}$-module carrier, a family of slot maps into it, the alphabet $\mathbb{A}_\delta(x)$ defined as the additive span of the slot maps' ranges, a dimension $a_\delta(x)$ with $|\mathbb{A}_\delta(x)| = p^{a_\delta(x)}$, and the realized species letter at each level. The cardinality identity is a field of the structure — part of what it means to be alphabet data, not a theorem proved here.

The Lean statement 
structure AlphabetData (Sp : SpeciesSyntax) where
  Pos : Type
  posNe : Nonempty Pos
  Carrier : ℕ+ → Pos → Type
  [carrierGrp : ∀ δ x, AddCommGroup (Carrier δ x)]
  [carrierMod : ∀ δ x, Module ↥(Fsub p δ) (Carrier δ x)]
  SlotDom : ℕ+ → Pos → Type
  [slotDomGrp : ∀ δ x, AddCommGroup (SlotDom δ x)]
  [slotDomMod : ∀ δ x, Module ↥(Fsub p δ) (SlotDom δ x)]
  slotMap : ∀ δ x, SlotDom δ x → Carrier δ x
  alpha : ∀ δ x, Set (Carrier δ x)
  alpha_attain : ∀ δ x,
    alpha δ x = ↑(AddSubgroup.closure (Set.range (slotMap δ x)))
  aDim : ℕ+ → Pos → ℕ
  card_eq : ∀ δ x, Nat.card (alpha δ x) = p ^ aDim δ x
  posLetter : ℕ+ → Pos → Sp.Letter

lean/LeanUrat/MovesRBase/Defs.lean — structure

definition Stable positions: the letter survives re-basing

The domain restriction that keeps the re-basing law faithful: it is asserted only where comparison across levels is meaningful.

A position class $x$ is $\delta$-stable when its realized species letter at level $\delta$ equals its letter at level $1$ — the classifying label persists under re-basing. Positions that split under base change fail this and are excluded from the comparison domain of the re-basing law.

The Lean statement 
def AlphabetData.Stable {Sp} (AD : AlphabetData p Sp) (δ : ℕ+) (x : AD.Pos) : Prop :=
  AD.posLetter δ x = AD.posLetter 1 x

lean/LeanUrat/MovesRBase/Defs.lean — definition

definition The open re-basing law for alphabets

The source file itself marks this law OPEN (conditional on its ambient re-basing theory). Naming the open law as an explicit proposition, rather than assuming it silently, is the project's standing discipline for unproved steps.

The hypothesized law (EQ-2): at every $\delta$-stable position, the re-based slot maps are $F_{p^\delta}$-linear and the alphabet dimension scales linearly, $a_\delta = \delta \cdot a_1$. Stated as a named proposition only — this is an open conjecture of the parked parent corpus, conditional on its ambient re-basing theory, and is proved nowhere in the repository; consumers must take it as an explicit hypothesis.

The Lean statement 
def EQ2law {Sp} (AD : AlphabetData p Sp) : Prop :=
  ∀ (δ : ℕ+) (x : AD.Pos), AlphabetData.Stable p AD δ x →
    IsLinearMap ↥(Fsub p δ) (AD.slotMap δ x) ∧
    AD.aDim δ x = (δ : ℕ) * AD.aDim 1 x

lean/LeanUrat/MovesRBase/Defs.lean — definition

conditional Alphabet size under re-basing, assuming the open law

The implication itself is machine-checked with no axioms beyond Lean's core; what remains open is its antecedent, which is exactly the parked parent corpus's central unproved law.

Assuming the open law (EQ-2), at every $\delta$-stable position the alphabet's size obeys the displayed consequence $|\mathbb{A}_\delta| = q_\delta^{\,a} = |\mathbb{A}_1|^{\delta}$, where $q_\delta = p^\delta$ and $a = a_1(x)$ is the level-one dimension — the cardinality identity the density bookkeeping uses.

Hypotheses this rests on: h : EQ2law — the open (EQ-2) re-basing law (linearity of the re-based slot maps plus linear dimension scaling), hypothesized and discharged nowhere in the repository; additionally the carrier structure's own cardinality field (|𝔸_δ| = p^{a_δ}) enters as data of the structure, not as a proved fact.

The Lean statement 
theorem EQ2law_card {Sp} (AD : AlphabetData p Sp) (h : EQ2law p AD) :
    ∀ (δ : ℕ+) (x : AD.Pos), AlphabetData.Stable p AD δ x →
      Nat.card (AD.alpha δ x) = qq p δ ^ AD.aDim 1 x ∧
      Nat.card (AD.alpha δ x) = Nat.card (AD.alpha 1 x) ^ (δ : ℕ)

lean/LeanUrat/MovesRBase/U12_EQ2law_card.lean — theorem

The graded-ring bridge (MovesGr) 3 definition · 3 open · 10 proved · 4 conditional
The genuine graded ring behind stage residuals; the facade identity proved conditionally

The graded-ring bridge. The tower formalization (the Moves corpus) works with a formal interface for each stage of the Okutsu-Montes tower in which the graded residual is defined backwards from the stage's residual map; an adversarial audit objected that this makes the residual layer a 'definitional facade'. This corpus constructs the genuine objects — the associated graded ring of a stage's own valuation, its homogeneous localization, the degree-1 normalizing unit and the structure isomorphism — and proves the formal residual laws (multiplicativity, rank-1 normalization, and the facade identity itself) as theorems of that genuine structure. Where the planned statements turned out vacuous or unprovable as stated, it ships compiled refutation certificates instead of proofs, and one interface repair (the componentwise-addition field) was forced by a verifier countermodel and adopted, with the project lead's approval, before the domain criterion could be proved.

Builds on: Moves

definition Side valuations and the associated graded ring of the p-adic polynomial ring

The ground object of the whole bridge: the graded ring is built from the stage's own valuation, not from its residual map — the exact point of the facade audit.

A *side valuation* on $A=\mathbb{Z}_p[X]$ is a map $w\colon A\to\mathbb{Z}\cup\{\infty\}$ with $w(0)=\infty$ and $w(f)<\infty$ for $f\neq 0$, $w(-f)=w(f)$, the ultrametric law $\min(w(f),w(g))\le w(f+g)$, submultiplicativity $w(f)+w(g)\le w(fg)$, $w(cf)\ge w(f)$ for scalars $c\in\mathbb{Z}_p$, and $w(1)=0$. From it the file builds the filtration $A_{\ge\gamma}=\{f: w(f)\ge\gamma\}\supseteq A_{>\gamma}$, the graded pieces $\mathrm{gr}_\gamma=A_{\ge\gamma}/A_{>\gamma}$, the associated graded group $\mathrm{gr}_w(A)=\bigoplus_{\gamma\in\mathbb{Z}}\mathrm{gr}_\gamma$, the piece products $\mathrm{gr}_\gamma\times\mathrm{gr}_\delta\to\mathrm{gr}_{\gamma+\delta}$ induced from multiplication in $A$ (well-defined by submultiplicativity), and the initial-form map $f\mapsto[f]\in\mathrm{gr}_{w(f)}$. The valuation of any stage of the Okutsu-Montes tower is such a bundle (a stage's $w$ in fact satisfies the product law with equality, $w(fg)=w(f)+w(g)$; the bundle demands only the inequality).

The Lean statement 
structure SideVal (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] where
  w : Polynomial ℤ_[p] → WithTop ℤ
  w_zero : w 0 = ⊤
  w_ne_top : ∀ f, f ≠ 0 → w f ≠ ⊤
  w_neg : ∀ f, w (-f) = w f
  w_add : ∀ f g, min (w f) (w g) ≤ w (f + g)
  w_smul : ∀ (c : ℤ_[p]) f, w f ≤ w (Polynomial.C c * f)
  w_mul : ∀ f g, w f + w g ≤ w (f * g)
  w_one : w 1 = 0

lean/LeanUrat/MovesGr/Defs.lean — structure

definition What it means to put the genuine graded ring structure on the associated graded

The componentwise-addition field was added (adopted 2026-07-28 with the project lead's approval) only after a verifier countermodel showed the original four-field version admitted pathological models — see the domain-criterion refutation record below.

A graded ring structure on $\mathrm{gr}_w(A)$ is a commutative ring structure whose addition is the componentwise direct-sum addition, whose product restricts on homogeneous elements to the piece product induced from $A$, whose unit is the initial form $[1]$, for which the initial form is multiplicative — $[fg]=[f]\,[g]$ for $f,g\neq 0$ (equivalently: the submultiplicative inequality holds with equality, i.e. $w$ is a genuine valuation, as every stage valuation is) — and which obeys the tie law: if $w(f)<w(g)$ then $[f+g]=[f]$ — the summand of strictly lower weight determines the initial form of the sum (here the graded "degree" of a class is its weight $w$, not the polynomial degree).

The Lean statement 
structure GradedRingStr {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] (S : SideVal p) where
  ring : CommRing S.Gr
  add_def : ∀ (x y : S.Gr) (γ : ℤ), (letI := ring; x + y) γ = x γ + y γ
  mul_of : letI := ring; ∀ (γ δ : ℤ) (x : S.grPiece γ) (y : S.grPiece δ),
      DirectSum.of _ γ x * DirectSum.of _ δ y = DirectSum.of _ (γ + δ) (S.pmul γ δ x y)
  one_def : letI := ring; (1 : S.Gr) = S.initialForm 1
  if_mul : letI := ring; ∀ f g : Polynomial ℤ_[p], f ≠ 0 → g ≠ 0 →
      S.initialForm (f * g) = S.initialForm f * S.initialForm g
  if_add_lt : ∀ f g : Polynomial ℤ_[p], f ≠ 0 → g ≠ 0 → f + g ≠ 0 →
      S.deg f < S.deg g → S.initialForm (f + g) = S.initialForm f

lean/LeanUrat/MovesGr/Defs.lean — structure

definition The genuine graded model of a tower stage

Conditionality: the identification $L_0\cong K[z^{\pm1}]$ — the last standing fiat — is not even a dischargeable hypothesis: it is hard-coded into the codomain type of the isomorphism $\Theta$ (a silent codomain choice, per the corpus's own audit record), and NO inhabitant of this bundle is constructed in the corpus — the planned base-stage inhabitation was refuted as stated (see the vacuity certificates).

For a stage $\sigma$ of the Okutsu-Montes tower (with valuation $w_\sigma$, key polynomial $\Phi$, residue field $K$, and residual map $R$ into Laurent polynomials over $K$), a genuine model consists of: a side valuation agreeing with $w_\sigma$ on nonzero polynomials; a genuine graded ring structure on $\mathrm{gr}_{w_\sigma}(A)$; the localization $L$ of the graded ring at the multiplicative set generated by the initial forms of $\sigma$'s representatives and its key; a distinguished unit $T\in L^\times$; a ring isomorphism $\Theta\colon L\cong L_0[T^{\pm1}]$ with $L_0=K[z^{\pm1}]$ sending $T$ to the outer Laurent variable; and the discharge identity $\Theta(\iota[f]) = C(R(f))\cdot T^{\,w_\sigma(f)}$ for all $f\neq 0$ — the stage's residual data is computed by the genuine initial form, never read backwards.

The Lean statement 
structure GenuineStageModel (σ : Stage p F) where
  S : SideVal p
  hSw : ∀ f, f ≠ 0 → S.w f = (σ.w f : WithTop ℤ)
  Rg : GradedRingStr S
  homMon : letI := Rg.ring; Submonoid S.Gr
  homMon_gen : letI := Rg.ring;
      homMon = Submonoid.closure
        ((fun ũ => S.initialForm ũ) '' {ũ | ũ ∈ σ.reps} ∪ {S.initialForm σ.Φ})
  Lo : Type
  Loring : CommRing Lo
  alg : letI := Rg.ring; Algebra S.Gr Lo
  isLoc : letI := Rg.ring; letI := alg; IsLocalization homMon Lo
  Tunit : letI := Loring; Loˣ
  Θ : letI := Loring; Lo ≃+* LExt ↥σ.K
  hT : letI := Loring; Θ (Tunit : Lo) = (LeanUrat.Moves.locT ↥σ.K : LeanUrat.Moves.LTwo ↥σ.K)
  discharge : letI := Rg.ring; letI := alg; ∀ f : Polynomial ℤ_[p], f ≠ 0 →
      Θ (algebraMap S.Gr Lo (S.initialForm f)) = σ.grRes f
