lean/LeanUrat/MovesGr/HeldUnits_certs.lean — theorem

Hard core 1: the tower and carry algebra (HC1) 5 definition · 2 open · 11 proved · 2 conditional
Tower-of-stages and carry-arithmetic kernel for wild-prime digit counting

This corpus is the kernel of the tower induction behind the wildly ramified case of the uniformity theorem. It builds, in Lean, the Montes-style tower of valuation stages obtained by successive Newton-polygon reads of a p-adic polynomial — base stage, increment step (with a machine-checked refutation showing the step's growth guard is necessary), and recentering step — together with the carry algebra F[u]/(u^e − z̄) that makes higher-digit residue arithmetic exact: multiplying digits produces explicit carries. On top of the tower it proves the height/slot-minimum/staircase combinatorics and the exact measure count of a landing stratum — each level fixes exactly the predicted number of base-p digits — both conditional on the tower's recorded Newton-polygon side data (an interface hypothesis never discharged inside this corpus) and, for the measure count, on supplied coding inputs; this is the mechanism that makes densities at wild primes computable. Its exports are the abstract carrier contract consumed by the density-assembly corpora — whose concrete instantiation is still open, blocked by two machine-certified statement-transcription bugs in the abstract statement shapes — and the vertex-pin kernel consumed by the run-level classifier side, substantive only on its recentering-shaped legs (the steep legs are vacuous under the current recording interface, as disclosed on the card).

Builds on: Moves, MovesC, MovesGr, HC2

definition The carry algebra: digit arithmetic with explicit carries

The portable digit-arithmetic object of the whole corpus: every deep-level residue computation is bookkept in this twisted group algebra, with the wild carry phenomenon isolated into one 2-cocycle.

For a finite field $F$, an integer $e \ge 1$ and a unit $\bar z \in F^\times$, the carry algebra is the space of functions $\mathcal{D} = \{x : \mathbb{Z}/e \to F\}$ with pointwise addition and the twisted convolution $(x \star y)(\tau) = \sum_{\rho} x(\rho)\, y(\tau-\rho)\, \bar z^{\,c(\rho,\tau-\rho)}$, where the carry cocycle $c(\rho,\sigma)$ is $1$ exactly when the canonical representatives overflow ($\rho + \sigma \ge e$). The homogeneous generators $u_\rho c$ (the function supported at $\rho$ with value $c$) multiply by $u_\rho c \cdot u_\sigma c' = u_{\rho+\sigma}(c\,c'\,\bar z^{\,c(\rho,\sigma)})$: multiplying digits produces an explicit carry weighted by $\bar z$. A carry-ring pack is a commutative ring structure on $\mathcal{D}$ whose product is exactly this convolution and whose unit is $u_0 1$ — a concrete model of $F[u]/(u^e - \bar z)$.

The Lean statement 
def CarryAlg (F : Type u) (e : ℕ) : Type u := ZMod e → F

def carryExp (ρ σ : ZMod e) : ℕ := if e ≤ ρ.val + σ.val then 1 else 0

noncomputable def cmul [NeZero e] (zbar : Fˣ) (x y : CarryAlg F e) : CarryAlg F e :=
  fun τ => ∑ ρ : ZMod e, x ρ * y (τ - ρ) * (zbar : F) ^ carryExp ρ (τ - ρ)

noncomputable def dOf (ρ : ZMod e) (c : F) : CarryAlg F e := Pi.single ρ c

structure CarryRingPack (F : Type u) [Field F] (e : ℕ) [NeZero e] (zbar : Fˣ) where
  ring : CommRing (CarryAlg F e)
  mul_def : ∀ x y : CarryAlg F e, (letI := ring; x * y) = cmul zbar x y
  add_def : ∀ (x y : CarryAlg F e) (τ : ZMod e), (letI := ring; x + y) τ = x τ + y τ
  one_def : (letI := ring; (1 : CarryAlg F e)) = dOf 0 1

lean/LeanUrat/HC1/DefsD.lean — definition

definition Reading a residual into the carry algebra

The bridge between the valuation tower and the carry algebra; the multiplicativity theorem below is stated about exactly this map.

Each valuation stage $\sigma$ carries a residual map $R$ sending a nonzero $p$-adic polynomial $B$ in general position to a Laurent monomial $c\,z^{k}$ over the stage's residue field. The digit map reads this into the carry algebra born at index $e_b$: if $R(B) = c\,z^k$ is a monomial unit, $\mathrm{dig}(B)$ is the homogeneous element of grade $k \bmod e_b$ with the aligned scalar $c \cdot \bar z^{(k - (k \bmod e_b))/e_b}$; otherwise $0$. It converts valuation-theoretic residues into positional digits subject to carries.

The Lean statement 
noncomputable def _root_.LeanUrat.Moves.Stage.digD {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
    {F : Type u} [Field F] [Finite F] (σ : Stage p F) (zbar : Fˣ) (e_b : ℕ) [NeZero e_b]
    (B : Polynomial ℤ_[p]) : CarryAlg F e_b :=
  open scoped Classical in
  if h : IsMonomialUnit (σ.R B) then
    dOf ((h.choose_spec.choose : ℤ) : ZMod e_b)
      (((h.choose : ↥σ.K) : F) *
        ((zbar ^ ((h.choose_spec.choose -
            (((h.choose_spec.choose : ℤ) : ZMod e_b)).val) / (e_b : ℤ)) : Fˣ) : F))
  else 0

lean/LeanUrat/HC1/DefsD.lean — definition

definition A tower of valuation stages with witnessed moves

The central inductive object: the formal counterpart of an Okutsu–Montes chain of augmented valuations, with every step carrying its own machine-checked witness data (each move records the child's stage laws; the existence theorems below show such enriched witnesses exist for every legal read).

A tower is a finite chain of valuation stages $\sigma_0, \dots, \sigma_K$ on $\mathbb{Z}_p[x]$: the base stage carries the Gauss valuation with uniformizer $p$ and residue field fixed to the base field, and each step is a witnessed move — either an increment (a legal Newton-polygon read: a monic irreducible descend polynomial $\psi \ne X$ of degree $g$ with root $\bar z$, a standard lift $\hat\Phi$, coprime new invariants $e', h'$ with the steepness condition $h' > e' \cdot w(\hat\Phi)$, plus the recorded transition core and the child's stage laws) or a recentering (change of center at the same level); the ambient residue field has characteristic $p$. From the move data one defines per-level slopes $\kappa_k = h_k/(e_0 \cdots e_k)$, coordinates $c = (l, \text{slots})$ addressing basis monomials $m_c = p^{l} \prod_r \Phi_r^{s_r}$, and the absolute height $\mathrm{ht}(c) = l + \sum_r s_r \kappa_r$.

The Lean statement 
inductive MoveWitness (σ σ' : Stage p F) : Type u
  | inc (ψ : Polynomial ↥σ.K) (g : ℕ) (Φhat : Polynomial ℤ_[p]) (e' h' : ℕ) (zbar : Fˣ)
      (hyp : TransHyp σ ψ g Φhat e' h' zbar)
      (core : TransitionCoreL σ σ' Φhat e' h' zbar)
      (core' : StageCoreL σ') : MoveWitness σ σ'
  | recenter (cc : ↥σ.K) (tt : Polynomial ℤ_[p])
      (core : IsRecenteringCore σ σ' cc tt)
      (core' : StageCoreL σ') : MoveWitness σ σ'

structure Tower (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (F : Type u) [Field F] [Finite F] where
  K : ℕ
  stg : Fin (K + 1) → Stage p F
  base : IsBaseStage (stg 0) ∧ StageCoreL (stg 0)
  move : ∀ k : Fin K, MoveWitness (stg k.castSucc) (stg k.succ)
  hcharF : CharP F p

lean/LeanUrat/HC1/DefsTower.lean — structure

definition Recorded Newton-polygon side data of a tower (open interface)

The corpus's named open kernel: theorems consume it as an explicit hypothesis and it is never discharged inside this corpus — its per-instance discharge is classifier-side work, so every result gated on it is conditional.

The realizability data of a tower: for each read $k$, a side line, a window (left slot $s_0$ and width), a descend order $\mu$, a recorded vertex height $u^*$, and per-read factor-interior ends, subject to: the slope law (line $k$ descends at $\kappa_k$ per fine slot), the vertex-height tie (the line passes through the window's right endpoint at height $u^*$), the strictly-steeper chain, vertex-entry domination at each interior end, and window nesting (each read's window sits inside the previous read's rim).

The Lean statement 
structure TowerRealizable (T : Tower p F) where
  line : ℕ → MovesC.Line
  s0 : ℕ → ℕ
  wSide : ℕ → ℕ
  mu : ℕ → ℕ
  ustar : ℕ → ℚ
  interiorEnd : ℕ → ℕ
  slope_law : ∀ k : Fin (T.K + 1), (line k.val).slope * (T.frameWidth k.val : ℚ) = T.kappa k
  vertex_tie : ∀ k : Fin (T.K + 1),
    (line k.val).at ((s0 k.val + wSide k.val) * T.frameWidth k.val) = ustar k.val
  steeper : ∀ k : Fin (T.K + 1), ∀ m, m < k.val → (line m).slope < (line k.val).slope
  vertex_entry : ∀ k : Fin (T.K + 1), ∀ m, m ≤ k.val →
    (line m).at (interiorEnd k.val) ≤ (line k.val).at (interiorEnd k.val)
  window : ∀ k : ℕ, k < T.K → s0 (k + 1) + wSide (k + 1) ≤ mu k

lean/LeanUrat/HC1/DefsCar.lean — structure

definition The abstract carrier contract exported to the density pipeline

The seam other corpora consume: they hypothesize this contract, and the (still open) instance node below is the obligation to realize it with a genuine tower.

An abstract package of a graded carrier (the graded object of a valuation) together with abstract histories, coordinates and digits, plus height, block, level-set, slot-coefficient, alphabet-dimension, line, block-edge, interior-end, window, monomial and floor data. Over this pack four statement shapes — the height/slot-minimum/level-locality/staircase package, the typicality (additivity + alphabet-count) package, the line-domination package, and the graded additivity law — form the typed contract that downstream density corpora hypothesize rather than assert; a distinguished implication shape says a consequence follows once every pack satisfies the contract.

The Lean statement 
structure CarrierPackR (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] where
  G : GradedCarrierR p
  Hist : Type
  Coord : Type
  Digit : Type
  [digitAdd : AddCommGroup Digit]
  kIdx : Hist → ℕ
  ht : Hist → Coord → ℚ
  blk : Hist → Coord → ℕ
  lvl : Hist → ℕ → ℚ → Set Coord
  slotCoeff : Hist → ℕ → (Coord → Digit) → G.Coeff
  aDim : Hist → ℕ → ℚ → ℕ
  lines : Hist → ℕ → MovesC.Line
  blockEdge : Hist → ℕ → ℕ
  interiorEnd : Hist → ℕ → ℕ

  mono : Hist → Coord → G.Coeff
  floorB : Hist → ℕ → ℚ

def REL1Pack (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (K : CarrierPackR p) : Prop :=
  LSTStmt' p K ∧ TYPStmt' p K ∧ DOMStmt' p K ∧ SecB1Stmt' p K

def EQ2lawIfREL1' (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (EQ2law : Prop) : Prop :=
  (∀ K : CarrierPackR p, REL1Pack p K) → EQ2law

lean/LeanUrat/HC1/R1_LSTStmt.lean — structure

proved The carry algebra is the ring F[u]/(u^e − z̄)

Certifies that the hand-rolled carry-cocycle object is faithfully the intended algebra, so all digit computations downstream are computations in F[u]/(u^e − z̄).

For any carry-ring pack on the carry algebra there is a ring isomorphism $\Theta : \mathcal{D} \xrightarrow{\;\sim\;} F[u]/(u^e - \bar z)$ sending the homogeneous generator $u_\rho c$ to $c \cdot u^{\rho}$ (with $u$ the class of $X$ in the quotient by $X^e - \bar z$). The concrete function-space model with twisted convolution really is this quotient ring.

The Lean statement 
theorem D4_carryAdjoinRoot {F : Type*} [Field F] {e : ℕ} [NeZero e] {zbar : Fˣ}
    (P : CarryRingPack F e zbar) :
    ∃ Θ : (letI := P.ring;
        CarryAlg F e ≃+* AdjoinRoot (Polynomial.X ^ e - Polynomial.C ((zbar : F)))),
      ∀ (ρ : ZMod e) (c : F),
        Θ (dOf ρ c) = algebraMap F _ c *
          AdjoinRoot.root (Polynomial.X ^ e - Polynomial.C ((zbar : F))) ^ ρ.val

lean/LeanUrat/HC1/D4_carryAdjoinRoot.lean — theorem

proved Digit reading is multiplicative up to carries

The heart of the carry calculus: an earlier untwisted form of this law was refuted by a compiled counterexample condition, forcing the twisted restatement — with the anchor position made explicit — before the proof went through.

Let $\sigma$ be a stage satisfying the core laws, $\psi$ an irreducible descend polynomial of degree $g$ with root $\bar z$, and $\hat\Phi$ a standard lift of the key. Suppose $B, B''$ are nonzero polynomials of degree below the key block, division gives $B \cdot B'' = Q\,\hat\Phi + R_{\mathrm{dev}}$ with small remainder, and the three residuals are monomial units $R(B) = c_B z^{k_B}$, $R(B'') = c_{B''} z^{k_{B''}}$, $R(R_{\mathrm{dev}}) = c_R z^{k_B + k_{B''}}$ (the anchor positions add — the exact-remainder bookkeeping). Then $\mathrm{dig}(R_{\mathrm{dev}}) = \mathrm{dig}(B) \star \mathrm{dig}(B'')$ in the carry algebra: multiplying residual digits and reducing modulo the key is exactly carry-algebra multiplication.

The Lean statement 
theorem D7_digD_mul {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] {F : Type*} [Field F] [Finite F]
    (σ : Stage p F) (hσ : StageCoreL σ) (ψ : Polynomial ↥σ.K) (g : ℕ)
    (hg : ψ.natDegree = g) (hψ : Irreducible ψ) (hψz : ψ ≠ Polynomial.X)
    (Φhat : Polynomial ℤ_[p]) (hlift : IsStandardLift σ ψ g Φhat)
    (zbar : Fˣ) (hzbar : Polynomial.eval₂ σ.K.subtype ((zbar : Fˣ) : F) ψ = 0)
    (e_b : ℕ) [NeZero e_b]
    (B B'' Rdev Q : Polynomial ℤ_[p]) (hB : B ≠ 0) (hB'' : B'' ≠ 0)
    (hBd : B.natDegree < σ.e * g * σ.Φ.natDegree)
    (hB''d : B''.natDegree < σ.e * g * σ.Φ.natDegree)
    (hdiv : B * B'' = Q * Φhat + Rdev)
    (hRd : Rdev.natDegree < σ.e * g * σ.Φ.natDegree)
    (cB cB'' cR : (↥σ.K)ˣ) (kB kB'' : ℤ)
    (h5B : σ.R B = LaurentPolynomial.C (cB : ↥σ.K) * LaurentPolynomial.T kB)
    (h5B'' : σ.R B'' = LaurentPolynomial.C (cB'' : ↥σ.K) * LaurentPolynomial.T kB'')
    (h5R : σ.R Rdev = LaurentPolynomial.C (cR : ↥σ.K) * LaurentPolynomial.T (kB + kB'')) :
    σ.digD zbar e_b Rdev = cmul zbar (σ.digD zbar e_b B) (σ.digD zbar e_b B'')

lean/LeanUrat/HC1/D7_digD_mul.lean — theorem

proved The tower induction starts: base stages exist

The base case of the tower induction, with a genuinely faithful base pin (Gauss valuation, not a vacuous clone) — the non-vacuity the earlier corpus certified was missing from a degenerate variant.

Given base read data at invariants $(e,h)$ — a monic polynomial $\varphi$ over $\mathbb{Z}_p$ whose reduction mod $p$ is irreducible with a root $z_0$ in the ambient finite field, and coprime $e, h \ge 1$ — there exists a base stage: parent valuation the Gauss valuation, uniformizer representative $p$, residue field fixed to the base field, satisfying all core stage laws, with invariants exactly $(e,h)$.

The Lean statement 
theorem T1_baseStage {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] {F : Type*} [Field F] [Finite F]
    (e h : ℕ) (bdata : BaseReadData p F e h) :
    ∃ σ : Stage p F, IsBaseStage σ ∧ StageCoreL σ ∧ σ.e = e ∧ σ.h = h

lean/LeanUrat/HC1/T1_baseStage.lean — theorem

proved The induction step: every legal read produces a child stage

The hardest constructive theorem of the corpus (the full child-stage construction: child valuation, residual laws, digit frame, all stage axioms), assembled from a six-unit decomposition; the growth guard it carries was forced by the compiled countermodel in the next node.

If $\sigma$ is a stage satisfying the core laws, $(\psi, g, \hat\Phi, e', h', \bar z)$ is a legal increment read (the full hypothesis interface: $\psi$ monic irreducible of degree $g$ with $\psi \ne X$ and root $\bar z$, $\hat\Phi$ a standard lift, $e', h' \ge 1$ coprime, and the steepness $h' > e' \cdot w(\hat\Phi)$), and the growth guard $e \cdot g > 1$ holds, then there exists a child stage $\sigma'$ realizing the recorded transition core, satisfying the core laws, whose residue field is the extension of $\sigma$'s generated by $\bar z$, with new invariants $(e', h')$.

The Lean statement 
theorem S9_transStage {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] {F : Type*} [Field F] [Finite F]
    (σ : Stage p F) (hσ : StageCoreL σ) {ψ : Polynomial ↥σ.K} {g : ℕ}
    {Φhat : Polynomial ℤ_[p]} {e' h' : ℕ} {zbar : Fˣ}
    (th : TransHyp σ ψ g Φhat e' h' zbar) (hEG : 1 < σ.e * g) :
    ∃ σ' : Stage p F, TransitionCoreL σ σ' Φhat e' h' zbar ∧ StageCoreL σ' ∧
      σ'.K = σ.nextField zbar ∧ σ'.e = e' ∧ σ'.h = h'

lean/LeanUrat/HC1/S9_transStage.lean — theorem

proved Countermodel: the induction step is false without the growth guard

A machine-checked refutation of the unguarded induction step: it caught a real specification error during development, forced the e·g > 1 guard onto the whole S9 family, and documents why recentering is a separate species.

Under the same legal-read hypotheses but with $e \cdot g = 1$, NO child stage realizing the recorded transition core exists: the record's representative and degree clauses collide, forcing $\deg \Phi < \deg \hat\Phi = e\,g \cdot \deg \Phi$, impossible at $e g = 1$. The $e g = 1$ boundary is a different species of move — recentering — handled by its own theorem.

The Lean statement 
theorem S9_ungated_corner_refuted {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] {F : Type*} [Field F] [Finite F]
    (σ : Stage p F) {ψ : Polynomial ↥σ.K} {g : ℕ}
    {Φhat : Polynomial ℤ_[p]} {e' h' : ℕ} {zbar : Fˣ}
    (th : TransHyp σ ψ g Φhat e' h' zbar) (hcorner : σ.e * g = 1) :
    ¬ ∃ σ' : Stage p F, TransitionCoreL σ σ' Φhat e' h' zbar ∧ StageCoreL σ' ∧
        σ'.K = σ.nextField zbar ∧ σ'.e = e' ∧ σ'.h = h'

lean/LeanUrat/HC1/S9_transStage.lean — countermodel

proved Recentering produces a stage (the boundary species)

Covers exactly the e·g = 1 corner that the increment step provably cannot: together with the base and increment theorems it makes every tower move inhabitable.

If $\sigma$ has $e = 1$ and $c \ne 0$ is a recentering constant in its residue field realized by some polynomial $t$ (nonzero, in the key's coefficient class, of weight $w(\Phi)$, with residual exactly the constant $c$), then there exist a recentered stage $\sigma'$ and such a $t$ with the recorded recentering core, and $\sigma'$ satisfies the core stage laws.

The Lean statement 
theorem S10_recStage {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] {F : Type*} [Field F] [Finite F]
    (σ : Stage p F) (hσ : StageCoreL σ) (he1 : σ.e = 1)
    (cc : ↥σ.K) (hcc : cc ≠ 0)
    (hlift : ∃ tt : Polynomial ℤ_[p], tt ≠ 0 ∧ inC σ.Φ tt ∧ σ.w tt = σ.w σ.Φ ∧
      σ.R tt = LaurentPolynomial.C cc * LaurentPolynomial.T 0) :
    ∃ (σ' : Stage p F) (tt : Polynomial ℤ_[p]),
      IsRecenteringCore σ σ' cc tt ∧ StageCoreL σ'

lean/LeanUrat/HC1/S10_recStage.lean — theorem

proved Every stage of every tower obeys the stage laws

The assembled spine every downstream tower theorem stands on; after the move-witness enrichment its first part is by projection — the real content lives in the existence theorems (base, increment, recentering) plus a separate minimality certificate showing the enrichment added only the independently-certified twist-class laws.

In any tower, every stage satisfies the core laws; and each move's bookkeeping holds as recorded — an increment step sets the residue field to the extension generated by the read's root $\bar z$ and the invariants to $(e', h')$, while a recentering step keeps the residue field and sets $e = 1$.

The Lean statement 
theorem S11_towerSpine {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] {F : Type*} [Field F] [Finite F]
    (T : Tower p F) :
    (∀ k : Fin (T.K + 1), StageCoreL (T.stg k)) ∧
    (∀ k : Fin T.K,
      match T.move k with
      | .inc _ _ _ e' h' zbar _ _ _ =>
          (T.stg k.succ).K = (T.stg k.castSucc).nextField zbar ∧
          (T.stg k.succ).e = e' ∧ (T.stg k.succ).h = h'
      | .recenter _ _ _ _ =>
          (T.stg k.succ).e = 1 ∧ (T.stg k.succ).K = (T.stg k.castSucc).K)

lean/LeanUrat/HC1/S11_towerSpine.lean — theorem

conditional Heights, slot minima, and the staircase floor of a tower

The combinatorial dictionary between tower coordinates and valuations that the counting arguments run on; its staircase leg (ii) was restated onto the step-floor after the raw-line form was machine-refuted (the refutation is kept on file in a scratch module).

Given realizability data for the tower: (i-a) the top-stage weight of a coordinate's basis monomial equals the total stretch times its height, $w_{\mathrm{top}}(m_c) = \mathrm{STR} \cdot \mathrm{ht}(c)$, the stretch $\mathrm{STR}$ being the product of the ramification indices along the tower; (i-b) a nonzero block-supported digit vector's slot coefficient has valuation exactly the minimum height over its support, attained at a minimizing coordinate; (i-c) the graded class of a slot coefficient at level $\gamma'$ depends only on the digits on the $\gamma'$-level set (digits strictly below vanishing); (ii) on factor-interior blocks the history step-floor is constant across the block, equal to the block floor; (iii) any level set strictly above the block floor avoids the floor set.

Hypotheses this rests on: TowerRealizable — the recorded side-line/window data of the tower (slope law, vertex-height tie, strictly steeper chain, vertex-entry domination, window nesting); carried as an explicit hypothesis structure and never discharged inside this corpus (its per-instance discharge is classifier-side work).

The Lean statement 
theorem C1_LST {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] {F : Type*} [Field F] [Finite F]
    (T : Tower p F) (rl : TowerRealizable T) :
    (∀ c : T.Coord, c.MonoNZ →
      (((T.stg (Fin.last T.K)).w (T.mono c) : ℤ) : ℚ) = (T.strTop : ℚ) * T.ht c) ∧
    (∀ (b : ℕ) (y : T.Coord → ↥(T.stg 0).FQ), (Function.support y).Finite →
      (∀ c, y c ≠ 0 → T.blk c = b) → T.slotCoeff b y ≠ 0 →
      ∃ c₀, y c₀ ≠ 0 ∧ T.wQ (T.slotCoeff b y) = (T.ht c₀ : WithTop ℚ) ∧
        ∀ c, y c ≠ 0 → T.ht c₀ ≤ T.ht c) ∧
    (∀ (b : ℕ) (γ' : ℚ) (x y : T.Coord → ↥(T.stg 0).FQ),
      (Function.support x).Finite → (Function.support y).Finite →
      (∀ c ∈ T.levelSet b γ', x c = y c) →
      (∀ c, T.blk c = b → T.ht c < γ' → x c = 0) →
      (∀ c, T.blk c = b → T.ht c < γ' → y c = 0) →
      T.inGr γ' (T.slotCoeff b x) = T.inGr γ' (T.slotCoeff b y)) ∧
    (∀ b, rl.interiorB b → ∀ c : T.Coord, T.blk c = b → rl.floorS c = rl.floorB b) ∧
    (∀ (b : ℕ) (γ' : ℚ), rl.interiorB b → rl.floorB b < γ' →
      T.levelSet b γ' ∩ {c | T.blk c = b ∧ T.ht c ≤ rl.floorB b} = ∅)

lean/LeanUrat/HC1/C1_LST.lean — theorem

conditional A landing stratum has exactly the predicted measure

The measure-theoretic payoff of the tower: each level pins exactly the predicted number of base-p digits, which is what makes wild-prime densities exact powers of p; its original fenced form was certified unprovable, and the exact two missing hypotheses were identified by a compiled certificate before the restatement.

Exact stratum counting at a tower level. Fix a level move (descend polynomial $\psi$, order $\mu$, anchor, transported digit pattern, cutoff $N$) whose pattern is anchor-exact, and code polynomials of degree $< D$ by $D \cdot N$ base-$p$ digits. Suppose a unitriangular coordinate change $\Theta$ and a digit system $E$ with exactly $k$ pinned rows are such that solutions of $E$ (after $\Theta$) cut out exactly the landing cylinder of the stratum, and $0$ is not a solution. Then the stratum has exact normalized density $p^{-k}$ in digit space: $\#\{\text{stratum codes}\} \cdot p^{k} = p^{D N}$.

Hypotheses this rests on: hexact — the stratum's transported digit pattern is anchor-exact (not divisible by X), threaded verbatim from the landing lemma; hzsol — the zero polynomial is not a solution of the pinned digit system; hcore — the full stage-core laws at the level stage (dischargeable from the tower spine, carried explicitly rather than taken); plus the certified coding inputs (unitriangular coordinate change, digit system with exactly k pinned rows cutting out the landing cylinder).

The Lean statement 
theorem S17_levelMeasureExact {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] {F : Type*} [Field F] [Finite F] (T : Tower p F) (k : Fin T.K) (lm : LevelMove T k) (hcore : StageCore (T.stg k.castSucc)) (hg : lm.ψ.natDegree = lm.g) (hgpos : 1 ≤ lm.g) (hmon : lm.ψ.Monic) (hψ : Irreducible lm.ψ) (hψz : lm.ψ ≠ Polynomial.X) (Φhat : Polynomial ℤ_[p]) (hlift : IsStandardLift (T.stg k.castSucc) lm.ψ lm.g Φhat) (hμN : lm.μ < lm.N) (hexact : ¬ Polynomial.X ∣ ∑ j ∈ Finset.range lm.N, lm.Cdig j * lm.ψ ^ j) (D ktot N : ℕ) (hD : D = Φhat.natDegree * lm.N) (hN : 1 ≤ N) (hkN : ktot ≤ D * N) (strat : (Fin (D * N) → ZMod p) → Prop) (code : Polynomial ℤ_[p] → (Fin (D * N) → ZMod p)) (hcode : ∀ x, ∃ f : Polynomial ℤ_[p], f.natDegree < D ∧ code f = x) (hstrat : ∀ f : Polynomial ℤ_[p], f.natDegree < D → (strat (code f) ↔ (f ≠ 0 ∧ StratumData (T.stg k.castSucc) lm.ψ lm.μ lm.a lm.Cdig lm.N f))) (hz : ∀ f : Polynomial ℤ_[p], f.natDegree < D → f = 0 → ¬ strat (code f)) (Θ : (Fin (D * N) → ZMod p) ≃ (Fin (D * N) → ZMod p)) (hΘ : LeanUrat.MovesC.IsUnitriangular fun x => Θ x) (E : DigitSystem (D * N) (ZMod p)) (hEk : E.numPinned = ktot) (hzsol : ¬ E.IsSolution (Θ (code 0))) (hcyl : ∀ (f : Polynomial ℤ_[p]) (B : ℕ → Polynomial ℤ_[p]), f.natDegree < D → f ≠ 0 → IsDevelopment Φhat f B lm.N → (E.IsSolution (Θ (code f)) ↔ LandingCylinderL (T.stg k.castSucc) lm.ψ lm.g lm.μ lm.a lm.Cdig B lm.N)) : Nat.card {x : Fin (D * N) → ZMod p // strat x} * p ^ ktot = p ^ (D * N)

lean/LeanUrat/HC1/S17_levelMeasureExact.lean — theorem

open How many digit values a level can realize (open, statement mistyped)

One of the corpus's two remaining fenced unproved goals, kept open deliberately: the statement quantifies over the wrong index set (level-set coordinates instead of the height alignment class); the restatement is queued for the project lead's explicit approval (the project never changes a fenced statement without it), and the corrected quantification is already specified by the compiled certificate node.

As currently fenced: on a nonempty level set, (1) the alphabet — the group of graded classes reachable by level-set digit vectors — has cardinality $p^{\dim}$; (2) it is full (equal in size to the top residue field) if and only if every level-set coordinate contributes a nonzero class; (3) if some coordinate is degenerate, the dimension drops strictly. Conjunct (1) is separately proved; conjuncts (2)–(3) are mistyped (see the certificate node), so the fenced statement is not provable as written.

The Lean statement 
theorem C6_alphabetCard {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] {F : Type*} [Field F] [Finite F]
    (T : Tower p F) (b : ℕ) (γ : ℚ) (hne : (T.levelSet b γ).Nonempty) :
    Nat.card ↥(T.alphabet b γ) = p ^ T.aDim b γ ∧
    (Nat.card ↥(T.alphabet b γ) = Nat.card ↥(T.stg (Fin.last T.K)).K ↔
      ∀ c : ↥(T.levelSet b γ), ∃ y : ↥(T.stg 0).FQ,
        T.typComposite b γ (Pi.single c y) ≠ 0) ∧
    ((∃ c : ↥(T.levelSet b γ), ∀ y : ↥(T.stg 0).FQ,
        T.typComposite b γ (Pi.single c y) = 0) →
      T.aDim b γ < Nat.log p (Nat.card ↥(T.stg (Fin.last T.K)).K))

lean/LeanUrat/HC1/C6_alphabetCard.lean — theorem

proved Certificate: the alphabet-count statement collapses to a refuted claim

Rather than proving a wrong statement or silently changing it, the corpus compiled a certificate of exactly why the fenced statement fails and left the repair as a named item awaiting the project lead's approval.

A compiled certificate that the alphabet-count statement is mistyped: for every tower, every level-set coordinate automatically realizes a nonzero class (level-set membership forces attainability by weight detection), so the right-hand side of conjunct (2) is a theorem and the hypothesis of conjunct (3) is refutable outright. Consequently the fenced statement, if it held, would force unconditional fullness $\#\mathbb{A}(b,\gamma) = \#F_{K+1}$ on every nonempty level set — precisely the uniform display already refuted at shallow heights (concrete towers exist where the alphabet is strictly smaller). The correct statement must quantify over the alignment class of heights, not over level-set coordinates.

The Lean statement

The carry-bridge kernel exported to the run-level classifier corpus (wired there statement-verbatim), proved with no unproved goals and no axioms beyond Lean's core — with a loud disclosure: for steep reads with e·g ≥ 2 the recorded-run hypotheses are contradictory (the inconsistency node below), so those legs hold vacuously and the theorem's non-vacuous content is exactly the recentering-shaped (e·g = 1) range; the interface repair awaits the project lead's approval.

lean/LeanUrat/HC1/V9_K1nonrec.lean — theorem

proved Refutation record: the recorded steep-read transition is contradictory

The corpus's standing interface finding of record: it exposes that a companion transport theorem in the same file is proved vacuously (its hypotheses are contradictory — disclosed in-file), and the named repair (re-key the recorded child at the regraded side value) is deliberately left awaiting the project lead's approval rather than silently applied.

A machine-checked inconsistency in the run-recording interface: the recorded transition data for a steep read ($e^\star h_\sigma < h^\star$) with $e^\star g \ge 2$ — child key equal to the read lift, ramification tie $e = e^\star$, parent-weight tie, slot-minimum weight law, and Bézout ties — proves False. Two of the child's own laws weigh $\Phi^{e^\star g}$ differently, forcing $h^\star = (e^\star)^2 g\, h_\sigma$ (hence $e^\star = 1$, $t^\star = 0$), after which two renderings of the key's residual clash. Consequence: genuinely steep increments are unrecordable under the current interface, so any theorem quantifying over recorded steep reads is vacuous there.

The Lean statement 
theorem V10_readTransition_incompatible {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] {F : Type*} [Field F]
    [Finite F] (σ σ' : Stage p F) (estar hstar : ℕ) (sstar tstar : ℤ) (g : ℕ)
    (ψ : Polynomial ↥σ.K) (Φhat : Polynomial ℤ_[p])
    (hsteep : (estar : ℤ) * (σ.h : ℤ) < (hstar : ℤ))
    (hEG : 2 ≤ estar * g)
    (hcop : Nat.gcd estar hstar = 1)
    (hbez : (estar : ℤ) * sstar + (hstar : ℤ) * tstar = 1)
    (he1t : estar = 1 → tstar = 0)
    (hψ0 : ψ.coeff 0 ≠ 0)
    (hlift : IsReadLift σ ψ g estar hstar Φhat)
    (hkey : σ'.Φ = Φhat) (hce : σ'.e = estar) (hcw : ∀ x, σ'.wPrev x = σ.w x)
    (hslot : IsSlotMinWeight σ'.w Φhat estar hstar σ.w)
    (hs_tie : σ'.s = sstar) (ht_tie : σ'.t = tstar) : False

lean/LeanUrat/HC1/V10_transportWindow.lean — countermodel

proved Concrete seal: the carry algebra checked over the field of four elements

One of the corpus's concrete gates: a fully computed instance confirming that the abstract carry apparatus produces the right numbers on a real field — every kernel in the project gets such a concrete computational check.

A computational check over $\mathbb{F}_4$: there is a unit $\bar z$ with $\bar z^2 = \bar z + 1$ such that in every carry-ring pack with $e = 2$: $u_1 \cdot u_1 = \bar z\, u_0$ (a genuine carry into the twist), $u_1 c \cdot u_0 c' = u_1(c c')$ (no carry), and the carry cocycle satisfies the associativity identity $c(\rho,\sigma) + c(\rho+\sigma,\tau) = c(\sigma,\tau) + c(\rho,\sigma+\tau)$.

The Lean statement 
theorem G2_carryGate :
    ∃ zbar : (GaloisField 2 2)ˣ,
      ((zbar : GaloisField 2 2) ^ 2 = (zbar : GaloisField 2 2) + 1) ∧
      ∀ P : CarryRingPack (GaloisField 2 2) 2 zbar,
        (letI := P.ring;
          dOf (1 : ZMod 2) (1 : GaloisField 2 2) * dOf 1 1
            = dOf 0 ((zbar : GaloisField 2 2))) ∧
        (∀ c c' : GaloisField 2 2, letI := P.ring;
          dOf (1 : ZMod 2) c * dOf 0 c' = dOf 1 (c * c')) ∧
        (∀ ρ σ τ : ZMod 2,
          carryExp ρ σ + carryExp (ρ + σ) τ = carryExp σ τ + carryExp ρ (σ + τ))

lean/LeanUrat/HC1/G2_carryGate.lean — theorem

Hard core 2: the existence bridge (HC2) 4 definition · 3 open · 8 proved · 5 conditional
Builds jet presentations from graded seeds; defines and gates classifier runs

HC2 is the bridge between the Newton-polygon classifier's combinatorial engine and the graded presentation ring. It does three things: (1) defines, polynomial-explicitly, what it means for a recorded tower of Newton-polygon reads to be an actual classifier run on a monic p-adic polynomial f, and proves the structural laws of runs (key towers, canonicity of lifts, Galois normalization, rigidity of normalized classes); (2) proves that from a small "graded seed" — the presentation fragment that the graded-ring corpus HC1 must supply — the full jet-presentation package (digit-system chain, fresh clauses, counting laws) assembles, discharging the presentability and threshold interfaces the density-counting corpus MovesD consumes; (3) checks both directions with compiled concrete gates at p = 2 and p = 3, including a recomputation of the 1/4 inert-stratum density, and keeps compiled refutations of two retired over-strong statements on record. Remaining obligations are carried either as named hypotheses that consumers quantify over explicitly or as fenced unproved goals (nine recorded ones, five of them in the zero-set induction step) — the corpus declares zero axioms.

Builds on: MovesC, Moves, MovesD, HC1

definition What it means for a recorded tower to be a run on f

The central new definition of the corpus — its inbound definitional boundary: the six-clause per-read bundle it references is the single negotiating interface that the transport corpus re-keys to; its non-vacuity is not assumed but compiled (see the run-predicate gate).

Fix a prime $p$ and a finite residue field $F$. A history is a recorded tower of Newton-polygon reads: each node carries a stage (a key polynomial $\Phi$ over $\mathbb{Z}_p$ with a weight function $w$ and a residual-coefficient map $R$), a side of slope $h/e$ with total weight $\gamma$, a digit pattern, a residual order $\mu$, a residue root $\bar z$, and a lift. The run predicate asserts: $f$ is monic of degree $n$, the history is internally coherent, and at every read $i$ the $\Phi_i$-adic expansion $f = \sum_j B_j \Phi_i^j$ realizes all recorded data — (i) the recorded side is a genuine supporting line of the expansion's polygon: $e\,w(B_j) + jh \ge \gamma$ for every nonzero $B_j$, with equality exactly at the recorded stride slots $j = s_0 + ek$ with nonzero pattern digit; (ii) at each such slot the coefficient's residual is the recorded monomial unit with scalar $\mathrm{pat}(k)$; (iii) the frame residual of $f$ has the recorded anchor and residual order $\mu$; (iv) the recorded landing law holds at a designated next key, equal to the recorded child key at every interior read; (v) the node's lift is the one dictated by the canonical policy and its residue root is the canonical root; (vi) the $\mu$-th coefficient of $f$'s expansion in the next key has residue digit equal to the recorded transported vertex value.

The Lean statement 
def ReadsOf (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (F : Type*) [Field F] [Finite F] (n : ℕ)
    (f : Polynomial ℤ_[p]) (H : History p F) : Prop :=
  f.Monic ∧ f.natDegree = n ∧ HistoryCoherent H ∧
  ∀ (i : ℕ) (hi : i < H.nodes.length),
    ∃ (B : ℕ → Polynomial ℤ_[p]) (Nd : ℕ) (Φnext : Polynomial ℤ_[p]),
      IsDevelopment (H.nodes[i]'hi).σ.Φ f B Nd ∧
      (∀ hi1 : i + 1 < H.nodes.length, Φnext = (H.nodes[i+1]'hi1).σ.Φ) ∧
      SideReads (H.nodes[i]'hi) B Nd Φnext

lean/LeanUrat/HC2/Defs.lean — definition

definition The graded seed: the interface the presentation ring must fill

The inbound interface: supplying one of these per class and level is HC1's open deliverable (the hypothesis named SEED-EXIST); the seed-law list is fenced — a first transcription over-claimed one law and was machine-refuted, then repaired with a chart-coverage guard (a documented correction, not a silent fix).

For a history $H$ and box size $n \times N$: a family of presentation predicates $\mathrm{pres}_i(x, f)$ between polynomials $f$ and digit vectors $x \in \mathbb{F}_p^{nN}$ indexed by the $n \times N$ coordinate box (coefficient positions $\times$ $p$-adic levels), such that (a) the level-0 presentation reads off the literal base-$p$ digits of $f$'s coefficients; (b) every monic degree-$n$ polynomial admits a presentation at every stage; (c) consecutive presentations are related by unitriangular digit transports $\Theta_i$; (d) presentations are block-local in the $i$-th key's expansion; (e) each recorded exact-valuation support $S$ carries a full digit alphabet of size $p^{|S|}$ (every assignment attained); and (f) the seed laws: a valuation clause $w(B_j) \ge \beta$ is — whenever the chart records the entire zero system of the threshold, the box-size proviso — literally the vanishing of the chart digits of height $< \beta$; attainable accounting on value supports; the transports are literal on valuation content; and the root read carries no exact-valuation coordinate in its factor interior. This is exactly the fragment of the jet package that the graded-ring construction must supply; HC2 builds everything else from it.

The Lean statement 
structure PresentSeed (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (F : Type*) [Field F] [Finite F]
    (H : History p F) (n N : ℕ) (keys : ℕ → Polynomial ℤ_[p]) where
  pres : ℕ → (Fin (n * N) → ZMod p) → Polynomial ℤ_[p] → Prop
  pres_zero : ∀ (x : Fin (n * N) → ZMod p) (f : Polynomial ℤ_[p]), pres 0 x f ↔
      ∀ j : Fin (n * N), x j = baseDigit p (boxChart n N j).1 (f.coeff (boxChart n N j).2)
  pres_total : ∀ i : ℕ, i ≤ H.nodes.length → ∀ f : Polynomial ℤ_[p],
      f.Monic → f.natDegree = n → ∃ x, pres i x f
  Theta : ℕ → ((Fin (n * N) → ZMod p) → (Fin (n * N) → ZMod p))
  Theta_uni : ∀ i : ℕ, IsUnitriangular (Theta i)
  pres_theta : ∀ i : ℕ, i < H.nodes.length →
      ∀ (x : Fin (n * N) → ZMod p) (f : Polynomial ℤ_[p]), pres (i+1) x f → pres i (Theta i x) f
  pres_block : ∀ i : ℕ, i < H.nodes.length →
      ∀ (f f' : Polynomial ℤ_[p]) (x x' : Fin (n * N) → ZMod p),
        pres (i+1) x f → pres (i+1) x' f' →
      ∀ (B B' : ℕ → Polynomial ℤ_[p]) (Nd Nd' : ℕ),
        IsDevelopment (keys i) f B Nd → IsDevelopment (keys i) f' B' Nd' →
        ∀ c : Fin (n * N),
          B ((boxChart n N c).2 / (keys i).natDegree) = B' ((boxChart n N c).2 / (keys i).natDegree) →
          x c = x' c
  typObj : ∀ (i : ℕ) (hi : i < H.nodes.length) (S : Finset (Fin (n * N))),
      IsValueSupport H (boxChart n N) i (H.nodes[i]'hi) S →
      TypObject p (n * N) S (Fin S.card → ZMod p)
  zcSeed : ZCSeedLaws H (boxChart n N) pres Theta keys

lean/LeanUrat/HC2/Defs.lean — structure

definition The two named hypotheses (never axioms): stage transition and uniqueness

The corpus's discipline: content that could not yet be proved is a hypothesis every consumer names, never an axiom; the uniqueness hypothesis as typed is itself conditionally refuted by a compiled scratch witness and is consumed by nothing — the final uniqueness form is a pending negotiation.

Two open obligations are carried as named hypothesis objects that consumers quantify over explicitly — the corpus declares no axioms. Stage transition: for every lawful stage $\sigma$, every monic irreducible residual factor $\psi \ne X$ of degree $g \ge 1$ with root $\bar z$, every coprime read pair $(e', h')$ with normalized Bézout data, and every read-pair lift $\hat\Phi$ of $\psi$ meeting the augmentation threshold, a successor stage with key $\hat\Phi$ and the recorded transition laws exists. Uniqueness: for a fixed factorization shape, any two members of the shape's normalized prefix set whose invariant data are Galois-conjugate have literally equal invariant data.

The Lean statement 
def StageTransHyp (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (F : Type*) [Field F] [Finite F] : Prop :=
  ∀ (σ : Stage p F), StageCoreL σ →
    ∀ (ψ : Polynomial ↥σ.K) (g : ℕ), ψ.Monic → ψ.natDegree = g → Irreducible ψ →
      ψ ≠ Polynomial.X → 1 ≤ g →
    ∀ (zbar : Fˣ), Polynomial.eval₂ σ.K.subtype ((zbar : Fˣ) : F) ψ = 0 →
    ∀ (e' h' : ℕ) (s' t' : ℤ), 1 ≤ e' → 1 ≤ h' → Nat.gcd e' h' = 1 →
      (e' : ℤ) * s' + (h' : ℤ) * t' = 1 → 0 ≤ t' ∧ t' < (e' : ℤ) →
    ∀ (Φhat : Polynomial ℤ_[p]), IsReadLift σ ψ g e' h' Φhat →
      IAug σ Φhat e' h' →
    ∃ σ' : Stage p F, σ'.Φ = Φhat ∧ σ'.e = e' ∧ σ'.h = h' ∧ σ'.s = s' ∧ σ'.t = t' ∧
      TransitionCoreL σ σ' Φhat e' h' zbar ∧ StageCoreL σ'

def OmUniqHyp {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] {F : Type*} [Field F] [Finite F]
    (n : ℕ) (pol : CanonPolicy p F) (P : Shape n) : Prop :=
  ∀ H H' : History p F, H ∈ PrefSet n pol P → H' ∈ PrefSet n pol P →
    EtaGalEq (etaData (P : ShapePrefix) H) (etaData (P : ShapePrefix) H') →
    etaData (P : ShapePrefix) H = etaData (P : ShapePrefix) H'

lean/LeanUrat/HC2/Defs.lean — definition

definition The canonical lifting policy

The one policy instance every normalized class in the pipeline is keyed to; its totality on coherent realizable histories is a proved theorem of this corpus, not an assumption.

The classifier is run with one lift rule. At a recentering node $\nu$, the exact lift specification asks for a polynomial $t$ over $\mathbb{Z}_p$ in the key's coefficient class with $t \ne 0$, $w(t) = w(\Phi)$, and residual equal to the constant monomial at the recorded center. The canonical policy chooses (by the axiom of choice) any realizer of this specification when one exists, else $0$. It is deterministic and blind: it reads only the node's stage and center, never its lift field. It is ratified that every realizer of the same specification yields identical graded data, masses, counts and gates downstream, so the choice function can later be replaced by an explicit formula with zero consumer changes.

The Lean statement 
def RecenterLiftSpec {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] {F : Type*} [Field F] [Finite F]
    (ν : Node p F) (tL : Polynomial ℤ_[p]) : Prop :=
  inC ν.σ.Φ tL ∧ tL ≠ 0 ∧ ν.σ.w tL = ν.σ.w ν.σ.Φ ∧
    ν.σ.R tL = LaurentPolynomial.C ν.center * LaurentPolynomial.T 0

noncomputable def polOM (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (F : Type*) [Field F] [Finite F] :
    CanonPolicy p F where
  liftOf ν := if h : ∃ tL, RecenterLiftSpec ν tL then h.choose else 0
  blind := by
    intro ν t
    rfl

lean/LeanUrat/HC2/Defs.lean — definition

conditional Seed-to-jet assembly: the bridge's core deliverable

The corpus capstone: this field-by-field assembly is what turns the small seed into the 29-field package (29 named structure fields) the engine's declared boundary deferred; its own proof body is complete, but it is conditional both on named hypotheses and on the two open lemmas in its dependency cone.

Given a history $H$ with a lawful key tower, a graded seed at box size $n \times N$ (with $N \ge 1$), coherence, realizability, confinement to the degree-$n$ box, the root-shape law, an emitted-value family, and the per-step condition that each read's fresh clause supports are unpinned on the prior state — the full jet-presentation package for $H$ exists: a chain of digit systems on the $n \times N$ box, starting at the full box, with per-read fresh clauses (coordinate strips plus value clauses) satisfying the band, cover, assembly, multiplicity, recursion and zero-set laws that the counting engine consumes.

Hypotheses this rests on: hkeys — a lawful key tower (dischargeable from a run); S — the graded seed (HC1's open deliverable); hcoh/hreal/hbox — coherence, realizability, box confinement; hd4 — the root read's shape law; hunp — fresh supports unpinned per step (its discharge is the open zero-set geometry). ADDITIONALLY the compiled proof consumes two still-open lemmas (the zero-set induction step and a level-set no-straddle lemma), so its footprint records unproved dependencies (Lean's sorryAx marker).

The Lean statement 
theorem jetSetup_of_seed {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] {F : Type*} [Field F] [Finite F]
    {n N : ℕ} {H : History p F} {keys : ℕ → Polynomial ℤ_[p]}
    (hkeys : KeysLawful H keys) (S : PresentSeed p F H n N keys) (hN : 1 ≤ N)
    (hcoh : HistoryCoherent H) (hreal : Realizable H) (hbox : InBox n H)
    (hd4 : RootD4 H) (vOf : VOf p (n * N)) (hunp : SeedFreshUnpinned H n N S vOf) :
    Nonempty (JetSetup H n N (n * N))

lean/LeanUrat/HC2/U13_assembly.lean — theorem

conditional Presentability at every level above the threshold

The outbound seam to the density-counting corpus: this is the presentability row that corpus's interface demands, discharged down to the seed.

Fix a factorization shape $P$ of degree $n$. Suppose SEED-EXIST: for every class of $P$'s normalized prefix set and every box level $N$ at or above $P$'s threshold level, a lawful key tower and a graded seed exist. Suppose also normalization of presentation classes, the root-shape law, and the unpinned condition. Then at every such level $N$ the shape is presentable: a presented family — one jet package per class, with the normalization data — exists. The reduction is the deliverable; the seed is the remaining open input.

Hypotheses this rests on: hseed — SEED-EXIST, the single frozen statement of HC1's deliverable (key tower + graded seed per class and level); hnorm — normalization of presentation classes (its policy discharger is fenced, so consumers carry it explicitly); hd4 — root-shape law per class; hunp — per-step unpinned condition. Inherits the unproved-dependency marker (sorryAx) through the assembly theorem's open dependency cone.

The Lean statement 
theorem present_exist_of_seeds {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] {F : Type*} [Field F] [Finite F]
    {n : ℕ} {P : Shape n}
    (hseed : ∀ (i : PrefIdx n (polOM p F) P) (N : ℕ), P.NP (polOM p F) ≤ N →
       ∃ keys : ℕ → Polynomial ℤ_[p], KeysLawful (reprOf i) keys ∧
         Nonempty (PresentSeed p F (reprOf i) n N keys))
    (hnorm : PresentNorm n (polOM p F) P)
    (hd4 : ∀ i : PrefIdx n (polOM p F) P, RootD4 (reprOf i))
    (hunp : ∀ (i : PrefIdx n (polOM p F) P) (N : ℕ) (keys : ℕ → Polynomial ℤ_[p])
       (S : PresentSeed p F (reprOf i) n N keys) (vOf : VOf p (n * N)),
       SeedFreshUnpinned (reprOf i) n N S vOf) :
    ∀ N : ℕ, P.NP (polOM p F) ≤ N →
      Nonempty (Presented p F n N (n * N) (polOM p F) P)

lean/LeanUrat/HC2/U16_threshold.lean — theorem

conditional The digit-system recursion law

The hard kernel of the state chain: its route survived two machine-refuted transcription bugs (untransported-coordinate errors in the attachment spec, each repaired by a documented deviation-correction with the refutation artifact retired only after the repair).

The constructed digit-system chain $\Sigma$ satisfies the frozen recursion the jet package demands: for each read $i$ inside the history, provided read $i$'s fresh clause supports are unpinned on the prior state, a digit vector $x$ solves $\Sigma_{i+1}$ if and only if $\Theta_i(x)$ solves $\Sigma_i$ and $\Theta_i(x)$ satisfies read $i$'s fresh stratum. The condition is automatically free at $i = 0$ (nothing is pinned before the root read).

Hypotheses this rests on: hunp — read-i's fresh supports carry no pin of the prior state; genuinely gating: the unconditioned form was machine-refuted on junk histories and retired, and the condition's per-step discharge at i > 0 is exactly the open zero-set geometry.

The Lean statement 
theorem mkSigma_recursion {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] {F : Type*} [Field F] [Finite F]
    (H : History p F) (n N : ℕ) {keys : ℕ → Polynomial ℤ_[p]}
    (S : PresentSeed p F H n N keys) (vOf : VOf p (n * N))
    (hunp : SeedFreshUnpinned H n N S vOf)
    (i : ℕ) (hi : i < H.nodes.length) :
    ∀ x : Fin (n * N) → ZMod p,
      (mkSigma H n N S vOf (i+1)).IsSolution x ↔
        ((mkSigma H n N S vOf i).IsSolution (S.Theta i x) ∧
          mkStratum H n N S vOf i (S.Theta i x))

proved Every run has a lawful key tower

No axioms beyond Lean's core. This is the re-keyed replacement of a retired claim: the earlier version, keyed only to coherence rather than to runs, is provably false — see the compiled refutation node.

If a history $H$ is a classifier run on $f$, then a key family $(\mathrm{keys}_i)$ exists that equals the recorded child key at every interior read and satisfies the recorded landing law at every read, the final one included. (The keys are extracted from the run's own designated-next-key witnesses.)

lean/LeanUrat/HC2/U1_keysExist.lean — theorem

conditional The canonical policy is total on coherent histories

Verified clean footprint; guarantees the policy definition never silently degenerates to its junk branch on the histories the pipeline actually uses.

At every recentering node of a coherent, realizable history, a realizer of the recentering-lift specification exists — assuming the complete eligibility triple (unramified frame, nonzero recorded center, augmentation threshold) at the final read only; interior reads obtain it from coherence. Hence the canonical policy's choice is well-defined and satisfies its specification at every such node. Each leg of the eligibility triple is machine-checked necessary (a legal coherent history kills every realizer when any leg fails at the last node), and under the run predicate the final landing law supplies the triple outright, so runs need no extra hypothesis.

Hypotheses this rests on: helig — the complete eligibility triple at the final read only; this guard is not dead weight: its necessity is machine-checked in the same file (the unguarded statement was obstruction-refuted), and it is discharged automatically for runs.

The Lean statement 
theorem polOM_total {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] {F : Type*} [Field F] [Finite F]
    {H : History p F} (hcoh : HistoryCoherent H) (hreal : Realizable H)
    (i : ℕ) (hi : i < H.nodes.length)
    (hrec : (H.nodes[i]'hi).species = ReadSpecies.recentering)
    (helig : i + 1 = H.nodes.length → D10Eligible (H.nodes[i]'hi)) :
    ∃ tL : Polynomial ℤ_[p], RecenterLiftSpec (H.nodes[i]'hi) tL

lean/LeanUrat/HC2/U25_polTotal.lean — theorem

proved Galois normalization of residue roots

Verified clean footprint; one of the two halves (with lift swap, still open) of the normalization totality that presentation classes rest on.

Every shape-matched, coherent, realizable, in-box history $H$ has a Galois class-mate: there exist a field automorphism $\varphi$ of $F$ and a history $H'$ matching the same shape, fully root-normalized (every node's residue root is the canonical root), whose invariant data at every read is exactly the $\varphi$-transport of $H$'s — hence Galois-equal to $H$'s — and which is again coherent, realizable and in-box; if $H$ was canonical for the lifting policy, so is $H'$.

The Lean statement 
theorem galois_normalize {n : ℕ} {P : Shape n} (H : History p F)
    (hm : (P : ShapePrefix).MatchesHist H) (hcoh : HistoryCoherent H)
    (hreal : Realizable H) (hbox : InBox n H) :
    ∃ (φ : F ≃+* F) (H' : History p F),
      (P : ShapePrefix).MatchesHist H' ∧
      (∀ (r : ℕ) (hr : r < H'.nodes.length),
        (((H'.nodes[r]'hr).zbar : Fˣ) : F) = canonRoot (H'.nodes[r]'hr)) ∧
      (∀ r : Fin (P : ShapePrefix).reads.length,
        ((etaData (P : ShapePrefix) H' r).1
            = fun k => φ ((etaData (P : ShapePrefix) H r).1 k)) ∧
        ((etaData (P : ShapePrefix) H' r).2
            = fun k => φ ((etaData (P : ShapePrefix) H r).2 k))) ∧
      EtaGalEq (etaData (P : ShapePrefix) H) (etaData (P : ShapePrefix) H') ∧
      HistoryCoherent H' ∧ Realizable H' ∧ InBox n H' ∧
      ((polOM p F).IsCanon H → (polOM p F).IsCanon H')

lean/LeanUrat/HC2/U17b_galois.lean — theorem

proved Rigidity: Galois-transported normalized classes have equal invariants

The transport-keyed form is the third statement of this unit: two earlier stronger forms (unguarded, then adjacency-guarded) were each refuted by compiled scratch witnesses, and a note-read adjudicated the over-strength as a blueprint artifact — the surviving statement is the one the source note actually asserts.

Two members of a shape's normalized prefix set related by a full-history Galois transport — a field automorphism $\varphi$ of $F$ fixing the base frame field pointwise and carrying one history's residue roots, residual-factor images and side digits onto the other's — have literally equal invariant data. Proof mechanism: the least-root normalization fixes the residue root at every read, and the coherence tower propagates base-fixedness of $\varphi$ up every frame field, so every recorded value is $\varphi$-fixed and the transport collapses to the identity.

The Lean statement 
theorem presentNorm_unique {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] {F : Type*} [Field F] [Finite F]
    {n : ℕ} {P : Shape n} :
    ∀ H H' : History p F,
      H ∈ PrefSet n (polOM p F) P → H' ∈ PrefSet n (polOM p F) P →
      (∃ φ : F ≃+* F, HistGalTransport φ H H') →
      etaData (P : ShapePrefix) H = etaData (P : ShapePrefix) H'

open The zero-set induction step (open)

The pre-declared escalation-risk center of the whole bridge: five of the corpus's nine remaining unproved goals live here, and the seed-to-jet assembly's zero-set field flows through it — this hole is what keeps the assembly capstone conditional on unproved dependencies.

The inductive step of the zero-set/counting invariant, still open: if the invariant holds for the digit system after reads $0, \dots, i$ (downsets literal at the recorded heights, pins confined, interior coordinates zero), then it holds after read $i+1$ — the transported pins remain literal at the new frame's heights, the fresh cuts are exact, and interior coordinates of the new read solve to zero. Split into four legs (transport, fresh exactness, interior zeros, confinement) plus the assembled step; the interior-zeros leg is the blocking one: without the seed's transport-literality law the assembled statement is refutable for a bad seed — which is exactly why that law was placed as a seed obligation rather than assumed.

The pre-revision claim 'every coherent history admits a lawful key tower' is false. lean/LeanUrat/HC2/U1_negWitness.lean — countermodel

3 A finite alphabet of patterns

Only a bounded packet of a node's data — slope data, residual class, a bounded carry window — influences its subtree; the packet is the node's pattern (species). The number of patterns is proved finite with an explicit bound at every degree; at degree three the catalogue is machine-enumerated (exactly 53) and its closure under every transition is verified by exhaustive computation. Connecting every real classifier run to catalogue walks at every degree rides the same open bridge as the previous stage.

$$|\mathcal{A}_n| \;\le\; (n+2)^{\,n^2+3n+12},\qquad |\mathcal{A}_3| = 53\ \text{(enumerated)}$$
The species alphabet (MovesSp) 5 definition · 12 proved · 2 conditional
The finite, prime-independent alphabet of tree-reading states, exactly enumerated

This corpus builds the species alphabet: the finite, prime-independent set of bookkeeping states ("species") that a single read of the canonical p-adic factorization tree retains once all digit values, heights and lifts are forgotten. It proves the alphabet finite with an explicit bound, shows the successor graph becomes a DAG once self-loops are struck (every collapsed walk has at most 6n² letters), and proves exact catalogues at degree 1 (one letter) and degree 3 (53 letters with their complete edge menus) by exhaustive kernel computation (native_decide banned). Its completeness theorems connect the alphabet to the actual tree computation through an explicitly conditional engine interface: the per-read laws are consumed as named hypotheses whose discharge belongs to the tree-computation corpus (MovesD), with a real-carrier instantiation and a non-vacuity witness at n = 2 answering a finding of an independent adversarial review.

Builds on: Moves, MovesD, MovesT, MovesC

definition The species: what one tree read retains

The letter of the alphabet: every finiteness, DAG, and catalogue statement in this corpus quantifies over these tuples, and no field mentions a prime.

A species is the bookkeeping tuple retained from one read of the canonical factorization tree: a position tag (root / post-increment / post-recentering), the current key degree $D$, pool residue degree $w$, window width $W$, stride $e$, raw left endpoint $s_0'$, normalized side length $\ell$, the census anchor $a = \lfloor s_0'/e \rfloor$, digit count $d$, the slot set $\{a, \dots, a+\ell\}$, the residual partition $\lambda = \{(g^{(i)}, \mu^{(i)})\}$ (a multiset of degree–multiplicity pairs), an optional selected pair $(g,\mu) \in \lambda$, and the two flank compositions. Heights, depths, digit values and lifts are deliberately forgotten — the datum is value-free, which is what makes the alphabet finite and independent of the prime.

The Lean statement 
structure Species where
  tag : Tag
  D : ℕ            -- current key degree
  w : ℕ            -- pool residue degree
  W : ℕ            -- window width
  e : ℕ            -- stride e = e_read
  s0 : ℕ           -- raw left endpoint s₀′
  ell : ℕ          -- normalized side length ℓ = w′/e = deg R_anch
  a : ℕ            -- CENSUS anchor ⌊s₀′/e⌋
  d : ℕ            -- digit count = #slots
  slots : Finset ℕ -- census slot set {p̂_k}
  lam : Multiset (ℕ × ℕ)   -- residual partition λ = {(g⁽ⁱ⁾, μ⁽ⁱ⁾)}
  sel : Option (ℕ × ℕ)     -- selection (g, μ) ∈ λ, or ⊥ = none
  lflank : List ℕ  -- left flank composition (composition of span s₀′)
  rflank : List ℕ  -- right flank composition (span W − s₀′ − w′)
deriving DecidableEq

lean/LeanUrat/MovesSp/Defs.lean — structure

definition The catalogue: root letters closed under the generation rule

The central object of the corpus; the prime-independence of the alphabet is discharged by typing — the same catalogue serves every prime.

The degree-$n$ catalogue $\mathcal{S}_n^{\mathrm{raw}}$ is the least set of species containing every coherent, budget-admissible root-stage letter (root tag, $(D,w,W)=(1,1,n)$) and closed under the successor rule: $s'$ may follow $s$ when the raw stage laws hold — for the selected pair $(g,\mu)$ of $s$, $D' = e g D$, $w' = g w$, $W' = \mu$, with the new tag determined by whether $eg \ge 2$ — and $s'$ is itself coherent and budget-admissible. Coherence is the geometric bookkeeping (flank–span accounting, anchor–position, slot-set, residual-sum, and the terminal rule: no selection iff $W = 1$); budgets cap every field by $n$. No prime appears anywhere in the definition.

The Lean statement 
def SuccStep (n : ℕ) (s s' : Species) : Prop :=
  StageLaws s s' ∧ Coherent s' ∧ Budget n s'

inductive InCatalogue (n : ℕ) : Species → Prop
  | root {s} : RootAdmissible n s → InCatalogue n s
  | step {s s'} : InCatalogue n s → SuccStep n s s' → InCatalogue n s'

def SnRaw (n : ℕ) : Set Species := {s | InCatalogue n s}

lean/LeanUrat/MovesSp/Defs.lean — definition

definition The engine interface: an abstract canonical tree with per-read laws

The conditionality of every completeness theorem lives here: each law field is an open engine obligation to be discharged by the tree-computation corpus (MovesD), and the verdict field is deliberately weak — it says only that some verdict exists, with the full halting-rule content recorded separately as the strong verdict pin.

An abstract model of the canonical factorization tree of a monic degree-$n$ polynomial $f$ over $\mathbb{Z}_p$: a nonempty type of branches, each carrying a catalogue-word length in $\mathbb{N} \cup \{\infty\}$ and a species datum per read, subject to the engine laws — the first read is root-admissible, consecutive reads obey the raw stage recursion, coherence and budgets hold at every read, the word closes at its first confirming $W = 1$ read, and every finitely-ended nonempty word carries some terminal verdict. Infinite branches are represented (length $\infty$, catalogued prefixwise).

The Lean statement 
structure CanTreeModel (n p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (f : Polynomial ℤ_[p]) where
  Branch : Type
  hTotal : Nonempty Branch
  len : Branch → ℕ∞
  hN1 : n = 1 → ∀ η, 0 < len η
  datum : Branch → ℕ → Species
  hRoot : ∀ η, 0 < len η →
    RootStage n (datum η 0) ∧ Coherent (datum η 0) ∧ Budget n (datum η 0)
  hStage : ∀ η (r : ℕ), (r + 1 : ℕ∞) < len η →
    StageLaws (datum η r) (datum η (r + 1))
  hCoh : ∀ η (r : ℕ), (r : ℕ∞) < len η →
    Coherent (datum η r) ∧ Budget n (datum η r)
  hHalt : ∀ η (r : ℕ), (r : ℕ∞) < len η → (datum η r).W = 1 →
    len η = (r + 1 : ℕ∞)
  verdict : Branch → Option Verdict
  hVerdictPin : ∀ η (m : ℕ), len η = (m : ℕ∞) → 0 < m → (verdict η).isSome

lean/LeanUrat/MovesSp/Defs.lean — structure

definition Collapsing self-loop runs of a word

The device that turns arbitrarily deep self-looping walks into boundedly long DAG walks; all length bounds in the corpus are stated for the collapsed word.

The run-collapse of a list of species keeps one letter per maximal run of equal consecutive letters, striking the depth direction of self-loops: the empty and singleton lists are fixed, and in $s :: s' :: r$ the head $s$ is dropped exactly when $s = s'$.

The Lean statement 
def collapseRuns : List Species → List Species
  | [] => []
  | [s] => [s]
  | s :: s' :: rest =>
      if s = s' then collapseRuns (s' :: rest)
      else s :: collapseRuns (s' :: rest)

lean/LeanUrat/MovesSp/Defs.lean — definition

proved The species alphabet is finite, with an explicit prime-independent bound

A cornerstone of uniformity: one fixed finite alphabet of tower behaviors covers all primes at once, so densities can be organized by finitely many species patterns.

For every $n \ge 1$ the catalogue $\mathcal{S}_n^{\mathrm{raw}}$ is finite, with the explicit (deliberately crude) bound $|\mathcal{S}_n^{\mathrm{raw}}| \le (n+2)^{n^2+3n+12}$. The proof injects each coherent, admissible species into the vectors of length $n^2+3n+12$ with entries in $\{0,\dots,n+1\}$. Since no field of a species mentions a prime, the same alphabet serves every prime $p$ — $p$-independence is discharged by the type itself, not by a clause.

The Lean statement 
theorem SP_FIN (n : ℕ) (hn : 1 ≤ n) : (SnRaw n).Finite ∧
    Nat.card (SnRaw n) ≤ (n+2)^(n^2 + 3*n + 12)

lean/LeanUrat/MovesSp/SP2_finThm.lean — theorem

proved The generated enumeration covers every coherent admissible species

The kernel bridge: every exhaustively computed n = 3 and n = 1 fact routes universal quantification over species through this list (567 candidate species tuples at n = 3), with native_decide banned corpus-wide.

There is an explicitly computed finite list of species — generated by ranging each field over its budget-forced bounds, with the anchor and slot set computed from the other fields — that contains every coherent, budget-admissible species at level $n$. This is the completeness half of the reflection layer: it reduces any 'for all species' claim to a finite kernel scan.

The Lean statement 
theorem speciesEnum_complete {n : ℕ} {s : Species} (hc : Coherent s)
    (hb : Budget n s) : s ∈ speciesEnum n

lean/LeanUrat/MovesSp/SP2_speciesEnum.lean — theorem

proved Collapsed walks in the successor graph have at most 6n² letters

Bounds the tower: however deep a branch recenters, it passes through at most 6n² distinct species states — the finiteness input for entrance-shape and collapsed-word counting.

Along any walk in the successor graph whose consecutive letters are distinct (a collapsed walk, all letters in the catalogue), a rank function — lexicographic in key degree $D$, tag, $n - W$, and non-fullness, packed as a natural number below $6n^2$ — strictly increases. Hence the menu graph with self-loops struck is a DAG, and every collapsed walk has length at most $6n^2$.

The Lean statement 
theorem SP_DAG_walk {n} (hn : 1 ≤ n) {l : List Species}
    (h : CollapsedWalk n l) : l.length ≤ 6 * n^2

lean/LeanUrat/MovesSp/SP4_dagWalk.lean — theorem

proved Self-loops are exactly the post-recentering fixed windows

Isolates the single unbounded direction of a walk — the wild recentering spin — which is exactly what the run-collapse strikes before the 6n² bound applies.

A catalogue letter $s$ has a self-loop edge (it lies in its own successor menu) if and only if $s$ is post-recentering with stride $e = 1$ and selection $(g,\mu) = (1, W)$ — the window equal to its own selection multiplicity. Any other selection strictly grows the key degree, so no other letter can repeat.

The Lean statement 
theorem selfloop_iff {n : ℕ} {s : Species} (hs : InCatalogue n s) :
    s ∈ Succ n s ↔ (s.tag = .postRec ∧ s.e = 1 ∧ s.sel = some (1, s.W))

lean/LeanUrat/MovesSp/SP4_selfloopChar.lean — theorem

conditional Every read of a canonical-tree branch lands in the catalogue

The connecting theorem between the tree computation and the alphabet; its Lean proof is complete, but its substance is conditional on the engine interface being instantiated by the real tree — see the instantiation and non-vacuity nodes for how far that has been discharged.

For every prime $p$, every monic degree-$n$ polynomial $f$ over $\mathbb{Z}_p$, every abstract canonical-tree model of $f$ satisfying the engine laws, every branch $\eta$, and every read $r$ before the word's end: the retained species datum of read $r$ is a member of $\mathcal{S}_n^{\mathrm{raw}}$. The proof is a closure induction: the root read enters via root-admissibility, and each successor read assembles the generation rule from the stage, coherence, and budget laws — the assembly is derived, never assumed.

Hypotheses this rests on: hRoot / hStage / hCoh / hHalt — the interface's per-read engine laws (root read is root-admissible; consecutive reads obey the raw stage recursion; coherence and budgets hold at every read; the word halts at its first W = 1 read), consumed as hypotheses and cited to the tree-computation corpus (MovesD), where they remain OPEN engine obligations; hVerdictPin — the weak verdict pin (every finitely-ended nonempty word carries some verdict), a deliberately weak named carrier of the halting-rule residual, unused by this proof but part of the quantified interface.

The Lean statement 
theorem SP_COMP (n p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (f : Polynomial ℤ_[p])
    (hf : f.Monic) (hdeg : f.natDegree = n) (M : CanTreeModel n p f)
    (η : M.Branch) (r : ℕ) (hr : (r : ℕ∞) < M.len η) :
    InCatalogue n (M.datum η r)

lean/LeanUrat/MovesSp/SP3_compMember.lean — theorem

conditional The membership theorem fired through the real tree carrier

The instantiation record: everything the built tree vocabulary reaches is discharged (including nonemptiness, from a proved root law), and the residual conditionality is compressed into one named, documented premise.

For the actually built canonical-tree object — branches are the realized chains of the constructed tree model at a coefficient-box point, with the root's presence a proved law rather than a hypothesis — and given a species readout (the single named remaining premise supplying the per-read retained datum together with its mirrored engine laws), every read of every realized chain lands in $\mathcal{S}_n^{\mathrm{raw}}$. This answers an external review finding that the membership theorem was 'an interface closure lemma, not the note's actual-tree completeness theorem': the branch carrier is now the real one, and what remains open is exactly one named seam.

Hypotheses this rests on: SpeciesReadout T x — the one named interface premise: a per-read retained-species readout of the built canonical tree, with each engine law restated at the realized carrier; the built vocabulary cannot yet express the datum map itself (the residual partition needs the full residual factorization, and each read's window width is the parent read's μ threaded along the chain).

The Lean statement 
theorem SP_COMP_ofTreeModel (f : Polynomial ℤ_[p])
    (hf : f.Monic) (hdeg : f.natDegree = n)
    (T : TreeModel p F n N m pol) (x : Box p m) (R : SpeciesReadout T x)
    (o : Option (History p F)) (ho : T.mem o x)
    (r : ℕ) (hr : (r : ℕ∞) < R.lenOf o) :
    InCatalogue n (R.datumOf o r)

lean/LeanUrat/MovesSp/SP8_instantiation.lean — theorem

proved The interface is non-vacuous: a real three-read witness at degree 2

Answers-by-exhibition an adversarial review finding that the membership theorems 'can all hold with zero reads and no engine content'; the zero-read model does still satisfy the interface — that residual weakness is documented, not hidden.

There exists a canonical-tree model at $n = 2$, $p = 2$, $f = (x-1)^2$ whose single branch has catalogue-word length 3, whose first read is an actual root-stage letter, and all of whose reads land in $\mathcal{S}_2^{\mathrm{raw}}$ through the membership theorem. The three-letter word is transported from a built toy tree branch, and every interface law is discharged by computation on actual letters — no vacuous premise fires.

The Lean statement 
theorem SP_COMP_nonvacuous_at_n2 :
    ∃ (M : CanTreeModel 2 2 f₂) (η : M.Branch),
      M.len η = 3 ∧
      M.datum η 0 = wordHead ∧ RootStage 2 (M.datum η 0) ∧
      (∀ r : ℕ, (r : ℕ∞) < M.len η → InCatalogue 2 (M.datum η r))

lean/LeanUrat/MovesSp/SP8_instantiation.lean — theorem

definition The strong verdict pin: the full halting-rule residual, recorded

The recorded residual for the halting rule: an external review found the weak field insufficient (it determines neither which verdict, nor where the rule fires, nor the detection cap), so the full-content residual is now typed and named, with the strong-implies-weak bridge proved.

The interface's weak verdict field asserts only that a finitely-ended nonempty word carries some verdict. The strong pin records what a faithful verdict model must actually supply: a halting rule $\tau$ computed from cell data alone (never from $f$) into a fixed verdict alphabet, a dictionary onto the bare labels, the exactly-one-verdict law (a word of length $r+1$ carries exactly the $\tau$-verdict of its closing read; infinite branches carry none), and a uniform detection cap (the verdict is determined by the cap-truncated word). Strong implies weak is proved, so every existing consumer stands, and a strong pin is exhibited on the degree-2 witness.

The Lean statement 
structure StrongVerdictPin {f : Polynomial ℤ_[p]} (M : CanTreeModel n p f) where
  tauV : Species → MovesT.Vd
  dict : MovesT.Vd → Verdict
  htau : ∀ η (r : ℕ), M.len η = ((r + 1 : ℕ) : ℕ∞) →
    M.verdict η = some (dict (tauV (M.datum η r)))
  hinf : ∀ η, M.len η = ⊤ → M.verdict η = none
  cap : ℕ
  hcap : ∀ η η', M.len η = M.len η' →
    (∀ r : ℕ, r < cap → M.datum η r = M.datum η' r) →
    M.verdict η = M.verdict η'

theorem StrongVerdictPin.weak {f : Polynomial ℤ_[p]} {M : CanTreeModel n p f}
    (S : StrongVerdictPin M) :
    ∀ η (k : ℕ), M.len η = (k : ℕ∞) → 0 < k → (M.verdict η).isSome

proved At degree 3 the catalogue is exactly the 53 listed letters

The exact degree-3 alphabet the density instantiation consumes — the hand catalogue from the source notes is now a machine-checked exact enumeration, proved by kernel decide (native_decide banned).

A species lies in $\mathcal{S}_3^{\mathrm{raw}}$ if and only if it is one of 53 explicitly listed, pairwise-distinct letters: 21 root letters, 21 + 6 post-recentering window letters (windows 3 and 2), and 5 terminal letters ($53 = 21+21+6+1+4$, established by exhaustive kernel computation in a companion count). Forward direction: closure induction, with exhaustive kernel scans certifying that root admissibility picks out exactly the 21 root letters and that the successor step never leaves the list of 53; backward: kernel-computed reachability of each letter from a root letter.

The Lean statement 
theorem SnRaw3_eq : ∀ s, InCatalogue 3 s ↔ s ∈ catalogue3

proved Exact successor menus at degree 3

Upgrades the kernel-computed 53×53 edge table to a statement about all species: the degree-3 walk combinatorics downstream can trust the menus as exhaustive.

For every letter $s$ of the 53-letter degree-3 catalogue and every species $s'$ whatsoever: $s'$ is a legal successor of $s$ if and only if $s'$ appears in $s$'s recorded menu list. The full edge table of the degree-3 successor graph is thereby determined exactly — no off-catalogue edge exists.

theorem menu3_exact_global (s : Species) (hs : s ∈ catalogue3)

proved Which letters demand larger residue fields: the exact exclusion lists

The typed-gate story in miniature: it identifies exactly which species patterns are excluded at the smallest primes, while the docstrings state the necessary-not-sufficient caveat so the exclusion is never over-read as a realizability claim.

Filtering the 53 degree-3 letters for those whose residual partition contains at least two (respectively at least three) linear factors — which cannot all be realized by distinct nonzero roots when the residue field is too small — yields exactly the recorded 11-letter (respectively 2-letter) lists. Passing this field-size gate is necessary for a letter to be realized at a given prime, not sufficient.
