lean/LeanUrat/MovesV/V7_ledger.lean — structure

definition The open kernel: height-domain membership factorizes along composite templates

The hinge of clauses (v)/(vi): no unit proves it and none assumes it silently — a compiled census toy elsewhere in this corpus exhibits a system where it is false, so it can only ever ride as a hypothesis.

The height-domain membership factorization: for every one-step move $m$ followed by a template $\gamma$, a concatenated height point $(h_1, h_2)$ lies in the listed semilinear domain of the composite template if and only if $h_1$ lies in the domain of the single step and $h_2$ in the domain of $\gamma$. This is clause (v) of Theorem CTS-M — stated, numerically probed, and never proved in this corpus; a machine-checked toy shows it can genuinely fail, so every consumer (the composition clauses and the solve sentence) takes it as an explicit antecedent.

The Lean statement 
def HMC {n : ℕ} {S : StepSys n} (TE : TmplEvents n S) (D : XHDd n S TE) : Prop :=
  ∀ {α β} (m : S.Move α β) (γ : Template n S β) (h₁ : Hpt (S.dim m))
    (h₂ : Hpt γ.D),
    (D.dom (.cons m γ)).Mem (Hpt.append h₁ h₂) ↔
      (D.dom (.last m)).Mem h₁ ∧ (D.dom γ).Mem h₂

lean/LeanUrat/MovesV/Defs.lean — definition

definition Entrance shapes: a height-retaining prefix plus selection data

The central new object of the note this corpus formalizes: entrances into a block are post-split objects, so at a branching each sibling carries its own shape and its own mark — the granularity every entrance-mass statement is keyed to.

An entrance shape $\varepsilon = (\hat P, (g_{\mathrm{sel}}, \mu_{\mathrm{sel}}), \sigma_{\mathrm{sel}})$: a nonempty shallow shape-prefix $\hat P$ that retains its per-read height data, whose last read carries the selected pair $(g, \mu)$ subject to the eligibility budget, together with a branch-selection record listing, for each branching read of $\hat P$, which item of its finite menu was chosen. Entrance masses and entrance counts (clause (iii) of the measured theorem) are refined by these shapes; their heights are ranged through finitely many height-forgetting templates and an instantiation bijection.

The Lean statement 
structure EntShapeV (n : ℕ) where
  Phat   : MovesD.Shape n
  hne    : (Phat : MovesD.ShapePrefix).reads ≠ []
  gsel   : ℕ
  musel  : ℕ
  hsel_g : 1 ≤ gsel
  hsel_μ : 1 ≤ musel
  hentry : ∀ R, (Phat : MovesD.ShapePrefix).reads.getLast? = some R →
    R.g = gsel ∧ R.μ = musel
  helig  : ∀ R, (Phat : MovesD.ShapePrefix).reads.getLast? = some R →
    EligPair R gsel musel
  selRec : List ℕ
  hlen   : selRec.length =
    ((Phat : MovesD.ShapePrefix).reads.filter
      (fun R => decide (IsBranchSlot R))).length
  hmenu  : ∀ i (hi : i < selRec.length),
    selRec.get ⟨i, hi⟩ < branchMenuCard n Phat i

lean/LeanUrat/MovesV/Defs.lean — structure

definition The order-0 perimeter certificate for entrance-height points

Born of a soundness emergency: an earlier sorried unscoped totality shim contradicted the compiled countermodel (the environment briefly derived False), so it was deleted and replaced by this named scoping predicate — analogous to the order-0 decided stratum to which the project's main capstone is scoped.

The order-0 perimeter at one slot point: the entrance template's word is a single non-self-loop letter $s$, and every retained slot pair $(h, c)$ is anchor-admissible — $h \geq 1$, $\gcd(e_s, h) = 1$, and the Bezout anchor congruence $e_s \mid s_0 - \mathrm{bez}(e_s, h)\cdot \gamma$ holds. This is exactly the stratum on which the height-writing map is proved total; the unscoped totality is refuted by a compiled countermodel, so every downstream consumer threads this certificate as a hypothesis.

The Lean statement 
def Order0Perimeter {n : ℕ} (εT : EntTemplate n) (h : Hpt εT.entDim) : Prop :=
  ∃ s : MovesSp.Species, εT.word = [s] ∧ SelfLoopLetter n s = false ∧
    ∀ pr ∈ εT.pairSlots h, AnchorAdmissible s pr

lean/LeanUrat/MovesV/V3_spwordD.lean — definition

proved Sums over semilinear height sets split exactly into their disjoint linear components

The summation kernel of the corpus: every aggregate identity (entrance aggregation, total mass one, the composition aggregate) reduces its geometric-series bookkeeping to this exact component-splitting lemma.

Let $S \subseteq \mathbb{N}^D$ be a semilinear set presented as a listed disjoint union of linear components (explicit bases and periods), let $f \geq 0$, and suppose $f$ sums to $v_j$ over the $j$-th component. Then $f$ sums over all of $S$ to $\sum_j v_j$ — exactly, with no overlap terms, because disjointness of the components is part of the presentation type.

The Lean statement 
theorem semilin_sum_exact {D : ℕ} (S : SemilinPart D) (f : Hpt D → ℝ)
    (hf : ∀ h, 0 ≤ f h) (v : Fin S.comps.length → ℝ)
    (hv : ∀ j, HasSum (fun h : {h // (S.comps.get j).Mem h} => f h.1) (v j)) :
    HasSum (fun h : {h // S.Mem h} => f h.1) (∑ j, v j)

lean/LeanUrat/MovesV/V0_partition.lean — theorem

conditional Total mass one: the cell masses at any active state sum to exactly 1

Proved (no axioms beyond Lean's core) from the three typed legs; the exactness equality in the third leg had been dropped in an early draft and was restored after a machine-checked countermodel (the identically-zero mass) refuted the weakened form.

PART-1 of the measured theorem: at any pool point $q_0$ and any state active at $q_0$, for every admissible history, the fixed-height cell masses — summed over the full countable partition of all digit cells of the state and all height points of every dimension — form a convergent sum with value exactly $1$. Never a finite-pool identity: the proof squeezes the countable-additivity upper bound against the null-remainder lower bound.

Hypotheses this rests on: P1CtblAdd (the countable-partition sum exists and is at most 1), P1NullRem (no mass escapes to infinite height), P1FixedHeightExact (below-height blocks plus the height strip sum to exactly 1 at every truncation)

The Lean statement

lean/LeanUrat/MovesV/V4_part1C.lean — theorem

conditional The realized chain count equals a fixed polynomial evaluated at the prime power

Re-keyed at review from the internal table-side count to the note's realized linked count: the exported clause of the capstone now goes through this theorem via the ledger's realization-completeness field.

VAL(b), the chain-count identification: for a template $\gamma$, any admissible history $x$ at a pool point $q_0$, and any height $h$ in the listed process-side domain, the realized linked count $T_\gamma(x, h)$ — the census of marked boxes at the defining level — equals the path-product polynomial of $\gamma$ evaluated at $q_0$. lean/LeanUrat/MovesV/V2_valB.lean — theorem

conditional Composition aggregate: total composite mass = entrance mass times step product

Proved with no axioms beyond Lean's core after the adjudicated lands law closed a genuine leakage (the drafted statement let non-landing templates contribute) and after the soundness repair re-keyed its height-writing dependence onto the order-0 perimeter.

COMP-AGG: for a landing state $\beta_0$ and any template $\gamma$, at a pool point $q_0$, the aggregate of the composite marked-event masses over all entrance templates into $\beta_0$ and all components of their semilinear height domains equals (the total entrance mass into $\beta_0$) $\times$ (the stepwise product value of $\gamma$). Aggregation runs through the exact semilinear component sums, fiber constancy of the entrance data, and the law that non-landing templates carry zero entrance mass at every level.

Hypotheses this rests on: cc (the composite carrier's measure-definition laws, including the adjudicated lands law: non-landing templates carry zero entrance mass), Xs/XsEnt (the geometric-series summability faces), hdom (order-0 perimeter certificates on the entrance domain), hEU (entrance uniformity)

The Lean statement 
theorem comp_agg {n : ℕ} {C : CtsFamily n} {S : StepSys n}

lean/LeanUrat/MovesV/V5_compagg.lean — theorem

proved The cubic root-pattern census over every finite field: five exact polynomial counts

The concrete degree-3 gate census behind the value tables, proved for every finite field (strictly stronger than the nine prime-power spot values checked by kernel computation); one conjunct's keying was repaired after a machine-checked refutation of the drafted version (the count at profile (2,2) over the 3-element field is 6, not 9).

For every finite field $F$ with $|F| = q$, the monic cubics $x^3 + ax^2 + bx + c$ over $F$, classified by root profile (number of distinct roots in $F$, maximal multiplicity), are counted by five fixed polynomials in $q$: three distinct roots, $\tfrac{1}{6}q(q-1)(q-2)$; exactly one simple root (irreducible quadratic cofactor), $\tfrac{1}{2}q^2(q-1)$; no roots (irreducible cubic), $\tfrac{1}{3}(q^3 - q)$; a double plus a simple root, $q(q-1)$; a triple root, $q$. These five classes are exhaustive and sum to $q^3$.

The Lean statement 
theorem rootTable_census_complete (F : Type*) [Field F] [Fintype F]
    [DecidableEq F] (q : ℚ) (hq : (Fintype.card F : ℚ) = q) :
    (classCount F (3, 1) : ℚ) = TH3.eval q ∧
    (classCount F (1, 1) : ℚ) = TH12.eval q ∧
    (classCount F (0, 0) : ℚ) = TH3c.eval q ∧
    (classCount F (2, 2) : ℚ) = T21.eval q ∧
    (classCount F (1, 3) : ℚ) = T3.eval q

lean/LeanUrat/MovesV/V7_ts.lean — theorem

conditional Every coherent well-formed shape prefix is realized by a species word (order-0 scope)

The scoped-true survivor of two rounds of compiled refutation: both extra hypotheses are exactly the laws whose absence the two countermodels exploit, and discharging species coherence from the classifier engine's output laws is a named open item of the next work phase.

On the scoped stratum, the letter alphabet realizes the read grammar: every well-formed shape prefix $P$ (a sequence of reads carrying degrees, heights, and window data) with $n \geq 2$, all continued reads of width $\geq 2$, and satisfying the cross-read species-coherence law — read $r{+}1$ is increment-species exactly when read $r$'s selected move is proper, $2 \leq e_r g_r$ — admits a fitting species word: a word of catalogue letters, one per read, each realizing its read's data, chained by the stage-successor relation. The proof is an explicit letter-by-letter construction.

Hypotheses this rests on: hn + hcont (the width-≥-2 continuation scope: the catalogue's terminal rule forbids selections at window 1), hspec (the cross-read species-coherence law, which the well-formedness predicate does not carry)

    (hcont : ∀ (r : ℕ) (hr : r + 1 < P.reads.length),
      2 ≤ (P.reads[r]'(Nat.lt_of_succ_lt hr)).μ)
    (hspec : SpeciesCoherent P) :

lean/LeanUrat/MovesV/V3_spwordA.lean — theorem

conditional Height-writing is total on the order-0, anchor-admissible stratum

The proved half of the delete-and-scope repair: unrestricted totality is refuted by a compiled countermodel, so this scoped theorem, packaged as the order-0 perimeter certificate (the anchor-admissibility predicate defined above), is what every consumer up to the capstone actually threads.

The height-writing map — which reconstructs a concrete entrance shape from a height-forgetting entrance template and a numeric slot point — succeeds (returns a value) whenever the template's word is a single non-self-loop letter $s$ and every slot pair $(h, c)$ is anchor-admissible: $h \geq 1$, $\gcd(e_s, h) = 1$, and the Bezout anchor congruence $e_s \mid s_0 - \mathrm{bez}(e_s, h)\cdot\gamma$. The reconstruction exhibits an explicit fitting read with exact-division anchor, a well-formedness certificate, and the entrance ties.

Hypotheses this rests on: hword + hsl (the order-0/self-loop-free stratum: the template word is one non-self-loop letter), hadm (anchor admissibility of every slot point — the semilinear conditions the entrance domain is required to own)

The Lean statement

    (s : MovesSp.Species) (hword : εT.word = [s])
    (hsl : SelfLoopLetter n s = false)
    (hadm : ∀ pr ∈ εT.pairSlots h, AnchorAdmissible s pr) :
    (writeHeights? εT h).isSome

lean/LeanUrat/MovesV/V3_spwordD.lean — theorem

proved Countermodel: unrestricted species-word existence is false

The first of the refutation records for the species-word family: it forced the width scoping of the existence theorem, and a second compiled witness (in the round-2 countermodel file) shows even the width-scoped form fails without the species-coherence law.

It is not true that every well-formed shape prefix admits a fitting species word. Compiled witness at $n = 3$: the two-read prefix [root read with $(e, g, \mu) = (1, 1, 1)$; recentering read inside its width-1 window] is machine-checked well-formed, yet the stage laws force the second letter's window to $1$, where the catalogue's terminal rule forbids the selection that the fit clauses demand — so no fitting word exists.

proved Countermodel: unrestricted height-writing totality is false

The countermodel that triggered the corpus's one soundness emergency: an unproved unscoped totality shim coexisted with this compiled negation (so the environment briefly derived False) and was deleted, with every consumer re-keyed onto the order-0 perimeter certificate — all now proved with no axioms beyond Lean's core.

The height-writing map is not total: at the one-letter root template, the all-zero slot point already fails, because every read carries height $\geq 1$ by type; and the countermodel's record notes that positivity alone cannot repair it — a Bezout anchor congruence is needed at every letter with $e \geq 2$, so totality can only hold on a restricted semilinear domain (which became the order-0 perimeter).

The sentence "every entrance shape yields a height-forgetting template" is false: the entrance shape built over the two-read witness prefix admits no fitting species word, so its species word is the junk empty word and the template extraction returns nothing. lean/LeanUrat/MovesV/V7_scsData.lean — theorem

7 Exhaustion: the undecided mass vanishes

Reading only finitely many digits, the classifier decides all but a bounded fraction of polynomials. At degree 2 the vanishing of that fraction is proved outright at every prime. At general degree the bounds are proved in threshold form as implications from named hypotheses — the sharpest unconditional general form is one of the open kernels in the amber panel.

$$\frac{\#\{\text{undecided at level } N\}}{p^{\,nN}} \;\le\; \left(\left\lfloor N/2\right\rfloor + 1\right) p^{-(N-1)} \;\xrightarrow{\,N\to\infty\,}\; 0\quad(\deg 2:\ \text{proved})$$
The exhaustion bounds (MovesX) 6 definition · 7 proved · 7 conditional
Undecided polynomials drain to density zero, with explicit p-uniform envelopes

MovesX is the exhaustiveness ("drainage") layer of the proof, formalizing the source note's Section X-EXHAUST. Working against an audited interface that presents, for each prime p, the canonical branching tree the classifier attaches to every monic degree-n p-adic polynomial, it proves that the fraction of polynomials still undecided at inspection level N tends to zero — qualitatively (the density limit) and quantitatively, with p-uniform envelopes c3·p^(−c4·N) and c3'·p^(−c4'·√N) whose constants depend only on the degree and a fixed constant pack. Drainage then yields the per-splitting-type series identity: summing the content of the countable family of decided trees of a given type recovers exactly that type's density. Every analytic theorem is conditional on named kernel hypotheses (alignment, weight-charge, threshold/cap linearity, progress, bridge, tall-tails, and 'ns-null' — that every non-standard-track fiber has content zero), whose discharge — and the interface's concrete instance — must come from the classifier corpora.

Builds on: Moves, MovesD, MovesT, HC2

definition Nodes of the branching tower

The combinatorial vocabulary of the whole corpus: every depth, height, and index statement is about finite lists of these nodes.

A node of the residual tower for degree $n$ carries letter data $(e, \ell, h, s_0, u_0)$ with $e, h, \ell \ge 1$ and $\gcd(e,h) = 1$: $e$ the ramification jump, $h$ the slope numerator, $\ell$ the residual length, $(s_0, u_0)$ the current base point. A continuing node additionally selects a pair $(g, \mu)$ with $1 \le g$, $1 \le \mu$, $\mu g \le \ell$, and carries an increment-vs-recentering flag; coherence laws force the flag on the $\ell = 1$ rows (increment when $e \ge 2$, recentering when $e = 1$). Histories are finite lists of nodes, classified into rows: T1 ($\ell \ge 2$), T2 ($\ell = 1, e \ge 2, h \ge 2$), T3 ($\ell = 1, e \ge 2, h = 1$), T4 ($\ell = 1, e = 1$), T5 (terminal, no selection).

The Lean statement 
structure XNode (n : ℕ) where
  e : ℕ
  ell : ℕ
  h : ℕ
  s0 : ℕ
  u0 : ℕ
  sel : Option (ℕ × ℕ)
  isIncrement : Bool
  epos : 1 ≤ e
  hpos : 1 ≤ h
  ellpos : 1 ≤ ell
  hcop : Nat.Coprime e h
  selBound : ∀ g μ, sel = some (g, μ) → 1 ≤ g ∧ 1 ≤ μ ∧ μ * g ≤ ell
  t2inc : ell = 1 → 2 ≤ e → 2 ≤ h → sel.isSome → isIncrement = true
  t3inc : ell = 1 → 2 ≤ e → h = 1 → sel.isSome → isIncrement = true
  t4rec : ell = 1 → e = 1 → sel.isSome → isIncrement = false

lean/LeanUrat/MovesX/Defs.lean — structure

definition The space of monic p-adic polynomials and its discriminant valuation

The concrete probability space of the drainage statements: all densities are contents of subsets of this box, keyed by the discriminant valuation.

The carrier is the set of monic degree-$n$ polynomials over $\mathbb{Z}_p$. Its zero-discriminant locus is $\{f : \operatorname{disc} f = 0\}$, and for $f$ in the carrier $v(f) := v_p(\operatorname{disc} f) \in \mathbb{N}$ is the $p$-adic valuation of the discriminant (junk value $0$ on the zero locus, which every statement using $v$ excludes explicitly).

The Lean statement 
def MonicBox (n p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] : Type :=
  { f : Polynomial ℤ_[p] // f.Monic ∧ f.natDegree = n }

def discZero (n p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] : Set (MonicBox n p) := { f | f.1.discr = 0 }

noncomputable def vdisc {n p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] (f : MonicBox n p) : ℕ :=
  (f.1.discr).valuation

lean/LeanUrat/MovesX/Defs.lean — definition

definition The per-prime classifier-tree interface

The audited consumption surface: every analytic theorem of the corpus is proved against this interface, whose real instance (the Okutsu-Montes classifier engine) is owed by the classifier corpora.

For each prime $p$, an audited package presenting: for every polynomial $f$ in the carrier, a rooted, finitely branching tree of branches, each with a node history; a detection theory — per-branch threshold and cap such that leaf branches free of the non-standard track are detected at every level $N \ge \mathrm{thr}(b) + \mathrm{cap}(b)$; the undecided sets $U(N)$, antitone in $N$, with $f \notin U(N)$ iff its tree is finite and every leaf is detected at level $N$; a countable family of non-standard-track fibers covering all ns-leaves; and a normalized, monotone, finitely subadditive content $\mu$ on the carrier that kills countable unions of null sets, satisfies the monotone convergence $\mu(U(N)) \to \mu(\bigcap_N U(N))$, and the cylinder bound $\mu\{f : v_p(\operatorname{disc} f) \ge D \text{ or } \operatorname{disc} f = 0\} \le T(p,n,D)/p^{nD}$, where $T$ is the level-$D$ vanishing-discriminant count. Each field is one note-cited accepted or owed input.

The Lean statement 
structure XCtx (n p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] where
  Branch : MonicBox n p → Type
  hist : ∀ {f}, Branch f → XHistory n
  wf : ∀ {f} (b : Branch f), HistWF n (hist b)
  root : ∀ f, Branch f
  hist_root : ∀ f, hist (root f) = ([] : XHistory n)
  parent : ∀ {f}, Branch f → Option (Branch f)
  parent_hist : ∀ {f} (b b' : Branch f), parent b = some b' → ∃ ν, hist b = hist b' ++ [ν]
  parent_root : ∀ f, parent (root f) = none
  reach : ∀ {f} (b : Branch f),
    Relation.ReflTransGen (fun c d => parent c = some d) b (root f)
  children : ∀ {f}, Branch f → Finset (Branch f)
  children_iff : ∀ {f} (b c : Branch f), c ∈ children b ↔ parent c = some b
  complete : ∀ {f}, Branch f → Prop
  nsTrack : XNode n → Prop
  nsDec : DecidablePred nsTrack
  capDetectable : ∀ {f}, Branch f → Prop
  capDec : ∀ f, DecidablePred (@capDetectable f)
  detCap : ∀ {f}, Branch f → ℕ
  threshold : ∀ {f}, Branch f → ℕ
  DetectedAt : ∀ {f}, Branch f → ℕ → Prop
  detectBranch :
    ∀ {f} (b : Branch f), children b = ∅ → (∀ ν ∈ hist b, ¬ nsTrack ν) →
      ∀ N, threshold b + @ite ℕ (capDetectable b) (capDec f b) (detCap b) 0 ≤ N →
        DetectedAt b N
  Undec : ℕ → Set (MonicBox n p)
  undec_spec :
    ∀ f N, f ∉ Undec N ↔
      (Finite (Branch f) ∧ ∀ b : Branch f, children b = ∅ → DetectedAt b N)
  undec_antitone : Antitone Undec
  nsIdx : Type
  nsCountable : Countable nsIdx
  nsFiber : nsIdx → Set (MonicBox n p)
  nsCover :
    ∀ f, (∃ b : Branch f, children b = ∅ ∧ ∃ ν ∈ hist b, nsTrack ν) → ∃ i, f ∈ nsFiber i
  frac : Set (MonicBox n p) → ℚ
  frac_univ : frac Set.univ = 1
  frac_nonneg : ∀ s, 0 ≤ frac s
  frac_mono : ∀ s t, s ⊆ t → frac s ≤ frac t
  frac_union_le : ∀ s t, frac (s ∪ t) ≤ frac s + frac t
  frac_iUnion_null :
    ∀ (s : ℕ → Set (MonicBox n p)), (∀ i, frac (s i) = 0) → frac (⋃ i, s i) = 0
  frac_inter_tendsto :
    Filter.Tendsto (fun N => frac (Undec N)) Filter.atTop (nhds (frac (⋂ N, Undec N)))
  vdisc_le_tail :
    ∀ D, frac { f | D ≤ vdisc f ∨ f ∈ discZero n p } ≤ (tailCount p n D : ℚ) / p ^ (n * D)

lean/LeanUrat/MovesX/Defs.lean — structure

definition The all-primes family with the published index inequality

The index inequality 2·ind ≤ v_p(disc) is the cited Guardia–Montes–Nart (GMN) theorem of the index; the family carries the corpus's ONE inhabitation obligation, which the classifier corpora must supply — recorded as open.

The corpus's single shared parameter: for every prime $p$, a classifier-tree context as above together with an index interface — an index function and a stratum-membership relation satisfying the published inequality $2\,\mathrm{ind}(f) \le v_p(\operatorname{disc} f)$ off the zero-discriminant locus — linked by the requirement that every $f$ lies in the stratum of each of its own branch histories.

The Lean statement 
structure GMNIndex (n p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] where
  ind : MonicBox n p → ℕ
  inStratum : MonicBox n p → XHistory n → Prop
  p2 : ∀ f, f ∉ discZero n p → 2 * ind f ≤ vdisc f

structure XFamily (n : ℕ) where
  ctx : ∀ (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime], XCtx n p
  gmn : ∀ (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime], GMNIndex n p
  gmnLink :
    ∀ (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (f : MonicBox n p) (b : (ctx p).Branch f),
      (gmn p).inStratum f ((ctx p).hist b)

lean/LeanUrat/MovesX/Defs.lean — structure

definition The named kernel hypotheses everything is conditional on

These named propositions (the full list: alignment, weight-charge T4/full, affine threshold, cap, progress, tall-tails, bridge, ns-null, route) are exactly the gates of every conditional theorem below; they must be discharged by the classifier corpora.

The corpus's conditional inputs, stated once as named propositions about the shared family: alignment (the certified depth of any stratum is at most the index, off $\operatorname{disc}=0$); weight-charge (recentering and T4 step counts are at most $s(n)\, v_p(\operatorname{disc} f)$); threshold and cap linearity in the total height $\sum_r h_r$; progress (branches undetected at level $N$ are deep or tall at linear rates); tall-tails ($\mu(\text{some node of height} \ge h^*) \le C_T\, p^{-c_T h^*}$); ns-null (every non-standard-track fiber has content zero); the route disjunction combining alignment and weight-charge in two admissible ways; and the bridge, displayed here: at every level $N$ the undecided set is contained in (disc-zero) $\cup$ (ns-fibers) $\cup$ (infinite-tree locus) $\cup$ (leaf, ns-free witness branches with $N < \mathrm{thr}(b)+\mathrm{cap}(b)$), and the undecided mass carrying no such witness is zero.

The Lean statement 
def X2BridgeP (n : ℕ) (X : XFamily n) : Prop :=
  ∀ (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (N : ℕ),
    ((X.ctx p).Undec N ⊆ discZero n p ∪ (⋃ i, (X.ctx p).nsFiber i) ∪ InfTree (X.ctx p) ∪
      { f | ∃ b : (X.ctx p).Branch f,
          IsLeafB (X.ctx p) b ∧ NsFreeB (X.ctx p) b ∧
            N < (X.ctx p).threshold b + capHB (X.ctx p) b }) ∧
    (X.ctx p).frac { f | f ∈ (X.ctx p).Undec N ∧
      ¬ ∃ b : (X.ctx p).Branch f,
          IsLeafB (X.ctx p) b ∧ NsFreeB (X.ctx p) b ∧
            N < (X.ctx p).threshold b + capHB (X.ctx p) b } = 0

def X3aRouteP (n : ℕ) (X : XFamily n) (K : XConsts n) : Prop :=
  (X1aAlignP n X ⟨true, true⟩  ∧ WeightChargeT4P n X K) ∨
  (X1aAlignP n X ⟨true, false⟩ ∧ WeightChargeFullP n X K)

lean/LeanUrat/MovesX/Defs.lean — definition

definition Decided-tree series data and the density of a splitting type

Defines what 'the density of a splitting type' means and packages the countable decided-tree family that the series identity sums over; the concrete instance is owed to the classifier corpora.

A per-prime series presentation: a countable family of complete realizable finite trees, each carrying a fiber (a set of polynomials) and a letter-computed splitting type, together with the true splitting type of each polynomial. Fibers are pairwise disjoint, disjoint from the never-decided set $\bigcap_N U(N)$, and cover every decided polynomial off the zero-discriminant locus. The density of a splitting type $\sigma$ is defined as the content of its true-type event: $\mathrm{density}(\sigma) := \mu\{f : \mathrm{type}(f) = \sigma\}$.

The Lean statement 
structure SeriesData (n p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (C : XCtx n p) where
  FinTree : Type
  treeCountable : Countable FinTree
  fiber : FinTree → Set (MonicBox n p)
  typemult : FinTree → SplitType n
  splitType : MonicBox n p → SplitType n
  fiber_disjoint : ∀ T T', T ≠ T' → Disjoint (fiber T) (fiber T')
  fiber_decided : ∀ T, Disjoint (fiber T) (⋂ N, C.Undec N)
  decided_covered : ∀ f, f ∉ (⋂ N, C.Undec N) → f ∉ discZero n p → ∃ T, f ∈ fiber T

def densityOf (S : SeriesData n p C) (σ : SplitType n) : ℚ :=
  C.frac { f | S.splitType f = σ }

lean/LeanUrat/MovesX/Defs.lean — structure

proved The index column counts lattice points

Checks the index column of the row table against Newton-polygon lattice geometry; the 0 on rows T3/T4 records 'no positive per-side bound from the table', never 'the actual index is 0', and the T1/T2 positivity is what makes certified steps consume index in the depth bound.

For every continuing node $\nu$, the doubled table index — $\ell(eh\ell + 1 - e - h)$ on row T1, $(e-1)(h-1)$ on row T2, $0$ on the other rows — equals twice the number of lattice points $(x,y)$ with $s_0 < x \le s_0 + e\ell$, $u_0 < y \le u_0 + h\ell$, and $h(x - s_0) + e(y - u_0) \le eh\ell$ (strictly right/above the base point, on or below the Newton-polygon segment); moreover on rows T1 and T2 the doubled index is at least $1$.

The Lean statement 
theorem ind2Region {n : ℕ} (ν : XNode n) (hc : ν.continuing) :
    ind2 ν = 2 * ν.region.card ∧ ((rowOf ν = .T1 ∨ rowOf ν = .T2) → 1 ≤ ind2 ν)

lean/LeanUrat/MovesX/XA9.lean — theorem

conditional Tower depth is linearly bounded by the discriminant valuation

The engine of finiteness: certified tower depth is linear in the discriminant valuation, so trees over polynomials of bounded disc-valuation cannot be deep.

Assume the alignment hypothesis (increments certified, recenterings not) and the full weight-charge hypothesis. If $f$ has nonzero discriminant and lies in a well-formed stratum $H$, then $2\, d_{\mathrm{total}}(H) \le (2s+1)\, v_p(\operatorname{disc} f) + 2 \lfloor \log_2 n \rfloor$, where $d_{\mathrm{total}}(H)$ counts the continuing nodes of $H$ and $s = s(n)$ is the weight-charge constant.

Hypotheses this rests on: X1aAlignP ⟨true,false⟩ — the certified depth of any stratum is at most the GMN index (T1/T2 increments certified, recenterings not); WeightChargeFullP — #T1-recenterings + #T4 steps ≤ s(n)·v_p(disc f). Both owed to the classifier corpora.

The Lean statement 
theorem x1bReduction {n : ℕ} (X : XFamily n) (K : XConsts n)
    (AL : X1aAlignP n X ⟨true, false⟩) (WC : WeightChargeFullP n X K)
    (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (f : MonicBox n p) (H : XHistory n)
    (hf : (X.gmn p).inStratum f H) (hd : f ∉ discZero n p) (W : HistWF n H) (hn : 1 ≤ n) :
    2 * dTotal H ≤ (2 * K.s + 1) * vdisc f + 2 * Nat.log 2 n

lean/LeanUrat/MovesX/XC3.lean — theorem

conditional Undetected branches are deep or tall at rate square-root-of-N

Both prices are paid in the one currency Σ h_r; this dichotomy is the driver of the square-root fallback envelope.

Assume the affine threshold and cap hypotheses. If a fourth-piece branch $b$ (leaf-terminated or a prefix of a complete branch, and free of the non-standard track) is undetected at level $N$, i.e. $N < \mathrm{thr}(b) + \mathrm{cap}(b)$, then $N - 1 - c_{\mathrm{cap}} < (c_0 + c_{\mathrm{cap}})(d_{\mathrm{total}} + 1)^2$ or $N - 1 - c_{\mathrm{cap}} < (c_0 + c_{\mathrm{cap}})(\max_r h_r)^2$: escaping detection at level $N$ forces depth or height of order $\sqrt N$.

Hypotheses this rests on: X2AffP — the detection threshold of a fourth-piece branch is ≤ 1 + c₀·Σ h_r; X2CapP — its detection cap is ≤ c_cap·(1 + Σ h_r). Both open, to be discharged by the classifier corpora.

The Lean statement 
theorem x2hypAssembled {n : ℕ} (X : XFamily n) (K : XConsts n)
    (AF : X2AffP n X K) (CP : X2CapP n X K)
    (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (f : MonicBox n p) (b : (X.ctx p).Branch f)
    (hb : FourthPieceB (X.ctx p) b) (N : ℕ)
    (hN : N < (X.ctx p).threshold b + capHB (X.ctx p) b) :
    (N : ℚ) - 1 - K.ccap < (K.c0 + K.ccap) * ((dTotal ((X.ctx p).hist b) : ℚ) + 1) ^ 2 ∨
    (N : ℚ) - 1 - K.ccap < (K.c0 + K.ccap) * (maxH ((X.ctx p).hist b) : ℚ) ^ 2

lean/LeanUrat/MovesX/XD4.lean — theorem

proved A norm-compatible splitting extension exists

An existence witness (a definition whose fields carry the proof obligations) supplying the valued splitting extension the covering argument needs; by design it appears in no public statement and is discharged inside the tail bound's proof.

Every nonzero polynomial $g$ over $\mathbb{Q}_p$ admits a field extension $L / \mathbb{Q}_p$ carrying a multiplicative absolute value that extends the $p$-adic norm and over which $g$ splits into linear factors — constructed as the splitting field of $g$ equipped with the spectral norm.

The Lean statement 
noncomputable def valExtOf (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (g : Polynomial ℚ_[p]) (hg : g ≠ 0) :
    ValExt p g

lean/LeanUrat/MovesX/XF3.lean — definition

proved The discriminant event on a coefficient line is covered by n−1 small balls

The geometric heart of the tail bound (the valued-extension argument is supplied by the norm-extension construction, so nothing here is owed); an earlier draft proof failed independent re-verification (a deterministic kernel timeout) and was rebuilt — the final proof uses no axioms beyond Lean's core.

Fix $n \ge 2$, a level $D$, and tail coefficients $a_1, \dots, a_{n-1} \in \mathbb{Z}_p$. There is a labeling of $\mathbb{Z}_p$ by at most $n - 1$ labels such that any two constant terms $a_0, b_0$ for which $p^D$ divides $\operatorname{disc}(X^n + a_{n-1}X^{n-1} + \cdots + a_1 X + a_0)$ (resp. with $b_0$), and which receive the same label, satisfy $a_0 \equiv b_0 \pmod{p^E}$ with $E = \lceil (D - n\, v_p(n))/(n-1) \rceil$. Equivalently: on each constant-term line the level-$D$ discriminant event is covered by at most $n-1$ balls of radius $p^{-E}$, clustered near the critical points of the polynomial.

The Lean statement 
theorem ballCover (p n D : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (h2 : 2 ≤ n) (aTail : Fin (n - 1) → ℤ_[p])
    (V : ValExt p (fTail n p (by omega) aTail)) :
    ∃ idx : ℤ_[p] → Fin (n - 1), ∀ a0 b0,
      Event p n D (by omega) aTail a0 → Event p n D (by omega) aTail b0 →
      idx a0 = idx b0 → (p : ℤ_[p]) ^ (tailExp p n D).toNat ∣ (a0 - b0)

lean/LeanUrat/MovesX/XF6.lean — theorem

proved Counting bound for high discriminant divisibility

The corpus's fully concrete analytic estimate, proved over ℤ_p with no interface hypotheses at all; through the interface's cylinder-bound field it feeds every measure statement downstream.

Unconditionally: the number $T(p,n,D)$ of coefficient tuples in $(\mathbb{Z}/p^D)^n$ whose monic degree-$n$ polynomial has discriminant $\equiv 0 \bmod p^D$ satisfies $T(p,n,D)\, p^{E} \le (n-1)\, p^{nD}$, where $E = \lceil (D - n\, v_p(n))/(n-1) \rceil$. Equivalently, the fraction of level-$D$ coefficient boxes with $p^D \mid \operatorname{disc}$ is at most $(n-1)\, p^{-\lceil (D - n v_p(n))/(n-1) \rceil}$ — the note's display (3b).

The Lean statement 
theorem tailCountBound (p n D : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (h2 : 2 ≤ n) :
    (tailCount p n D : ℚ) * (p : ℚ) ^ (tailExp p n D) ≤
      (n - 1 : ℚ) * (p : ℚ) ^ ((n * D : ℕ) : ℤ)

lean/LeanUrat/MovesX/XF7.lean — theorem

proved The zero-discriminant locus has density zero

The polynomials on which the classifier can never halt carry no density, so drainage need only be asserted off a null set; proved for every context satisfying the interface (whose concrete instance is owed), by squeezing the interface's cylinder bound against the unconditional tail count.

For every degree $n \ge 2$, every prime $p$, and every context satisfying the audited interface, the set of monic degree-$n$ polynomials over $\mathbb{Z}_p$ with discriminant exactly zero has content zero: $\mu\{f : \operatorname{disc} f = 0\} = 0$.

The Lean statement 
theorem discZeroNull (n p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (h2 : 2 ≤ n) (C : XCtx n p) :
    C.frac (discZero n p) = 0

lean/LeanUrat/MovesX/XF10.lean — theorem

proved Koenig's lemma for the classifier tree

Pure interface combinatorics upgrading 'every branch is finite' to 'the whole tree is finite'; no kernel hypotheses consumed.

For any context and any polynomial $f$: if the rooted, finitely branching tree of branches of $f$ admits no infinite root-descending path (no sequence $g$ with $g_0$ the root and each $g_{k+1}$ a child of $g_k$), then $f$ has only finitely many branches — Koenig's lemma in contrapositive form, with finite branching supplied by the interface's finite child menus.

The Lean statement 
theorem koenigFinite {n p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] (C : XCtx n p) (f : MonicBox n p)
    (hbranch : ¬ ∃ g : ℕ → C.Branch f,
      g 0 = C.root f ∧ ∀ k, C.parent (g (k + 1)) = some (g k)) :
    Finite (C.Branch f)

lean/LeanUrat/MovesX/XG2.lean — theorem

conditional Off the discriminant locus, every classifier tree is finite

The pointwise exhaustiveness statement X.3(a): off the null discriminant locus every classifier tree halts.

Assume the route hypothesis (alignment plus weight-charge, in either admissible combination). Then for every prime $p$ and every monic degree-$n$ polynomial $f$ with nonzero discriminant, the canonical tree of $f$ is finite: an infinite path would produce strata of unbounded certified depth, contradicting the linear bound $2\, d_{\mathrm{total}} \le (2s+1) v_p(\operatorname{disc} f) + 2\lfloor \log_2 n \rfloor$; Koenig's lemma then closes the tree.

Hypotheses this rests on: X3aRouteP — either (alignment with recenterings certified + T4-only weight-charge) or (alignment without recenterings + full weight-charge); owed to the classifier corpora.

The Lean statement 
theorem treeFinite {n : ℕ} (X : XFamily n) (K : XConsts n) (R : X3aRouteP n X K)
    (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hn : 1 ≤ n) (f : MonicBox n p) (hd : f ∉ discZero n p) :
    Finite ((X.ctx p).Branch f)

lean/LeanUrat/MovesX/XG2d.lean — theorem

conditional Exponential envelope for the undecided fraction

The strongest quantitative drainage claim, restated after its original all-N form was refuted by a compiled countermodel: the threshold N₀ and constants are now bound before the prime, giving p-uniform exponential decay only above N₀.

For each degree $n \ge 2$ and each constant pack $K$ there exist a level $N_0$ and constants $c_3, c_4 > 0$, all depending only on $(n, K)$ — in particular bound before the prime — such that for every family satisfying the nine named kernel hypotheses, every prime $p$, and every $N \ge N_0$: $\mu(U(N)) \le c_3\, p^{-c_4 N}$.

Hypotheses this rests on: X2ProgressP — undetected fourth-piece branches are deep or tall at linear rates in N; X2BridgeP — Undec(N) splits into disc-zero / ns-fiber / infinite-tree / witness-branch pieces with the witness-free remainder of content zero; X2AffP and X2CapP — detection threshold and cap linear in Σ h_r; X1aAlignP ⟨true,false⟩ and WeightChargeFullP — depth charged against v_p(disc); X2TailsP — content of the height-≥h* event ≤ C_T·p^(−c_T·h*); NsNullP — ns fibers null; X3aRouteP — the route disjunction. All owed to the classifier corpora.

The Lean statement 
theorem envelopeExp (n : ℕ) (hn : 2 ≤ n) (K : XConsts n) :
    ∃ N₀ : ℕ, ∃ c3 c4 : ℝ, 0 < c3 ∧ 0 < c4 ∧
      ∀ (X : XFamily n), X2ProgressP n X K → X2BridgeP n X → X2AffP n X K → X2CapP n X K →
        X1aAlignP n X ⟨true, false⟩ → WeightChargeFullP n X K →
        X2TailsP n X K → NsNullP n X → X3aRouteP n X K →
        ∀ (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime], ∀ N, N₀ ≤ N →
          ((X.ctx p).frac ((X.ctx p).Undec N) : ℝ) ≤ c3 * (p : ℝ) ^ (-(c4 * N))

lean/LeanUrat/MovesX/XE2.lean — theorem

conditional Square-root fallback envelope for the undecided fraction

The fallback route that trades the hardest hypothesis (linear progress) for a weaker p^(−c√N) rate, restated after the same refutation as the exponential envelope.

Without the progress hypothesis: for each $n \ge 2$ and constant pack $K$ there exist $N_0$ and $c_3', c_4' > 0$ depending only on $(n, K)$ such that for every family satisfying the remaining eight kernel hypotheses, every prime $p$, and every $N \ge N_0$: $\mu(U(N)) \le c_3'\, p^{-c_4' \sqrt{N}}$ — subexponential decay, enough for the qualitative drainage but not for squeeze constants.

Hypotheses this rests on: Same hypothesis list as the exponential envelope minus X2ProgressP: X2BridgeP, X2AffP, X2CapP, X1aAlignP ⟨true,false⟩, WeightChargeFullP, X2TailsP, NsNullP, X3aRouteP; the square-root dichotomy replaces the linear progress hypothesis. All open, to be discharged by the classifier corpora.

The Lean statement 
theorem envelopeSqrt (n : ℕ) (hn : 2 ≤ n) (K : XConsts n) :
    ∃ N₀ : ℕ, ∃ c3' c4' : ℝ, 0 < c3' ∧ 0 < c4' ∧
      ∀ (X : XFamily n), X2BridgeP n X → X2AffP n X K → X2CapP n X K →
        X1aAlignP n X ⟨true, false⟩ → WeightChargeFullP n X K →
        X2TailsP n X K → NsNullP n X → X3aRouteP n X K →
        ∀ (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime], ∀ N, N₀ ≤ N →
          ((X.ctx p).frac ((X.ctx p).Undec N) : ℝ) ≤ c3' * (p : ℝ) ^ (-(c4' * Real.sqrt N))

lean/LeanUrat/MovesX/XE3.lean — theorem

conditional The undecided fraction tends to zero (drainage)

THE drainage capstone (the note's X.3): the exhaustiveness leg that the project's full density theorem still needs, obtained by squeezing the never-decided set inside the null discriminant locus and the null ns-fibers.

For every degree $n \ge 2$, every family satisfying the route and ns-null hypotheses, and every prime $p$: $\mu(U(N)) \to 0$ as $N \to \infty$ — the classifier eventually decides asymptotically every monic degree-$n$ polynomial over $\mathbb{Z}_p$.

Hypotheses this rests on: X3aRouteP — the alignment/weight-charge route disjunction; NsNullP — every non-standard-track fiber has content zero. Both owed to the classifier corpora, as is the family's real instance.

The Lean statement 
theorem x3Density {n : ℕ} (X : XFamily n) (K : XConsts n) (hn : 2 ≤ n)
    (R : X3aRouteP n X K) (NS : NsNullP n X) (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] :
    Filter.Tendsto (fun N => (X.ctx p).frac ((X.ctx p).Undec N)) Filter.atTop (nhds 0)

lean/LeanUrat/MovesX/XG3.lean — theorem

conditional The tree series computes each splitting-type density

The corpus's final output: the bridge from tree-by-tree computation to the exact density of every splitting type — what the uniformity theorem's rational function ultimately evaluates.

Under the route and ns-null hypotheses plus type-soundness and countable fiber additivity: for each splitting type $\sigma$, the countable family $\big(\mu(\text{fiber of } T)\big)$, indexed by the decided trees $T$ whose letter-computed type is $\sigma$, sums (unconditionally, as a convergent sum) to exactly $\mathrm{density}(\sigma) = \mu\{f : \mathrm{type}(f) = \sigma\}$.

Hypotheses this rests on: X3aRouteP — route disjunction; NsNullP — ns fibers null; VPSoundP — membership in a tree fiber pins the true splitting type to the letter-computed one; CountableFiberAdditive — the content is countably additive over the disjoint tree-fiber family (the Tonelli positive-term regrouping, owed); plus the series presentation itself, whose instance is owed.

The Lean statement 
theorem x3Series {n : ℕ} (X : XFamily n) (K : XConsts n) (hn : 2 ≤ n)
    (R : X3aRouteP n X K) (NS : NsNullP n X)
    (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (S : SeriesData n p (X.ctx p))
    (VP : VPSoundP S) (ADD : CountableFiberAdditive S) (σ : SplitType n) :
    HasSum (fun T : { T // S.typemult T = σ } => (X.ctx p).frac (S.fiber T))
      (densityOf S σ)

lean/LeanUrat/MovesX/XG4.lean — theorem

proved Compiled countermodel: the original all-levels envelope was false

The refutation record of the corpus: this machine-checked refutation (2026-07-27) forced the restatement of both envelope theorems to bind a threshold N₀ before the prime; the square-root analogue is refuted the same way in lean/notes/XE3_REFUTATION_2026-07-27.lean, and the adjudication is recorded in the blueprint's refutation section.

The originally stated form of the exponential envelope — constants allowed to depend on the family, with the bound asserted at every level $N$ — is false: a point-mass content at $f_0 = X^2 + X$ (whose discriminant is $1$, a unit at every prime) with the root-only tree and threshold $+$ cap $= 2$ satisfies all nine kernel hypotheses, yet the undecided mass at level $N = 1$ equals $1$ for every prime, contradicting $c_3\, p^{-c_4} \to 0$ as $p \to \infty$. The file derives False from the verbatim universal closure of the old statement.

The Lean statement 
theorem envelopeExp_statement_false :
    ¬ (∀ (n : ℕ) (hn : 2 ≤ n) (X : XFamily n) (K : XConsts n),
        X2ProgressP n X K → X2BridgeP n X → X2AffP n X K → X2CapP n X K →

        X2TailsP n X K → NsNullP n X → X3aRouteP n X K →

lean/notes/XE2_refutation_witness.lean — countermodel

8 The squeeze: Theorem U

From a per-prime instance bundle (decided counts, vanishing envelope, regularity data, a coefficient-class bridge), the capstone proves: at every prime passing the two-clause regularity gate, each splitting type's density exists, equals the candidate rational function's value, and does so in three provably equivalent readings, with the candidates summing to 1. This is a machine-checked implication whose axiom footprint is exactly Lean's core; what feeds it — including thirteen typed placeholder slots that currently impose no constraint — is itemized in the amber panel.

$$\bigl(R_\sigma(p) - \varepsilon_N\bigr)\, p^{\,nN} \;\le\; D_\sigma(N) \;\le\; R_\sigma(p)\, p^{\,nN}$$
Theorem U: the capstone (MovesU) 12 definition · 3 proved · 5 conditional
Assembles Theorem U: one rational function per splitting type, every prime

This corpus states and proves the project's capstone, Theorem U: for each splitting type σ of degree n there is one fixed rational function R_σ(q), with Σ_σ R_σ = 1 identically, such that at every prime p passing a typed regularity gate — wildly ramified primes included — the density of monic degree-n p-adic polynomials of factorization type σ exists and equals the literal value R_σ(p). The proof is a squeeze: an exact partition of the level-N coefficient box, a two-sided bracket whose upper leg comes from a positive tree-fiber series and whose lower leg comes from the checksum, and a vanishing undecided envelope. The theorem is machine-checked (no axioms beyond Lean's core, no unproved goals in its own proof) but conditional: its hypothesis surface is a fully typed ledger of named premises tied to the other corpora's real objects (the tree-count and solve interfaces, identifications of the abstract data with the real block system and the real tree corpus, a p-adic reading bridge), plus 13 explicitly untyped bare assumptions recorded as awaiting their carriers — and no per-prime instantiation bundle is constructed anywhere in the repository. MovesU consumes every other corpus and is imported by none — it is the top of the pipeline.

Builds on: MovesS, MovesT, MovesX, MovesV, MovesRBase, MovesD, MovesC

conditional Theorem U: one rational function computes every p-adic splitting density (conditional capstone)

The capstone of the whole project — machine-checked with no unproved goals and no axioms beyond Lean's core (verified in the corpus record) — but conditional: no instance of the per-prime bundle exists in the repository, and its own docstring records that the 13 bare parameters await their carriers; the named premises ARE the measured distance to an unconditional theorem.

Fix $n \ge 2$. Assume the full typed hypothesis ledger: the carrier pack of the built resolvent-solve corpus, the upstream kernel carriers with their typed rows, the prime-uniform finiteness kernel, thirteen named auxiliary assumptions, and — for every prime $p$ — a per-prime instantiation bundle. Then there exists one family of rational functions $R_\sigma \in \mathbb{Q}(q)$, indexed by the splitting types $\sigma$ of degree $n$ and identified with the solver's own output family, with $\sum_\sigma R_\sigma = 1$ identically in $q$, such that at every prime $p$ passing the regularity gate (REG-$p$): (U-n) for all $\sigma$ and all levels $N$, $(R_\sigma(p) - \mathrm{env}(N))\,p^{nN} \le \#\{\text{level-}N\text{ classes decided }\sigma\} \le R_\sigma(p)\,p^{nN}$, where $\mathrm{env}(N)$ is the undecided fraction; (i) the decided density $\#\{\text{decided }\sigma\}/p^{nN}$ converges to $R_\sigma(p)$; (ii) the true-factorization-type density converges to the same limit; (ii-$\mathbb{Z}_p$) the $\mathbb{Z}_p$-read density — the fraction of level-$N$ classes all of whose monic degree-$n$ lifts to $\mathbb{Z}_p$ factor over $\mathbb{Q}_p$ with shape $\sigma$, each pair read as (ramification index, residue degree) of the factor — converges to the same limit; and (iii) $\mathrm{env}(N) \to 0$. At a prime failing the gate, nothing is asserted.

Hypotheses this rests on: C (the built resolvent-solve corpus's carrier pack: table, measured side, rational burdens, degree conservation, K-matrix hypotheses, shape family, measured chain); KC (the built MovesX/MovesV/MovesRBase carrier corpora); KT (five numbered upstream counting laws of the source note — its rows CL-2/CL-3/CL-8/CL-13/CL-15 — stated at those carriers); K7 (the prime-uniform finiteness kernel with its counting laws); rel1, rel2a, rel2b, rel2d, rel2e, rel3, rs0Lump, trackRule, dnLattice, m1m5Echo, x1aDict, m4bConst, jcInvHist (13 named bare assumptions — untyped and trivially instantiable per the adjudicated audit record: the vocabulary needed to type them is not yet built, so they impose no mathematical obligation yet); inst (for EVERY prime, a per-prime bundle: the ledger with its tree and solve seams, roster pin, tree pin plus sibling/junction counting rows, p-adic bridge with stated residue, and slice bound — constructed nowhere in the repository); per-prime RegP (the (REG-p) regularity gate, gating each prime's conclusion).

The Lean statement 
theorem theoremU (n : ℕ) (hn : 2 ≤ n) (C : UCarriers n) (KC : KernelCarriers n C)
    (K7 : Cl7Kernel n KC) (S : SolveData n)
    (KT : UpstreamTyped n KC)
    (rel1 rel2a rel2b rel2d rel2e rel3 : Prop)
    (rs0Lump trackRule dnLattice : Prop)
    (m1m5Echo x1aDict m4bConst jcInvHist : Prop)
    (hrel1 : rel1) (hrel2a : rel2a) (hrel2b : rel2b) (hrel2d : rel2d)
    (hrel2e : rel2e) (hrel3 : rel3)
    (hrs0 : rs0Lump) (htrk : trackRule) (hdn : dnLattice)
    (hm15 : m1m5Echo) (hx1a : x1aDict) (hm4b : m4bConst) (hjc : jcInvHist)
    (inst : ∀ (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime), UInstance n C KC K7 S p hp) :
    ∃ R : SplittingType n → RatFunc ℚ,
      R = S.R ∧
      (∑ σ, R σ = 1) ∧
      ∀ (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime), RegP (inst p hp).D →
        (∀ (σ : SplittingType n) (N : ℕ),
          (evalℝ ⟨R⟩ σ p - (inst p hp).X.env N) * (p : ℝ) ^ (n * N)
              ≤ ((inst p hp).X.decided σ N : ℝ) ∧
            ((inst p hp).X.decided σ N : ℝ)
              ≤ evalℝ ⟨R⟩ σ p * (p : ℝ) ^ (n * N))
        ∧ (∀ σ : SplittingType n,
          Tendsto ((inst p hp).X.dmass σ) atTop (𝓝 (evalℝ ⟨R⟩ σ p)))
        ∧ (∀ σ : SplittingType n,
          Tendsto ((inst p hp).X.trueDmass σ) atTop (𝓝 (evalℝ ⟨R⟩ σ p)))
        ∧ (∀ σ : SplittingType n,
          Tendsto (@ZpBridge.zpDmass n p ⟨hp⟩ _ (inst p hp).bridge σ) atTop
            (𝓝 (evalℝ ⟨R⟩ σ p)))
        ∧ Tendsto (inst p hp).X.env atTop (𝓝 0)

lean/LeanUrat/MovesU/U10_theoremU.lean — theorem

definition Splitting types of degree n

The index of the entire theorem: densities, rational functions, and classifier verdicts are all keyed by these; finiteness (proved in U0b) is what makes the checksum sum well-defined.

A splitting type of degree $n$ is a multiset of pairs $(e_i, f_i)$ with $e_i, f_i \ge 1$ and $\sum_i e_i f_i = n$ — the shape of a factorization of a degree-$n$ polynomial into irreducible factors with ramification indices $e_i$ and residue degrees $f_i$. A companion unit proves there are finitely many splitting types for each $n$.

The Lean statement 
def SplittingType (n : ℕ) : Type :=
  {σ : Multiset (ℕ × ℕ) // (∀ x ∈ σ, 1 ≤ x.1 ∧ 1 ≤ x.2) ∧
    (σ.map fun x => x.1 * x.2).sum = n}

lean/LeanUrat/MovesU/Defs.lean — definition

definition The classifier interface: verdicts on coefficient boxes

The vocabulary every density statement is typed in; soundness of verdicts is deliberately NOT an interface axiom but a separately carried hypothesis (an audit-driven design choice), and the interface is later identified with the built tree corpus by the tree pin.

The classifier at a prime $p$: for each level $N$, a verdict map assigning to every class of the level-$N$ box (the $n$ free coefficients of a monic degree-$n$ polynomial mod $p^N$, giving $p^{nN}$ classes) either a splitting type or 'undecided'; a true-type field on the same classes; a stability law — a verdict made at level $N$ persists at every higher level; and a declared digit-section convention. From the verdict map the corpus defines $\mathrm{decided}_\sigma(N)$, $\mathrm{undec}(N)$, the envelope $\mathrm{env}(N) = \mathrm{undec}(N)/p^{nN}$, and the decided and true-type densities.

The Lean statement 
structure ClassifierSpec (n p : ℕ) where
  canonical : ∀ N : ℕ, Box p n N → Option (SplittingType n)
  trueType : ∀ N : ℕ, Box p n N → Option (SplittingType n)
  canonical_stable : ∀ {N N'} (h : N ≤ N') (f : Box p n N') (σ : SplittingType n),
    canonical N (boxProj p n h f) = some σ → canonical N' f = some σ
  baseSection : BaseSection

lean/LeanUrat/MovesU/Defs.lean — structure

definition The regularity gate (REG-p)

The typed per-prime gate in the theorem's quantifier: the conclusion is asserted exactly at primes passing it and nothing is claimed otherwise — this is how one fixed rational function can cover wild primes without overclaiming.

(REG-$p$): for every value $q_0$ in the pool $Q(p) = \{p^\delta : \delta \in \text{depth set}\}$ and every block $e$ of the block system: (r1) the determinant $\det(I - K_e)$ is defined and nonzero at $q_0$; and (r2) every member of the displayed entry list $E(e)$ — the entries of $K_e$, the terminal and split vectors, the per-cell branching entries, the injection entries, the shallow-shape weights, and the $\beta$-legs at every depth — is defined at $q_0$ and its literal value equals the prescribed active value. This is exactly the note's (r1) ∧ (r2) schema; definedness and agreement of $R_\sigma$ itself are derived consequences, not extra conjuncts.

The Lean statement 
def RegP {p : ℕ} (D : RegData p) : Prop :=
  ∀ q₀ ∈ D.Pool, ∀ e : D.Block,
    (letI := D.instBi e; letI := D.instBd e;
     DefinedAt (Matrix.det (1 - D.K e)) (q₀ : ℚ) ∧
     (Matrix.det (1 - D.K e)).eval (RingHom.id ℚ) (q₀ : ℚ) ≠ 0) ∧
    ∀ g ∈ D.entryList e,
      DefinedAt g (q₀ : ℚ) ∧ g.eval (RingHom.id ℚ) (q₀ : ℚ) = D.act g q₀

lean/LeanUrat/MovesU/Defs.lean — definition

definition The canonical-tree fiber series

The positive series whose threshold-N partial sums ARE the decided counts — the route to the upper bracket leg; its sum is later tied to the measured solver value by the solve seam.

For each splitting type $\sigma$: an index family of canonical trees, each carrying a mass $\mu(\mathrm{fiber}\,T) \in [0,\infty]$ and a decision threshold, together with the finite threshold-$\le N$ slices (characterized, not chosen). The full series $\sum_T \mu(\mathrm{fiber}\,T)$ exists unconditionally as an extended real; every partial sum over a slice is dominated by it (the Tonelli unit).

The Lean statement 
structure FiberSeries (n p : ℕ) (X : ClassifierSpec n p) where
  Tree : SplittingType n → Type
  instTreeDeq : ∀ σ, DecidableEq (Tree σ)
  mass : ∀ σ, Tree σ → ℝ≥0∞
  thr : ∀ σ, Tree σ → ℕ
  thrSlice : ∀ σ : SplittingType n, ℕ → Finset (Tree σ)
  mem_slice_iff : ∀ σ N (T : Tree σ), T ∈ thrSlice σ N ↔ thr σ T ≤ N
  slice_exhausts : ∀ σ (T : Tree σ), T ∈ thrSlice σ (thr σ T)

lean/LeanUrat/MovesU/Defs.lean — structure

definition The tree-count seam: exact fiber counts per canonical tree (named open premise)

The distance to unconditionality on the tree side: the count tie is the open premise, with the built tree-expansion theorem of the MovesT corpus recorded as its named discharge candidate through the tree pin.

The named open TREE-N premises: (a) every level-$N$ class decided as $\sigma$ is assigned a canonical tree of type $\sigma$; (b) the assigned tree has decision threshold $\le N$; and (c) the exact count tie — for each tree $T$ of threshold $\le N$, $\mu(\mathrm{fiber}\,T)\cdot p^{nN}$ equals the exact number of decided-$\sigma$ classes assigned $T$.

The Lean statement 
structure TreeSeam (n p : ℕ) (X : ClassifierSpec n p) (F : FiberSeries n p X) where
  treeOf : ∀ (σ : SplittingType n) (N : ℕ)
    (f : {f : Box p n N // X.canonical N f = some σ}), F.Tree σ
  thr_le : ∀ σ N f, F.thr σ (treeOf σ N f) ≤ N
  count_tie : ∀ (σ : SplittingType n) (N : ℕ) (T : F.Tree σ), F.thr σ T ≤ N →
    F.mass σ T * (p : ℝ≥0∞) ^ (n * N)
      = (Nat.card {f : {f : Box p n N // X.canonical N f = some σ} //
          treeOf σ N f = T} : ℝ≥0∞)

lean/LeanUrat/MovesU/DefsLedger.lean — structure

conditional Decided counts are partial sums of the tree-fiber series

Formerly an assumed ledger field; an independent review demanded it be derived from the seam's named premises — an audit-driven de-vacuification that shrank the trusted surface.

Given the tree seam: $\mathrm{decided}_\sigma(N) = p^{nN} \sum_{T:\ \mathrm{thr}(T) \le N} \mu(\mathrm{fiber}\,T)$ — partition the decided-$\sigma$ classes by their assigned canonical tree and sum the exact per-tree count tie over the threshold-$\le N$ slice.

Hypotheses this rests on: seam — the tree seam: the canonical-tree assignment, its threshold bound, and the exact per-tree count tie (the open TREE-N premise, supplied per prime by the instantiation bundle).

The Lean statement 
theorem TreeSeam.finiteness_stack {n p : ℕ} [NeZero p] {X : ClassifierSpec n p}
    {F : FiberSeries n p X} (seam : TreeSeam n p X F)
    (σ : SplittingType n) (N : ℕ) :
    (X.decided σ N : ℝ≥0∞)
      = (p : ℝ≥0∞) ^ (n * N) * ∑ T ∈ F.thrSlice σ N, F.mass σ T

lean/LeanUrat/MovesU/DefsLedger.lean — theorem

proved The exact box partition: decided plus undecided fills the box

The exact identity that lets the checksum convert per-type upper bounds at all types except σ into the lower bracket leg at σ.

For every level $N$: $\sum_\sigma \mathrm{decided}_\sigma(N) + \mathrm{undec}(N) = p^{nN}$ — the level-$N$ box of monic coefficient classes partitions exactly into the decided-$\sigma$ classes and the undecided complement. Tag-free: a pure counting identity for any classifier.

The Lean statement 
theorem boxN {n p : ℕ} (X : ClassifierSpec n p) [NeZero p] (N : ℕ) :
    (∑ σ : SplittingType n, X.decided σ N) + X.undec N = p ^ (n * N)

lean/LeanUrat/MovesU/U1_boxN.lean — theorem

conditional The two-sided density bracket (U-n)

Assembles the upper-bound leg (SQ.2, via the fiber series) and the lower-bound arithmetic (SQ.3, via the box partition and checksum) into the exported bracket the capstone feeds to the squeeze.

Given the tree seam, the solve seam, the roster pin, the checksum $\sum_\sigma R_\sigma = 1$ in $\mathbb{Q}(q)$, and the gate (REG-$p$) at a prime $p$: for every splitting type $\sigma$ and level $N$, $(R_\sigma(p) - \mathrm{env}(N))\, p^{nN} \le \mathrm{decided}_\sigma(N) \le R_\sigma(p)\, p^{nN}$. Upper leg: the decided count is a partial sum of the positive tree series whose full sum is identified with $R_\sigma(p)$. Lower leg: the box partition plus the evaluated checksum turn the upper bounds at all $\tau \ne \sigma$ into the lower bound at $\sigma$.

Hypotheses this rests on: seam (tree seam: the exact per-tree fiber count tie — open premise); ss (solve seam: the rational family is the real solver output and the series sums to the measured value — open premise); P (roster pin identifying the regularity data with the real block system); hchk (the ℚ(q) checksum Σ_σ R_σ = 1); hreg (the (REG-p) gate); hp (p prime).

The Lean statement 
variable {n p : ℕ} {C : UCarriers n} {X : ClassifierSpec n p} {F : FiberSeries n p X}
  {S : SolveData n} {D : RegData p}

theorem un_bracket (seam : TreeSeam n p X F) (ss : SolveSeam n p C F S)
    (P : RegPin C D) (hchk : ∑ σ, S.R σ = 1) (hreg : RegP D) (hp : p.Prime)

lean/LeanUrat/MovesU/U6_un_bracket.lean — theorem

proved The squeeze: a vanishing envelope forces the density limit

The analytic engine of clause (i): existence of the splitting density and its exact value both fall out of the bracket plus the vanishing undecided envelope — an unconditional real-analysis lemma.

If $(r - \mathrm{env}(N))\,p^{nN} \le \mathrm{decided}_\sigma(N) \le r\,p^{nN}$ for every level $N$, and $\mathrm{env}(N) \to 0$, then the density $\mathrm{decided}_\sigma(N)/p^{nN}$ converges and its limit is $r$.

The Lean statement 
theorem squeeze_limit {n p : ℕ} (X : ClassifierSpec n p) (hp : 1 < p) (r : ℝ)
    (σ : SplittingType n)
    (hbr : ∀ N, (r - X.env N) * (p : ℝ) ^ (n * N) ≤ (X.decided σ N : ℝ) ∧
      (X.decided σ N : ℝ) ≤ r * (p : ℝ) ^ (n * N))
    (henv : Tendsto X.env atTop (𝓝 0)) :
    Tendsto (X.dmass σ) atTop (𝓝 r)

lean/LeanUrat/MovesU/U7_squeeze.lean — theorem

conditional Identification: decided density equals the true-type density

Upgrades the classifier-keyed limit to the true-factorization-type density — the step that makes Theorem U about actual splitting behavior rather than about one algorithm's labels.

Suppose the classifier is verdict-sound: every class it decides as $\sigma$ truly has factorization type $\sigma$. If the decided density tends to $r$ and the undecided fraction tends to $0$, then the TRUE-type density also tends to $r$ — via the exact sandwich $\mathrm{decided}_\sigma(N) \le \mathrm{true}_\sigma(N) \le \mathrm{decided}_\sigma(N) + \mathrm{undec}(N)$ (a true-$\sigma$ class is either decided $\sigma$ or undecided).

Hypotheses this rests on: hvs — verdict soundness: every class decided σ truly has type σ (an open kernel with a citation duty, carried as a per-prime ledger row, never proved here); hd, henv — the decided-density limit and vanishing envelope (supplied by the squeeze and the ledger).

Two named open premises tying the abstract data to the built solver corpus: (a) for each $\sigma$, the tree-fiber series sums exactly to the measured chain value at $q = p$; and (b) under the determinant hypothesis, $R_\sigma$ IS the shallow resolvent solve — the fixed rational function output by the Cramer/adjugate route of the block system.

lean/LeanUrat/MovesU/DefsLedger.lean — theorem

definition The roster pin: abstract regularity data equals the real block system

Closes the surrogate-instance bypass on the solve side — no toy block system can satisfy the ledger; the leg-roster bijection was the final audit round's repair, killing duplicated/omitted β-legs so every real consumed value is genuinely gated by (REG-p).

Every piece of the abstract regularity data is equated to the real built block system: blocks biject with the working degrees $e \in [1,n]$ and per-block indices with the real table states; depth set, matrix entries $K_e$, injections $\iota_e$, per-cell branching entries $J$, and shape weights $W$ equal the real tables'; the active-value operation is the real post-cancellation evaluation semantics on its defined locus; the terminal and split vectors are the designated assembled solver entries (no instance-chosen type); and the $\beta$-leg labels BIJECT onto the real consumed roster — one label per continuing member of each split outcome of each state — each reading its true target (degree, state). As a corollary the gate's (r1) forces the real symbolic determinant hypothesis.

The Lean statement 
structure RegPin {n : ℕ} (C : UCarriers n) {p : ℕ} (D : RegData p) where
  blk : D.Block ≃ {e : ℕ // e ∈ Finset.Icc 1 n}
  st : ∀ b : D.Block, D.bidx b ≃ C.T.State (blk b).1
  depth_pin : D.depthSet
    = (MovesS.consumedDeltas C.T C.Fam).image (fun d : ℕ+ => (d : ℕ))
  K_pin : ∀ (b : D.Block) (i j : D.bidx b),
    D.K b i j
      = MovesS.Kmat C.T C.RB (blk b).1 (C.hK (blk b).1 (blk b).2) (st b i) (st b j)
  iota_pin : ∀ (b : D.Block) (i : D.bidx b),
    D.iota b i = C.RB.ι (blk b).1 (st b i)
  act_pin : ∀ (g : RatFunc ℚ) (q₀ : ℕ) (hok : g ∈ MovesS.OKat (q₀ : ℚ)),
    D.act g q₀ = MovesS.evalAt (q₀ : ℚ) ⟨g, hok⟩
  bterm_pin : ∀ (b : D.Block) (i : D.bidx b),
    D.bterm b i
      = ∑ σ' ∈ MovesS.verdictImage C.T (blk b).1 (st b i),
          MovesS.bTerm C.T C.RB (blk b).1 σ' (st b i)
  bsplit_pin : ∀ (hdet : MovesS.DetHyp C.T C.RB C.hK) (b : D.Block) (i : D.bidx b),
    D.bsplit b i
      = ∑ σ' ∈ C.chain.Sigmas,
          MovesS.bSplit C.T C.RB C.hdc (blk b).1 (blk b).2
            (MovesS.blockSolveLt C.RB C.hdc C.hK hdet (blk b).1) σ' (st b i)
  legSt : ∀ b : D.Block,
    D.legIdx b → (e' : {e' : ℕ // e' ∈ Finset.Icc 1 n}) × C.T.State e'.1
  legEquiv : ∀ b : D.Block, D.legIdx b ≃ LegRoster C.T (blk b).1
  legSt_pin : ∀ (b : D.Block) (l : D.legIdx b),
    legSt b l = legTarget C (blk b).1 (blk b).2 (legEquiv b l)
  betaLeg_pin : ∀ (hdet : MovesS.DetHyp C.T C.RB C.hK) (b : D.Block)
    (l : D.legIdx b) (δ : ℕ) (hδ : 0 < δ), δ ∈ D.depthSet →
    D.betaLeg b l δ
      = MovesS.powSubst ⟨δ, hδ⟩
          (∑ σ' ∈ C.chain.Sigmas,
            MovesS.blockSolve C.T C.RB C.hdc C.hK hdet
              (legSt b l).1.1 (legSt b l).1.2 (legSt b l).2 σ')
  blockDim_pin : ∀ b, D.blockDim b = Fintype.card (C.T.State (blk b).1)
  cell_pin : ∀ b, D.cellCount b
    = ∑ τ : C.T.State (blk b).1, ∑ o ∈ MovesS.splitOuts C.T (blk b).1 τ,
        (C.MS.cells (blk b).1 τ o).card
  Jcell_pin : ∀ b,
    (letI := D.instJ b; letI := Classical.decEq (RatFunc ℚ)
     Finset.univ.image (D.Jcell b))
      = (letI := Classical.decEq (RatFunc ℚ)
         (Finset.univ : Finset (C.T.State (blk b).1)).biUnion fun τ =>
           (MovesS.splitOuts C.T (blk b).1 τ).biUnion fun o =>
             (C.MS.cells (blk b).1 τ o).image fun c =>
               (C.RB.jPCell (blk b).1 τ c).val)
  shape_pin : D.shapeCount = C.Fam.Sh.card
  W_pin : (letI := D.instW; letI := Classical.decEq (RatFunc ℚ)
      Finset.univ.image D.Wcoef)
    = (letI := Classical.decEq (RatFunc ℚ)
       C.Fam.Sh.image fun Ŝ => (C.chain.WshP Ŝ).val)
  leg_pin : ∀ b, D.legCount b
    = ∑ τ : C.T.State (blk b).1, ∑ o ∈ MovesS.splitOuts C.T (blk b).1 τ,
        MovesS.Outcome.c (C.T.odata (blk b).1 τ o)

lean/LeanUrat/MovesU/DefsLedger.lean — structure

definition The tree pin: abstract trees are the built canonical-tree corpus

Closes the surrogate-instance bypass on the tree side: the classifier must speak about the built canonical-tree corpus, and (final audit round) the completeness direction kills the proper-subfamily cheat, so the count tie is a claim about the FULL realizable family.

Identifies the abstract canonical trees with the built verified-tree corpus: an injective map sending each abstract $\sigma$-tree to a complete finite canonical tree with the same verdict type and the same threshold, realizable at every positive level at or above its threshold; conversely (completeness) every such realizable tree of type $\sigma$ and threshold $\le N$ is hit. The level-$N$ coefficient box is identified with the digit box via the base-$p$ digit expansion (no free relabeling), and the classifier verdict is pinned: 'decided $\sigma$ at level $N$' holds iff the class fibers at a carried $\sigma$-tree within threshold, and the seam's assigned tree is exactly that fibering tree. The coefficient chart is restricted to positive levels $N > 0$; the pin is silent at $N = 0$ (a single-class box).

The Lean statement 
structure TreePin (X : ClassifierSpec n p) (F : FiberSeries n p X)
    (seam : TreeSeam n p X F) where
  pol : MovesD.CanonPolicy p (ZMod p)
  Tm : ∀ N : ℕ, MovesD.TreeModel p (ZMod p) n N (n * N) pol
  chart : ∀ N : ℕ, 0 < N → Fin n → Fin (n * N)
  chart_inj : ∀ (N : ℕ) (hN : 0 < N), Function.Injective (chart N hN)
  boxeq : ∀ N : ℕ, Box p n N ≃ MovesD.Box p (n * N)
  boxeq_digits : ∀ (N : ℕ) (f : Box p n N) (i : Fin n),
    f i = ∑ k : Fin N,
      ((boxeq N f (digitIdx n N i k)).val : ZMod (p ^ N))
        * (p : ZMod (p ^ N)) ^ (k : ℕ)
  vt : ∀ σ : SplittingType n, F.Tree σ → MovesT.VTree p (ZMod p)
  vt_inj : ∀ σ, Function.Injective (vt σ)
  vt_typemult : ∀ (σ : SplittingType n) (T : F.Tree σ), (vt σ T).typemult = σ.1
  vt_thr : ∀ (σ : SplittingType n) (T : F.Tree σ), F.thr σ T = (vt σ T).thr n
  vt_real : ∀ (σ : SplittingType n) (T : F.Tree σ) (N : ℕ) (hN : 0 < N),
    F.thr σ T ≤ N → MovesT.Realizes (Tm N) (chart N hN) (vt σ T)
  vt_surj : ∀ (σ : SplittingType n) (V : MovesT.VTree p (ZMod p)) (N : ℕ)
    (hN : 0 < N), V.typemult = σ.1 → V.thr n ≤ N →
    MovesT.Realizes (Tm N) (chart N hN) V →
    ∃ T : F.Tree σ, vt σ T = V
  canonical_pin : ∀ (N : ℕ) (hN : 0 < N) (f : Box p n N) (σ : SplittingType n),
    X.canonical N f = some σ ↔
      ∃ T : F.Tree σ, F.thr σ T ≤ N ∧
        (vt σ T).fiberAt (Tm N) (chart N hN) (boxeq N f)
  treeOf_pin : ∀ (σ : SplittingType n) (N : ℕ) (hN : 0 < N)
    (f : {f : Box p n N // X.canonical N f = some σ}),
    (vt σ (seam.treeOf σ N f)).fiberAt (Tm N) (chart N hN) (boxeq N f.1)

lean/LeanUrat/MovesU/DefsLedger.lean — structure

proved Non-vacuity witness for the guarded coefficient chart

The repair of record: the final audit found the original unguarded chart type uninhabitable at level 0, making the capstone's instantiation premise inconsistent and the theorem vacuously true — the guard plus this proved witness restore a consistent, inhabitable premise.

For every $n$ and every positive level $N$, the digit chart $i \mapsto i\cdot N$ is an injective map $\{0,\dots,n-1\} \to \{0,\dots,nN-1\}$ — so the positive-level slice of the tree pin's chart type is inhabited and the guard is consistent.

The Lean statement 
def chartWitness (n N : ℕ) (hN : 0 < N) : Fin n → Fin (n * N) :=
  fun i => digitIdx n N i ⟨0, hN⟩

theorem chartWitness_inj (n N : ℕ) (hN : 0 < N) :
    Function.Injective (chartWitness n N hN)

lean/LeanUrat/MovesU/DefsLedger.lean — theorem

definition The p-adic reading bridge: verdicts as true ramification and residue data (named open premise)

Makes clause (ii-Z_p) about genuine ramification/residue invariants rather than bare (e,f) labels (an audit showed the earlier version could not distinguish (1,6) from (2,3)); its instantiation at the real classifier, plus the local-ring plumbing and two local-field laws, is the exactly stated open residue.

The named bridge from classifier verdicts to genuine $p$-adic factorizations: a reading assigning to each monic degree-$n$ polynomial over $\mathbb{Z}_p$ a multiset of pairs $(e,f)$, subject to: all entries have $e, f \ge 1$; the polynomial factors over $\mathbb{Q}_p$ into monic $\mathbb{Z}_p$-coefficient irreducible factors whose (ramification index, residue degree) pairs — the true valuation-theoretic invariants, read through the local ring obtained by adjoining a root — form exactly that multiset, with $e\cdot f$ equal to each factor's degree; every level-$N$ class has a monic lift; and the classifier's true type is $\sigma$ exactly when EVERY monic lift reads $\sigma$. Consequently the $\mathbb{Z}_p$-read density equals the true-type density.

The Lean statement 
variable (n p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime]

structure ZpBridge (X : ClassifierSpec n p) where
  zfType : Polynomial ℤ_[p] → Multiset (ℕ × ℕ)
  zf_pos : ∀ g : Polynomial ℤ_[p], g.Monic → g.natDegree = n →
    ∀ ef ∈ zfType g, 1 ≤ ef.1 ∧ 1 ≤ ef.2
  zf_factor : ∀ g : Polynomial ℤ_[p], g.Monic → g.natDegree = n →
    ∃ factors : Multiset (Polynomial ℤ_[p]),
      g.map (PadicInt.Coe.ringHom)
          = (factors.map (Polynomial.map PadicInt.Coe.ringHom)).prod ∧
      (∀ h ∈ factors, h.Monic ∧ Irreducible (h.map PadicInt.Coe.ringHom)) ∧
      zfType g = factors.map (fun h => (MovesT.ramIdx h, MovesT.resDeg h)) ∧
      (zfType g).map (fun ef => ef.1 * ef.2) = factors.map Polynomial.natDegree
  lift_exists : ∀ (N : ℕ) (f : Box p n N), ∃ g : Polynomial ℤ_[p],
    g.Monic ∧ g.natDegree = n ∧ g.map (PadicInt.toZModPow N) = f.toPoly
  lift_true : ∀ (N : ℕ) (f : Box p n N) (σ : SplittingType n),
    X.trueType N f = some σ ↔ ZpReads n p zfType N f σ

The per-prime conditionality itemized: every field is a named premise with a recorded source corpus responsible for its discharge, replacing an earlier bare-proposition pack that an audit showed could be trivially instantiated.

  cl11_ksub : KsubM1C1T C.T

  cl19_rep : ∀ e (τ : C.T.State e) (x x' : C.MS.Rep e τ) (c : C.MS.Cell e τ)
    (q₀ : ℚ), q₀ ∈ C.MS.Pools → C.MS.activeState q₀ e τ →
    C.MS.μcell e τ x c q₀ = C.MS.μcell e τ x' c q₀
  o3_teichmuller : X.baseSection = BaseSection.teichmuller
  seam : TreeSeam n p X F
  ssrc : SolveSeam n p C F S
  rs4_checksum : ∑ σ, S.R σ = 1
